Under mobility-constrained communication at a parameter-level privacy budget of 1, correlated private quantization reduces mean travel time by 14.3% relative to independent quantization at the same budget and communication cost. At a parameter-level privacy budget of 0.3, a vehicle-origin inference attack trained on independent simulations reaches 30% accuracy with correlated private quantization and 22% with independent quantization after 100 rounds, versus 91% without privacy and 10% for random guessing.

</details>


### [33] [Nonlinear Maps Between Irreducible Representations in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41059)
*Mani Shayestehfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出按次数分解的非线性表示交互框架与 Specht Triple 三元组残差，在冻结 GIN 主干上相比参数匹配的投影器对照组将标准化测试 MSE 降低 11.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变线性映射无法混合非同构不可约表示，而非线性激活可以，但这一非线性交互机制缺乏系统的刻画与利用方式，本文希望将其形式化并转化为可用的归纳偏置。

Method: 构建按次数（degree-resolved）分解的非线性交互图景，得到"块清单"（block inventory）；据此设计 Specht Triple——作用于无序图三元组上的残差模块；在合成结构基准上，以冻结 GIN 为骨干，与使用相同不可约投影器及完整线性等变空间、参数量匹配的 Projector Control 对照。

Result: 聚合标准化测试 MSE 相对 Projector Control 下降 11.05%。

Conclusion: 结果支持将不可约分解作为非线性高阶等变处理的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Equivariant linear maps cannot mix non-isomorphic irreducible representations, whereas nonlinear activations can. We develop a degree-resolved version of this nonlinear interaction picture and use the resulting block inventory to construct Specht Triple, a residual on unordered graph triples. On a synthetic structural benchmark with a frozen GIN backbone, Specht Triple reduces aggregate standardised test MSE by 11.05% relative to a parameter-matched Projector Control using the same irreducible projectors and complete linear equivariant space. The results support irreducible decomposition as an inductive bias for nonlinear higher-order equivariant processing.

</details>


### [34] [bf16 Rounding Silently Discards Most RL Policy Updates: A Hidden Training-Inference Mismatch and an Unbiased One-Line Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41073)
*Andrew Gao,William Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出 RL 后训练中 fp32 主权重经 round-to-nearest 转为 bf16 用于 rollout 时会丢弃大量小步更新，导致服务权重与优化器严重滞后；提出 Fair Cast，用无偏舍入（随机舍入或误差反馈）导出主权重，并在服务副本上计算训练损失，仅改一行导出调用即可消除该滞后并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用学习率下多数单步更新小于半个 bf16 间距，round-to-nearest 转换会直接丢弃这些更新：在 Qwen3-1.7B 上每步仅 0.4–0.5% 的服务权重发生变化，生成训练数据的策略比优化器滞后 50–150 步。而现有 mismatch 修正方法无法察觉该滞后，因为它们比较的是同一舍入下游的两个副本。

Method: Fair Cast：1）导出 fp32 主权重时采用无偏舍入规则（stochastic rounding 或 error feedback）；2）在服务副本上计算训练损失。改动仅为导出调用处的一行代码，不引入新超参数，1.7B 参数单次导出开销 22–38 ms。

Result: 在 Qwen3-0.6B 至 4B、AdamW 与 Muon 的 25 组配对种子实验中，Fair Cast 18 胜、6 平、1 负，平均提升 6.1 分；在 zebra puzzles 上四次运行达到 0.57 ± 0.16，而 round-to-nearest 仅为 0.33 ± 0.10。此外，保存的 fp32 checkpoint 与服务模型保持一致，并将 round-to-nearest 的隐式滞后显式化为一种可按需恢复的正则项。

Conclusion: RL 后训练中权重导出舍入并非无关细节，无偏舍入加服务副本损失计算能有效消除策略滞后、稳定提升效果，且实现成本极低；同时该工作将 round-to-nearest 的隐式滞后重新解释为可显式控制的正则化手段。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of language models keeps fp32 master weights in the optimizer and serves rollouts from a bf16 copy produced by round-to-nearest conversion. At the learning rates in common use, most per-step updates are smaller than half a bf16 spacing and the conversion discards them: on Qwen3-1.7B only 0.4-0.5% of served weights change per step, and the policy that generates the training data lags the optimizer by 50-150 steps. Existing mismatch corrections cannot see this lag because they compare two copies downstream of the same rounding. We propose Fair Cast: export the master with an unbiased rounding rule (stochastic rounding or error feedback) and compute the training loss on the served copy. The change is one line at the export call, adds no hyperparameters and costs 22-38 ms per export of 1.7B parameters. Across 25 matched seed-pairs on Qwen3-0.6B to 4B with AdamW and Muon, Fair Cast is ahead on 18, tied on 6 and behind on 1, with a mean gain of 6.1 points; on zebra puzzles it reaches 0.57 +/- 0.16 over four runs where round-to-nearest collapses to 0.33 +/- 0.10. It also makes the saved fp32 checkpoint agree with the served model, and it exposes the implicit lag of round-to-nearest as a regularizer that can be restored explicitly when a task needs it.

</details>


### [35] [Where, When, and How Many Bits? Scaling Laws for SGD with Stochastic Rounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41074)
*Zhichao Wang,Michael W. Mahoney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了随机舍入(SR)如何在模型规模、训练时长与精度变化下影响预测风险,给出匹配的上下界,并刻画最优计算量缩放、比特需求与低精度获益条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度训练降低计算量,但引入额外舍入误差。现有工作缺乏对随机舍入如何在模型规模、训练时间与精度三者交互下影响预测风险的理论刻画,尤其不清楚前向/反向舍入、参数平均的差异化作用。

Method: 考虑固定随机第一层的两层线性网络,在新高斯数据(幂律谱)上用mini-batch SGD训练;每步前向与反向特征独立舍入。针对最终模型与参数均匀平均两种情形,在学习率与精度调度可在训练前任意选择的一般设定下,证明预测误差的匹配上界与下界。

Result: 固定精度与常数学习率、配合参数平均时,导出最小预测误差随计算量的下降规律,并识别达到最优计算量缩放的模型规模与训练时间;仅前向舍入保持精确算术的计算缩放,反向舍入则改变该缩放;参数平均会改变需高精度的计算位置及高精度何时有益;给出给定精度容差下的比特需求,并说明反向舍入如何影响学习率调度设计;固定计算预算下,当更大模型或更久训练的收益超过SR误差时,低精度可提升预测。

Conclusion: 随机舍入误差与模型规模、训练时长、精度存在可量化的权衡;前向与反向舍入作用不对称,参数平均可重塑精度需求与调度设计。模型仿真验证了权衡,MLP与小Transformer实验进一步考察了理论模型之外的舍入选择。

Abstract: Low-precision training reduces computation but introduces additional rounding error. We theoretically study how stochastic rounding (SR) affects prediction risk as model size, training time, and precision change. We consider a two-layer linear network with a fixed random first layer, trained by mini-batch SGD on fresh Gaussian data with power-law spectra. At each SGD step, the features used in forward and backward computations are rounded independently. We prove matched upper and lower bounds on prediction error for the final model and for uniform parameter averaging, with general learning-rate and precision schedules chosen before training. For fixed precisions and constant learning rates with parameter averaging, we derive how the smallest prediction error decreases with compute, and we identify model sizes and training times that achieve this optimal-compute scaling. Rounding only the forward features preserves the compute scaling of exact arithmetic, while rounding backward features changes it. Averaging parameters can change which computation needs higher precision and when higher precision is useful. Our results also give bit requirements for a given accuracy tolerance and show how backward rounding can change learning-rate schedule design. At a fixed compute budget, lower precision can improve prediction when the benefit of a larger model or longer training outweighs the additional SR-induced error. Model simulations illustrate these tradeoffs, and experiments with an MLP and a small transformer examine rounding choices beyond the theoretical model.

</details>


### [36] [MUON-RT Reusing the Teacher's Trajectory for Momentum-Free Muon for LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41104)
*Aryan Das,Vinay Kumar Verma,Aakansha Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Muon-RT，重用自蒸馏 EMA 教师的师生位移作为动量，去除隐层权重矩阵的独立动量缓冲，在减少持久内存的同时提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏训练中 EMA 教师已隐含保存学生更新历史，但 Muon 优化器仍额外存储一份动量缓冲，造成不必要的持久训练显存开销。

Method: 将师生位移视为指数加权更新历史，并在局部模型下证明其能平滑时间不相关噪声；对该位移去除径向分量、做谱归一化，经 Newton-Schulz 正交化后与当前梯度按比例混合，替代原有动量。

Result: 在显式正则性与对齐假设下证明期望目标差距界线性收缩至残差水平。Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 上 SDFT 相比 AdamW 平均准确率+2.6 点、持久内存省 42%；相比动量 Muon +5.4 点、内存省 27%。Olmo-3-7B-Instruct 上 SDPO 相比 AdamW +2.0 点。

Conclusion: Muon-RT 通过复用教师信号实现动量无缓冲化，兼得内存效率与收敛/精度收益，为自蒸馏场景下优化器与蒸馏信号的耦合提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Self-distillation methods for large language models with an exponential moving average (EMA) teacher already retain a history of student updates. Muon nevertheless stores a separate momentum buffer, increasing persistent training memory. We propose Muon with Reused Teacher (Muon-RT), which reuses the teacher-student displacement to eliminate momentum buffers for hidden weight matrices. We show that this displacement encodes an exponentially weighted history of applied updates and smooths temporally uncorrelated noise under a local model. Muon-RT removes its radial component, normalizes it spectrally, and mixes it with the current gradient after Newton-Schulz (NS) orthogonalization. Under explicit regularity and alignment assumptions, we establish linear contraction of an expected objective-gap bound toward a residual error level. With Self-Distillation Fine-Tuning (SDFT) on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Muon-RT improves average accuracy over AdamW by 2.6 points with 42% less persistent memory, and over momentum Muon by 5.4 points with 27% less persistent memory. With Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) on Olmo-3-7B-Instruct, it improves average accuracy over AdamW by 2.0 points.

</details>


### [37] [TalentRL: Strategy-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Hierarchical Skill Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41143)
*Huancheng Chen,Xiaodi Sun,Zhaoqiong Huang,Michelle Chang,Jingwen Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TalentRL 提出一种策略引导的强化学习框架，在程序性记忆上进行分层路由：粗粒度"talent"（策略）引导细粒度"skill"（可执行过程）的选择，并用对比式奖励训练路由、用组相对优势优化执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体多从扁平 SkillBank 中检索单个技能，未显式建模支配技能选择与使用的高层策略；而任务结果同时取决于策略选择、检索到的技能上下文与下游执行，仅靠终端奖励无法区分路由决策的贡献。

Method: 1) 分层记忆：粗粒度 talent 表示反复出现的技能使用策略，细粒度 skill 提供可执行过程；2) 策略模型为每个任务选择一个 talent 作为引导，并在基于相似度的检索中对关联 skill 进行软性优先排序；3) 路由训练：对同一任务在 talent 条件上下文上做匹配干预，利用不同 rollout 间的奖励对比来估计 talent 路由的贡献；4) 下游执行使用组相对优势（group-relative advantages）优化。

Result: 在 7 个开放域与多跳问答基准上，talent-aware SFT 相比对应 zero-shot 基线平均成功率提升 7.53%（Qwen2.5-7B）、6.71%（Qwen3-8B）、9.21%（Llama-3.1-8B）；RL 在此基础上再提升 7.58%–9.32%，且在三个骨干模型上平均成功率均为最高。智能体基准上，ALFWorld 成功率 99.3%、WebShop 得分 90.7，均为所有对比方法中最高。

Conclusion: 显式建模策略层的分层路由优于扁平技能检索；将路由学习与执行优化解耦（对比奖励 + 组相对优势）能有效提升 LLM 智能体在问答与具身/交互任务上的表现。

Abstract: Natural-language skills enable large language model (LLM) agents to reuse procedures across tasks, yet most systems retrieve individual skills from a flat SkillBank without explicitly modeling the high-level strategy that governs their selection and use. We introduce TalentRL, a strategy-guided reinforcement learning framework for hierarchical routing over procedural memory. Coarse-grained talents represent recurring strategies for skill use, while fine-grained skills provide executable procedures. For each task, the policy model selects a talent that guides execution and softly prioritizes its associated skills during similarity-based retrieval. However, since task outcomes depend jointly on talent selection, retrieved skill context, and downstream execution, terminal rewards alone cannot isolate the contribution of the routing decision. TalentRL therefore trains talent routing with reward contrasts between rollouts of the same task under matched interventions on the talent-conditioned context, and optimizes downstream execution with group-relative advantages. Across seven open-domain and multi-hop question-answering benchmarks, talent-aware supervised fine-tuning improves average success rate by 7.53%, 6.71%, and 9.21% over the corresponding zero-shot baselines for Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B, respectively. Reinforcement learning further improves these SFT checkpoints by 7.58% to 9.32%, and TalentRL achieves the highest average success rate among all compared methods on all three backbones. On the agentic benchmarks ALFWorld and WebShop, TalentRL achieves the highest ALFWorld success rate (99.3%) and the highest WebShop score (90.7) among all compared methods.

</details>


### [38] [CAP: Learning to Optimize from Sparse Observations of Related Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41173)
*Lin Fu,Siwei Fu,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAP（Candidate Acquisition Policy）：从稀疏的已完成相关优化任务记录中学习共享结构与任务特异变异的生成模型，采样交互式练习函数，并在其上训练一个以最终优化性能为目标、受反馈条件化的查询策略；部署时策略参数固定，仅凭新观测决定下一次查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验驱动的黑盒优化中每次评估消耗材料、设备机时等资源，新任务可用实验次数极其有限；虽已有相关任务的完成记录，但任务特异经验往往只是少量稀疏历史，难以直接学到序列查询策略。

Method: CAP（Candidate Acquisition Policy）：(1) 对固定历史记录建模任务间共享结构与任务特异变异，用于采样交互式练习函数；(2) 在这些练习函数上学习反馈条件化的查询策略，优化目标为固定评估预算下的最终优化性能；(3) 部署阶段参数保持固定，以每个新观测作为条件生成下一次查询。

Result: 在覆盖6维与20维的五个合成任务族、以及BOPTEST与SU2两个工程仿真基准上，CAP在四个合成任务族和两个工程基准上取得所报告方法中最低的最终预算中位值。

Conclusion: 来自已完成相关任务的稀疏记录足以支撑新任务上的自适应查询决策，从而更有效地利用有限且昂贵的实验评估。

Abstract: In experiment-driven black-box optimization, each evaluation can consume materials, equipment time, and other resources, limiting the number of experiments available for a new task. Records from completed related optimization campaigns could help, but learning a sequential query policy remains challenging when task-specific experience consists of only a few sparse histories. We propose CAP (Candidate Acquisition Policy), which models shared structure and task-specific variation in fixed historical records to sample interactive practice functions. On these functions, CAP learns a feedback-conditioned query policy aimed at final optimization performance under a fixed evaluation budget. At deployment, its parameters remain fixed while each new observation informs the next query. Across five synthetic task families spanning 6 and 20 dimensions and two engineering simulation benchmarks, BOPTEST and SU2, CAP attains the lowest median final-budget value among the reported methods on four synthetic families and both engineering benchmarks. These results indicate that sparse records from completed related tasks can support adaptive query decisions on new tasks, making more effective use of limited, costly experimental evaluations.

</details>


### [39] [Role-Aware Server Optimization for Multimodal Federated Learning with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41193)
*Liuyin Shi,Wenbo He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出角色感知服务器优化RASO，在多模态联邦学习缺失模态场景下协调贡献者选择与角色专属服务器更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺失模态下客户端各自适配活跃模态编码器与共享预测块，服务器需回答两个问题：每个组件应由哪些客户端贡献，以及其聚合结果跨轮如何被纳入。现有基于可用性的聚合可能丢弃编码器更新，而贡献者选择又会影响服务器动量，预测块动量效果依编码器更新策略而定，可能提升也可能损害性能。

Method: RASO依据预测依赖性与本地优化中的参与程度选择贡献者；对编码器保留动量更新；对共享预测块则直接同步到其当前聚合结果，实现角色专属的服务器更新策略。

Result: 在多数据集与多融合架构上的实验验证了RASO在缺失模态条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: 将贡献者选择与角色差异化服务器更新协同设计，可有效缓解缺失模态对多模态联邦学习的影响。

Abstract: Multimodal federated learning (MFL) trains a shared model by aggregating client updates without exchanging raw data. Under missing modalities, clients adapt active modality encoders and a shared prediction block (fusion and classifier) to different input subsets. This raises two server-side questions: which clients should contribute to each component, and how should their aggregates be incorporated across rounds? We observe that an absent-modality encoder can still adapt through missing-input handling. Availability- based aggregation can therefore omit its update while retaining jointly learned prediction-block updates. Contributor selection also shapes server momentum, a weighted history of aggregated updates. We observe that prediction-block momentum can improve or reduce performance depending on the encoder update policy. We therefore propose Role-Aware Server Optimization (RASO), which coordinates contributor selection with role-specific server updates. RASO selects contributors through prediction dependence and inclusion in local optimization, retains encoder momentum, and synchronizes the shared prediction block to its current aggregate. Experiments across datasets and fusion architectures demonstrate the effectiveness of RASO under missing modalities.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond Scarcity: When Encoder Adaptation Is Worthwhile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41213)
*Zexing Zhang,Jichao Li,Tianyang Lei,Yiyin Zhang,Kewei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 长尾识别中，分类器再平衡后是否还需继续更新编码器的问题被标准基准混淆。作者提出受控评估框架，分离"唯一支撑样本数"与"表征分配"，发现只有在表征分配更不均衡时，编码器自适应才带来实质收益，且该转变在多种控制与ImageNet尺度下稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长尾基准同时改变了稀有类的独特样本数量与这些样本影响表征的频率，导致无法判断分类器校正后编码器微调是否仍有价值；最终准确率也无法揭示这一增量价值。

Method: 构建受控评估框架：将unique support与representation allocation解耦；从共同校正模型出发，设置匹配的"仅分类器"与"全网络"延续训练以估计编码器更新的增量价值；结合分阶段控制、多种表征与自适应目标、全分布指标，并对类分配、支撑重构与优化运行做分层推断。

Result: 相同的低支撑区间，仅当表征分配更不均衡时，才从"仅训练分类器足够"转变为"编码器自适应有实际收益"；该转变在分类器校正方式、表征目标、归一化与增强控制以及ImageNet尺度评估下均持续存在，同时保持频繁类准确率；在预设的1个百分点操作裕度下，两种区间由同时置信界分离。

Conclusion: 基准构建方式本身会改变校正后的训练决策；支撑样本规模不足以刻画表征自适应的剩余价值，表征分配是关键因素。

Abstract: Long-tailed recognition often learns a shared representation before correcting the classifier for class imbalance. Once the classifier has been rebalanced, however, final accuracy does not reveal whether further encoder adaptation remains worthwhile. Standard imbalance benchmarks obscure this question because they jointly vary the number of distinct examples available to rare classes and the frequency with which those examples shape the representation. We introduce a controlled evaluation framework that separates unique support from representation allocation and estimates the incremental value of encoder updates through matched classifier-only and full-network continuations from a common corrected model. The framework combines stage-specific controls, multiple representation and adaptation objectives, whole-distribution metrics, and hierarchical inference over class assignments, support reconstructions, and optimization runs. Across the controlled study, the same low-support regime moves from one in which classifier-only training is adequate to one in which encoder adaptation yields a practically meaningful benefit solely as representation allocation becomes more imbalanced. The transition persists across classifier corrections, representation objectives, normalization and augmentation controls, and ImageNet-scale evaluation, while preserving frequent-class accuracy. Under the prespecified one-percentage-point operating margin, the two regimes are separated by simultaneous confidence bounds. These results show that benchmark construction can alter a post-correction training decision and that support size alone does not characterize the remaining value of representation adaptation.

</details>


### [41] [A Positional Realizability Theory of Length Generalization in Single-Head Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41218)
*Jiasong Han,Yunkai Chai,Jintao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对"长度泛化(LG)为何强依赖位置编码(PE)"这一问题，构建了仅含单个纯位置注意力头的受控抽象模型，提出模拟迁移算子 $T_L$ 与标量 $\alpha_L$ 作为可计算见证，给出可实现性理论：含严格唯一全局最大化核的相对头深行刻画(Thm 2)、推出经验规律绝对/相对对偶(Prop 4)、学习绝对编码欠定性与静态表达能力上界，并以充分性定理(Thm 1)把 $\alpha_L$ 与 LG 相连。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 PE 分类中，相对方案只能外推固定偏移、绝对方案固定位置，二者都无法处理不断增长的偏移，导致 LG 的机制缺乏理论解释。作者希望把"经验性 PE 分类"升级为可证明的位置核迁移定理，并界定其成立与失效的边界。

Method: 构造受控抽象：单个 positional-only 注意力头；定义模拟迁移算子 $T_L$，在更长网格上重新评估选定的、可实现长度 $L_0$ 的位置核，并仅由 $(\Phi, t_n)$ 在训练前读出标量 $\alpha_L\in[0,1]$；以 $\alpha_L$ 为态射见证，给出相对头深行刻画、绝对/相对对偶、学习绝对编码欠定性、静态表达能力界与充分性定理；同时指出一般确定性情形仍为开放猜想，并纠正"outside-colspan"论证的错误。

Result: 理论侧：Thm 2 刻画严格唯一全局最大化核的相对头深行结构；Prop 4 由绝对/相对对偶推出既有经验规律；给出学习绝对编码的欠定性结论与静态表达能力上界。实验侧：在受控单头设置中，$\alpha_L$ 精确区分该分类下可泛化与不可泛化的 (PE, 任务) 组合（2625 次训练模型测量，在 vocab 64/d=128 下稳定）。

Conclusion: 本文将既有 PE 分类转化为位置核迁移定理，并指出可绕过该框架的内容机制与深度机制；理论仅覆盖位置通道，依赖 token 内容的目标任务（如加法、排序等标准 LG 测试集）不在 Thm 2 范围内，一般确定性情形仍是开放猜想。

Abstract: Why does length generalization (LG) depend so sharply on the positional encoding (PE)? Relative schemes extrapolate fixed offsets, absolute schemes fix positions, and neither handles growing offsets. We give a realizability theory for a deliberately controlled abstraction, a single positional-only attention head, that derives this well-documented empirical pattern and says precisely where it holds and breaks. The device is a simulation transfer operator $T_L$ that re-evaluates a selected length-$L_0$-realizing positional kernel on the longer grid, with a scalar $α_L \in [0,1]$ read off before any training from $(Φ, t_n)$ alone. Here $α_L$ serves as the computable witness of that taxonomy's derivation, not as a stronger empirical predictor. Our results are a relative-head deep-row characterization for kernels with a strict unique global maximizer (Theorem 2), an absolute/relative duality that derives the pattern (Proposition 4), a learned-absolute underdetermination result, and a static expressivity bound; a sufficiency theorem (Theorem 1) bridges $α_L$ to LG, and the general deterministic case is a precise open conjecture (correcting a tempting but invalid "outside-colspan" argument). Experiments confirm the structure: in the controlled single-head regime $α_L$ exactly separates the taxonomy's generalizing and non-generalizing (PE, task) cells (2625 trained-model measurements; stable at vocab 64/d=128). Together, these results turn the received PE taxonomy into a positional-kernel transfer theorem, and identify the content and depth mechanisms that escape it. The theory concerns the positional channel alone: tasks whose targets depend on token content, including the standard length-generalization suite such as addition and sorting, fall outside Theorem 2.

</details>


### [42] [Corroborative Learning: Mutual Enhancement through Task-Response Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41222)
*Zexing Zhang,Jichao Li,Tianyang Lei,Yiyin Zhang,Kewei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"佐证式学习"(Corroborative Learning, CL)：利用同伴表示对响应增量做"源排除重建"，在被隔开的模型组间选择性约束方向，从而在保留互补性差异的同时实现知识迁移，无需输出/特征匹配或额外推理依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一任务的独立模型因架构、数据与优化差异而形成互补能力。现有协同学习方法多通过缩减预测、关系或表示差异来建立迁移，但差异恰恰是互补性的来源，因而存在"缩差以交换知识"与"保差以维持互补"的结构性权衡。

Method: CL让每个模型形成任务驱动响应，并由同伴提供跨组支持：用同伴表示对其响应增量做"源排除重建"(source-excluded reconstruction)，使拟合与评估分处互不相交的组，并按跨组可重建性选择性地约束方向；无需直接输出或特征匹配，也不增加推理依赖。理论上，在线性重建与固定同伴表示下，该损失等价于一个半正定方向正则项，对越难重建的方向约束越强。

Result: 在信号与噪声条件下刻画了方向收缩降低响应估计误差的成立条件。六个语言任务、24个模型、36个配对的实验中，CL在每个任务上均优于容量匹配的独立自适应，多数有向模型组合均获正增益；成对比较显示相对迁移学习与互学习方法具广泛优势，且不同协作规则下从互补性获得的增益不同；低标签语言与视觉结果支持跨域适用性。

Conclusion: 任务相关差异可在不消除分歧的前提下支持相互增强，即通过可重建性约束实现有选择的知识传递，是对"缩差—保差"权衡的一种结构性替代方案。

Abstract: In pattern analysis, models trained independently for the same task often develop complementary capabilities through differences in architecture, data, and optimization. Exploiting complementarity while retaining independent deployment is central to collaborative learning. Most methods establish transfer by reducing differences in predictions, relations, or representations between target and collaborator, although these differences also underlie complementarity. This creates a structural trade-off between reducing differences for knowledge exchange and preserving them for complementarity. We therefore propose Corroborative Learning (CL). Each model forms task-driven responses, with cross-group support from its collaborator. Source-excluded reconstruction of response increments from collaborator representations separates fitting and evaluation across disjoint groups and selectively constrains directions by cross-group reconstructability. The process requires no direct output or feature matching or additional inference dependency. Furthermore, with linear reconstruction and fixed collaborator representations, the loss equals a positive semidefinite directional regularizer, constraining less reconstructable directions more strongly. Signal and noise conditions characterize when directional shrinkage reduces response-estimation error. Across six language tasks, 24 models, and 36 pairs, CL improves over capacity-matched independent adaptation on every task, with positive gains across most directed model combinations. Pairwise comparisons show broad advantages over transfer and mutual-learning methods and different gains from complementarity across collaboration rules. Low-label language and vision results support cross-domain applicability. Task-relevant differences can support mutual enhancement without eliminating disagreement.

</details>


### [43] [Generative SDEs with Trainable Diffusion and Density-Based Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41243)
*Angxiu Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出散度核方法，联合优化神经SDE的漂移场、扩散场与初始高斯分布，直接以终端密度为损失目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（如基于分数的生成器）在参数规模相当时仍产生较多偏离模态的样本，需直接从终端密度出发优化SDE的整体分布变换。

Method: 基于散度核构造直接依赖终端密度的损失，联合优化神经SDE的时间无关漂移场与扩散场，以及初始高斯分布。

Result: 在二维与八维高斯混合实验中成功学到时间无关场，脱模态样本数显著少于同参数量级的基于分数的生成器。

Conclusion: 漂移负责将样本吸引至各模态，扩散负责在模态间分散多余概率；联合优化终端密度可有效减少脱模态样本。

Abstract: We develop a divergence-kernel method for jointly optimizing neural SDE drift and diffusion fields and the initial Gaussian distribution, using losses that depend directly on the terminal density. In two- and eight-dimensional Gaussian-mixture experiments, we learn time-independent fields and obtain far fewer off-mode samples than score-based generators with similar parameter counts. The drift attracts samples toward the modes, while the diffusion helps disperse excess probability between them.

</details>


### [44] [A Unified Framework for Probability Estimation of Data-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41244)
*Vitaly Feldman,Satyen Kale,Vinod Raman,Kunal Talwar,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架研究估计 D(φ(S))——即未知分布 D 对样本 S 经已知映射 φ 所确定事件的概率。在此框架下分析了经验插件估计量（误差即泛化差距）与留一法估计量（即 Good–Turing，对 1-点删除稳定映射均准确），并针对"基分类器+记忆例外"结构提出自适应缺失质量估计问题及"限制于选中区域的 Good–Turing 估计量"，给出基于 VC 维与互信息的两条误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典缺失质量、自适应数据分析与后选择推断的数据依赖目标，以及数据训练预测器的测试误差估计（Gastpar 等 2024a,b）本质上是同一问题：估计 D 对由样本自身确定的事件的概率。作者希望在同一形式化框架下统一审视两种经典估计途径，并刻画当事件区域依赖同一样本时估计的可行性条件。

Method: 1) 形式化 D(φ(S))，S 为 n 个 i.i.d. 样本，算法已知 φ 与 S、无额外采样；2) 分析经验插件估计量——其误差恰为泛化差距，故泛化界可直接迁移；3) 分析留一法估计量，证明其等价于 Good–Turing 估计量，且对任何在删除单点下稳定的映射（如样本邻域、pierced sets）均准确；4) 针对基分类器加记忆例外的预测器，将测试误差归结为限制在基分类器错误区域上的缺失质量，定义"自适应缺失质量估计"，提出限制区域的 Good–Turing 混合估计量；5) 通过 VC 维与选中区域-样本互信息两种复杂度度量给出误差上界。

Result: 经验插件估计量的误差精确等于泛化差距，泛化界可直接转移；留一法/Good–Turing 估计量对 1-点删除稳定映射具有准确性，涵盖邻域、pierced sets 等新结构实例。对于混合预测器，作者证明基分类器自身的泛化性不足以保证该估计量的准确性，并给出两条误差界（分别基于错误区域族的 VC 维与选中区域同样本的互信息）。

Conclusion: D(φ(S)) 的统一视角把缺失质量、后选择推断与预测器测试误差纳入同一分析体系；估计的准确性取决于映射的稳定性（留一法适用）或选中区域的复杂度（VC 维/互信息控制），而仅靠基分类器的泛化保证并不充分。

Abstract: We study the general problem of estimating $D(\dde(S))$, the probability that an unknown distribution $D$ assigns to an event determined by a sample $S$ of $n$ i.i.d.\ draws from $D$ through a known mapping $\dde$. The estimation algorithm knows $\dde$ and $S$ but does not have access to any additional samples from $D$. This formulation captures classical missing mass, the data-dependent targets of adaptive data analysis and post-selection inference, and the test error of a data-trained predictor, whose estimation was recently studied by \new{Gastpar et al.\ (2024a,b)}. Within this framework we examine the two classical approaches to estimation. The error of the empirical plug-in estimator is exactly a generalization gap, so generalization bounds transfer to it directly. The leave-one-out estimator, which recovers the Good--Turing estimator, is accurate for every mapping that is stable under the deletion of one sample point, including new structured examples such as neighborhoods of the sample and pierced sets.
  The main application of our framework is to error estimation for a predictor that combines a base classifier with memorized exceptions, a structure common in theory and in practice. For it, the test error is exactly the missing mass restricted to the error region of the base classifier, a region selected by the same sample. We formulate the general problem of \emph{adaptive missing mass estimation} to address such dependence and propose a natural hybrid estimator, the Good--Turing estimator restricted to the selected region. We show that the generalization of the base classifier alone does not guarantee the accuracy of this estimator, and derive two bounds on its error: the first one based on the VC dimension of possible error regions and another based on the mutual information of the selected region with the sample.

</details>


### [45] [SPT-Flow: One Integration, Many Futures in Continuous-Time World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41273)
*Mingyang Lyu,Yinqian Sun,Gongce Cao,Yuhao Zhang,Lu Jia,Qian Zhang,Bo Wang,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 连续时间世界模型虽通过单次ODE积分生成潜轨迹，但随机条件输入未必被真正利用；作者提出SPT-Flow，用自由运行的ODE rollout在多个读出时刻对齐示范状态，从而强化轨迹相关潜变量的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在teacher-forcing基线中，后验均值变化仅约占先验尺度的1%，且匹配与互换潜变量在报告精度下重建PSNR不可区分，说明模型未有效利用轨迹相关潜信息。作者据此提出"监督捷径"假说：提供真值中间状态会削弱对轨迹关联潜信息的需求。

Method: 提出SPT-Flow：每条轨迹采样单一潜变量，将自由运行的ODE rollout在多个读出时刻与示范状态匹配；并设计re-anchoring（重锚定）实验检验所提机制。

Result: 在LIBERO上，两者均训练30k优化步，SPT-Flow相较重训的ODEWorld基线，在轨迹中段与末帧的best-of-10 PSNR分别提升2.0 dB与2.4 dB，平均LPIPS降低23%，单样本推理开销相当。rollout控制项恢复了大部分精度增益；学习式潜条件带来多样性与best-of-K增益，但相对该控制项降低了平均样本精度。

Conclusion: 诊断结果支持轨迹相关潜变量确实被利用；但在机器人场景下，匹配条件下的语义模式覆盖仍未验证。

Abstract: Continuous-time world models generate a latent trajectory through a single ODE integration, but adding stochastic conditioning does not ensure that the model uses it. In our teacher-forced baseline, posterior means vary by only about 1% of the prior scale, and matched and swapped latents yield indistinguishable reconstruction PSNR at the reported precision. We study a supervision-shortcut hypothesis: ground-truth intermediate states can reduce the need for trajectory-associated latent information. We introduce SPT-Flow, which samples one latent per trajectory and matches a free-running ODE rollout to demonstration states at multiple readout times. Re-anchoring experiments support the proposed mechanism. On LIBERO, with both models trained for 30k optimizer steps, SPT-Flow improves best-of-10 PSNR over our retrained ODEWorld baseline by 2.0 and 2.4 dB at the mid-trajectory and final frames and reduces mean LPIPS by 23%, with comparable per-sample inference cost. The rollout control recovers most of the accuracy gain; learned latent conditioning adds diversity and best-of-K gains while reducing mean sample accuracy relative to this control. The diagnostics support trajectory-associated latent utilization; semantic mode coverage under matched conditions in the robotic setting remains unverified.

</details>


### [46] [rl_rob: A transparent, from-scratch Python library for teaching reinforcement learning from tabular methods to deep policy gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41303)
*Abdulrahman Altahhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: rl_rob 是一个面向强化学习教学的开源 Python 库，覆盖从表格型 TD 学习、线性函数逼近到深度值函数与策略梯度方法的完整课程脉络，所有算法均从第一性原理实现，使更新规则在数行代码中可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习教学库多封装为黑箱，学习者难以直接观察算法核心更新规则，也难以在不同方法阶段之间进行对比。该库旨在解决教学与评估中"只会写可运行代码"而无法实现、修改和解释更新规则的问题。

Method: 设计统一的智能体接口，使所有算法共享同一交互规范；按课程阶段（表格型 TD → 线性函数逼近 → 深度值函数方法 → 策略梯度）从零实现各算法，保证更新规则以少量代码显式呈现；从而支持跨阶段方法直接对比，并可从 TD 误差追溯到权重变化，完整追踪深度强化学习的更新链路。

Result: 实现了覆盖多阶段强化学习算法的教学库；统一接口支撑跨方法对比与更新链条追踪；可支撑要求学生实现、修改并解释更新规则的教学与考核模式，而非仅产出可运行代码。

Conclusion: rl_rob 以"更新规则可见、接口统一、从零实现"为核心设计理念，为强化学习课程提供兼顾理论透明性与实践可操作性的教学基础设施，强调对算法机理的理解而非仅功能实现。

Abstract: rl_rob is an open-source Python library for teaching reinforcement learning. It implements the arc of a modern course, from tabular temporal-difference learning through linear function approximation to deep value-based and policy-gradient methods, with every algorithm written from first principles so that its update rule is visible in a few lines of code. All agents share one interface, so learners can compare methods directly across stages and trace a deep reinforcement learning update from the temporal-difference error to the weight change. The library supports teaching and assessment in which students implement, modify and explain update rules, rather than only produce working code.

</details>


### [47] [Transferring Peptide-Target Interaction Patterns to Small Molecules by Joint Guidance for Diffusion and Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41322)
*Jian Lee,Changyun Cho,Sun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PIFT框架，将已知多肽-靶标相互作用模式迁移到小分子，通过掩码离散扩散与连续流匹配的联合采样引导生成靶向PPI的小分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPI界面宽而浅，小分子难以像传统药物那样结合深口袋；利用已知多肽配体可提供丰富的界面级约束，但对多肽结构做刚性的原子级条件约束会过度限制生成空间，排除有效的小分子解。

Method: PIFT将多肽-靶标与小分子-靶标界面映射到共享的、以靶标为条件的相互作用空间，获得可迁移的界面参考；该多肽衍生参考在采样时联合引导（building-block）离散logits与3D坐标，在保持适配的分子先验的同时趋向多肽兼容的分子。

Result: 在多种PPI靶标上的实验表明，所学表示能捕捉跨模态相互作用兼容性；多肽引导提升了界面恢复能力，并获得较强的对接表现与良好的分子性质。

Conclusion: 以软性、界面级的跨模态引导替代刚性原子级条件约束，是解决多肽信息向小分子迁移中生成空间受限问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Generating small molecules to target protein-protein interactions (PPIs) remains one of the most challenging yet high-reward frontiers in drug discovery. Unlike traditional small-molecule drugs that bind to deep, well-defined pockets, PPI interfaces often comprise broad, shallow binding surfaces. A natural strategy is to leverage known peptide-target interactions, as peptide ligands provide rich interface-level constraints. However, a major algorithmic challenge is that rigid atom-level conditioning on peptide structures can over-constrain the generative space and exclude valid small-molecule solutions. To address this challenge, we present PIFT (Peptide InterFace Transfer), a novel framework that transfers peptide-target interaction patterns to small molecules through joint sampling-time guidance of masked discrete diffusion and continuous flow matching. PIFT first maps peptide-target and small-molecule-target interfaces into a shared, target-conditioned interaction space, capturing transferable interface information across molecular modalities. The resulting peptide-derived reference then jointly steers building-block logits and 3D coordinates toward peptide-compatible molecules while preserving a strong adapted molecular prior. Extensive experiments across diverse PPI targets show that the learned representation captures cross-modal interaction compatibility, while peptide guidance improves interface recovery and achieves strong docking performance and favorable molecular properties.

</details>


### [48] [StrictPRMBench: A Replayable Evaluation Benchmark for Auditing Fixed-Score PRM Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41333)
*Pengfei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将"分数聚合规则"（reducer）作为独立评估对象，提出 StrictPRMBench 诊断协议，在固定数学推理池、打分证据与删除干预源轨迹下审计选择器的稳定性，发现乘积聚合比几何平均对删除更不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程奖励模型（PRM）为推理步骤打分，但 best-of-N 选择还需一条聚合规则；现有研究多关注打分器本身，聚合规则的影响未被单独评估。

Method: 构建 StrictPRMBench：固定数学推理池、scorer 证据与删除干预的源轨迹，使不同 reducer 面对同一记录化的选择问题；比较乘积 vs 几何平均（GM）；在 7 个打分检查点 × 2 个基准（14 个单元）上基于 N=32 的存储结果计算删除诱导的选择不稳定性；采用共享问题 bootstrap 给出置信区间，并做注入前定义的敏感性分析（剔除任一无污染获胜者的替换槽位，保留 4,622 条中 4,459 条）；提供带版本的结果记录、CPU 复现与明确的证据可用性契约。

Result: 14/14 单元中乘积聚合的删除诱导选择不稳定性均高于 GM，等权单元差 +0.0965，95% 区间 [0.0846, 0.1086]；敏感性分析下保留差值为 +0.0986 [0.0875, 0.1098]。效应异质：两个 Qwen 单元的百分位 bootstrap 区间为正，但双侧精确配对 p 值为 0.0625 与 0.0703；干净池 Qwen 准确率未检出乘积优势，但非等价性结论。

Conclusion: 贡献是诊断性评估协议与对已记录选择器行为的可复现审计，而非新 PRM、通用最优 reducer 或部署层面危害的证据；原始分数回放与结果记录再分析被区分开，缺失的编辑后原始分数向量限制了在同一压力下独立评估新 reducer。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) score reasoning steps, but best-of-N selection also requires a rule that aggregates those scores. We study that rule as a separate evaluation object. StrictPRMBench fixes mathematical reasoning pools, scorer evidence, and the source trace of a deletion intervention, so that different reducers face the same recorded selection problem. Across seven scoring checkpoints and two benchmarks, the stored N=32 outcomes give higher deletion-induced selection instability for product than geometric mean (GM) in all 14 cells. The equal-cell difference is +0.0965; a shared-problem bootstrap, conditional on these checkpoints and frozen pools, gives a 95% interval of [0.0846,0.1086]. A pre-injection-defined sensitivity excludes replacement slots that are either reducer's clean winner and retains 4,459 of 4,622 cell-problem records. The retained difference is +0.0986 [0.0875,0.1098]. Effects are heterogeneous: the two Qwen cells have positive percentile-bootstrap intervals on retained support but two-sided exact paired p-values of 0.0625 and 0.0703. Clean-pool Qwen accuracy does not detect a product advantage, but this is not an equivalence result. We provide versioned outcome records, CPU reanalysis, and an explicit evidence-availability contract. The contribution is a diagnostic evaluation protocol and a reproducible audit of recorded selector behavior, not a new PRM, a universally optimal reducer, or evidence of deployment-wide harm. Raw-score replay and outcome-record reanalysis are distinguished: missing primary edited-score vectors limit independent evaluation of new reducers under the same stress.

</details>


### [49] [Quantile Propagation Planning for Budgeted Tail-Risk Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41351)
*Weijia Han,Zhi Huang,Lisha Qu,Yuanyuan Tan,Jiayue Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分位数传播规划(QPP)：将少量固定扰动分位数通过动力学传播并积分代价曲线，以极低模型评估预算同时评估均值与尾风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险感知规划需在有限模型评估下识别稀有高代价结果。蒙特卡洛 rollout 把预算耗在噪声大的尾部估计上，而基于均值的规划可能完全忽略尾部。

Method: QPP 传播一小套固定扰动分位数穿过系统动力学，对所得代价曲线积分，从而同时刻画均值与尾部风险；在传播分位点上精确，随分位网格加密收敛，并在给定结构条件下具有单侧保守性保证。

Result: 在受控过载与库存任务中，相较均值 rollout 规划，QPP 降低了实际尾部代价与约束违反；在过载任务上与使用远多轨迹的采样评估器保持竞争力。

Conclusion: QPP 是一种透明、预算可控的尾部风险评估方法。

Abstract: Risk-aware planning must identify rare, costly outcomes using only a limited number of model evaluations. Monte Carlo rollouts spend this budget on noisy tail estimates, while planning from the mean can miss the tail entirely. We introduce quantile propagation planning (QPP), which propagates a small, fixed set of disturbance quantiles through the dynamics and integrates the resulting cost curve to evaluate both mean and tail risk. The method is exact at the propagated levels, converges as the quantile grid is refined, and admits a one-sided conservatism guarantee under stated structural conditions. In controlled overload and inventory tasks, QPP reduces realized tail cost and constraint violations relative to mean-rollout planning. On overload, it remains competitive with sampled evaluators that use substantially more trajectories. These results position QPP as a transparent, budget-controlled approach to tail-risk evaluation.

</details>


### [50] [Generated storm lifecycles fill the rare-event tail of short rainfall records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41365)
*Valerie Tsao,Nathaniel W. Chaney,Manolis Veveakis,Marta Zaniolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StormGen是一个生成式机器学习模型，可将风暴初期数小时观测转化为完整、物理合理的逐小时降雨生命周期，用于极端风暴的应力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史罕见的“一代一遇”风暴频率空前上升，造成严重灾害，而基础设施设计依赖稀疏的短观测记录，难以覆盖未见的极端事件。

Method: 提出生成式模型StormGen，以观测风暴最初几小时降雨为输入，在数分钟内生成完整的逐小时降雨生命周期序列，并保持空间一致性。

Result: 在德州东南部十年降雨数据上评估，对最严重2%风暴中的45%生成了高度相似的对应事件（随机风暴移置法仅3%），并在24小时序列上保持空间连贯性；仅凭1小时初始降雨即成功重建了训练中完全缺失的飓风Harvey的具体生命周期、空间格局与极端流域累积雨量。

Conclusion: 鉴于风暴时间安排本身即可使峰值流量增加一倍以上，完整生成的情景为针对未经历极端事件的防洪保护应力测试提供了关键手段。

Abstract: Historically rare ''one-in-a-generation'' storms are occurring with unprecedented frequency, causing catastrophic damage and mounting casualties, and exposing the vulnerability of infrastructure designed around short observational records. To overcome the limitations of sparse data, we present StormGen, a generative machine-learning model that transforms an observed storm's initial hours into complete, physically plausible hourly rainfall lifecycles in minutes. Evaluated on a decade of southeast Texas rainfall, StormGen produced close counterparts for 4\5% of the most severe 2\% of storms--compared to just 3\% for stochastic storm transposition--and maintained spatial coherence over full 24-hour sequences. Crucially, from a single hour of initial rainfall, the model successfully reconstructed the specific lifecycle, spatial pattern, and extreme catchment totals of Hurricane Harvey itself, despite the event being entirely absent in training. Because storm timing alone can more than double peak flows, these complete generated scenarios provide a vital mechanism to stress-test flood protections against unprecedented extremes.

</details>


### [51] [What We Keep of What We Forget: Replacement Operators and Routing Semantics in Training-Free MoE Expert Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41366)
*Ezza Ali,Sehrish Bibi,Momina Moetesum,Faisal Shafait*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定剪枝掩码的前提下，系统比较「被移除专家被选中时该层计算什么」的替换算子族，发现最大影响来自幸存专家门控缩放（是否重归一化），并提出低秩闭式映射「tilted novice」及五项报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练专家剪枝同时决定「删哪些专家」和「路由器仍选中被删专家时该层算什么」，已有方法两者同时改变，无法归因；本文意在解耦替换算子本身的影响，并揭示被忽视的实现选择（门控缩放）与评估设定对结论的左右。

Method: 固定掩码，在 OLMoE、DeepSeek-V2-Lite、Moonlight（另以 Granite 作为第四种路由器）上，用配对、多重性校正的统计检验比较三类替换算子族；考察幸存者门控是否重归一化；提出 tilted novice——对被剪枝专家输入的闭式低秩映射（秩 0 退化为 MoNE 的常数输出）；并分析量化、校准数据分布与评估任务的影响。

Result: 在未做 top-k 重归一化训练的路由器上，对幸存者门控重归一化会损失 0.8–8.5 分，并反转 rerouting 与 MoNE 均值替换的优劣；在两种经重归一化训练的路由器上，50% 剪枝时训练尺度最优。50% 剪枝下 tilted novice 增加 1.2–1.8% 参数，在 OLMoE 与 Moonlight 上较常数分别提升 3.45 与 1.37 分，跨校准抽样稳定，4-bit 与 3-bit 量化下基本不变。无算子全面占优：DeepSeek-V2-Lite 上训练尺度 rerouting 最佳，部分拷贝在三个模型中的两个上与 tilted novice 统计不可区分。25% 剪枝时 MoNE 常数保留 92–97% 稠密多选题精度，但仅保留 5–65% GSM8K 与 0–17% 代码分数；校准域对掩码的影响是重采样的 4–9 倍。

Conclusion: 替换算子的选择高度依赖路由器训练方式与评估任务，门控缩放这一隐性实现选择影响最大；tilted novice 以极小参数代价提供稳健增益但不普遍最优；评估协议可翻转结论，故提出五项报告清单以规范免训练专家剪枝的对比与报告。

Abstract: Training-free expert pruning makes two decisions: which experts to remove, and what the layer computes when its router still selects a removed expert. Published methods change both at once. Holding the mask fixed, we compare replacement operators from three families on OLMoE, DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Moonlight, with Granite as a fourth router, using paired, multiplicity-corrected tests. The largest effect is a choice usually left implicit: the survivors' gate scale. On routers trained without top-k renormalization, renormalizing the survivors' gates, a common implementation choice, costs 0.8-8.5 points and reverses whether rerouting beats MoNE's mean-output replacement; on two routers trained with renormalization, the trained scale wins at 50% pruning. We introduce the tilted novice, a closed-form low-rank map of the pruned expert's input whose rank-0 case is MoNE's constant. At 50% pruning it adds 1.2-1.8% parameters and gains 3.45 and 1.37 points over the constant on OLMoE and Moonlight, stable across calibration draws and largely unchanged by 4- and 3-bit quantization. No operator dominates: trained-scale rerouting is best on DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and a partial copy of each pruned expert is statistically indistinguishable from the tilted novice on two models. Evaluation can change conclusions: at 25% pruning MoNE's constant keeps 92-97% of dense multiple-choice accuracy but only 5-65% of GSM8K and 0-17% of code scores, and the calibration domain moves the mask 4-9 times more than resampling. We propose a five-item reporting checklist.

</details>


### [52] [Anomalies as Residuals: Cauchy Displacement Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41387)
*Yang Cao,Zihao Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cauchy Displacement Network（CDN），把KNN/LOF/LUNAR等邻域型检测器所利用的邻域几何"编译"进一个小型单隐层网络，单次前向即可打分；46个基准、28个基线中AUC-ROC与AUC-PR平均排名最优，且推理快至三个数量级、模型尺寸与训练集无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邻域型检测器（KNN、LOF、LUNAR）在表格异常检测中精度最高，但每次查询都需与全训练集比较，推理成本与内存随数据规模增长，且部署时必须保留训练数据。作者希望将这些检测器所利用的邻域几何编译为一个小型网络，仅靠一次前向传播完成打分，从而摆脱对训练集的依赖。

Method: CDN为单隐层Cauchy激活网络，将每个正常样本回归到其k个最近训练邻居的均值上；查询时的异常分数为预测位移向量的长度。该位移即经验均值漂移（mean-shift）向量，其固定半径的总体对应量在局部近似于对数密度的缩放梯度。理论上证明：Cauchy单元可精确表示一类结构化Student-t专家密度的score场；给出单隐层Cauchy网络对相应总体邻域均值映射的构造性逼近界；并证明有界网络输出保证残差分数沿任意固定方向呈渐近线性增长。

Result: 在46个基准数据集上对比28个基线，CDN在AUC-ROC与AUC-PR下均取得最佳平均排名；单CPU线程几毫秒内完成10^4次查询，比邻域型检测器快最多三个数量级，且模型规模不随训练集大小变化。

Conclusion: CDN表明邻域几何可被有效蒸馏为紧凑参数化网络：在保持甚至超越邻域检测器精度的同时，实现常数级内存占用与极低推理延迟，为表格异常检测的可部署化提供了理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Neighbourhood-based detectors such as KNN, LOF and LUNAR remain among the most accurate methods for tabular anomaly detection, but they compare every query with the stored training set, so their inference cost and memory grow with the data and the data must be kept at deployment. We ask whether the neighbourhood geometry these detectors exploit can be compiled into a small network that scores a query with a single forward pass. We propose the Cauchy Displacement Network (CDN), which regresses each normal point onto the mean of its $k$ nearest training neighbours with a single-hidden-layer network of Cauchy activations and scores a query by the length of its predicted displacement. This displacement is an empirical mean-shift vector, whose fixed-radius population counterpart locally approximates a scaled gradient of the log density. We show that Cauchy units exactly represent the score fields of a structured family of Student-$t$ expert densities, and establish a constructive approximation bound for the corresponding population neighbourhood-mean map using a single-hidden-layer Cauchy network. We further show that the bounded network output guarantees asymptotically linear residual-score growth along every fixed direction. On 46 benchmark datasets against 28 baselines, CDN obtains the best mean rank under both AUC-ROC and AUC-PR, while scoring $10^4$ queries in a few milliseconds on a single CPU thread, up to three orders of magnitude faster than neighbourhood-based detectors, with a model whose size does not depend on the training set.

</details>


### [53] [Certifying Retraining Regret through Residual Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41410)
*Peida Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对区间值数据表的“全特征重训练”场景，为固定岭回归预测器建立逐行残差认证框架，给出与冗余特征尺度/秩无关的一致误差上界并可计算地得到紧致认证宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区间值表格中未知特征缺失（nuisance/不可观测字段）导致对候选预测器的真实风险无法直接评估，需要可验证的保证：在任意连续补全下，候选固定岭预测器相对全特征重训练的后悔（regret）是否被一致界定，且上界不应随冗余特征尺度与秩恶化。

Method: 以原始行残差条件构造一致误差上界，证明其具有渐近锐利的主项常数与定量泄漏稳定性；用满秩构造将认证维度与特征维度解耦；用观测行锚点使条件可计算，结合精确行几何与全局搜索返回有效上界及可行原始表见证；并以有限尺度收缩与公共域矩回退实现结构结果。

Result: 四个真实任务保留全部模型特征，将20个秩三实例认证到宽度1e-5，主宽度中位数优于中心化矩方法；联合残差细化在76.21秒内将全部20个新响应无关掩码认证到同一精度；在固定点预测器契约下，纯能量法10秒内认证13/15停止决策（矩法仅5个），细化在91.68秒内以同一目标阈值认证全部15个。

Conclusion: 原始行残差条件是区间值补全下岭回归后悔认证的有效且可计算工具，能给出尺度/秩无关的紧致上界，并在真实任务与固定点契约中显著优于基于矩的基线，兼顾认证效率与精度。

Abstract: We certify a fixed ridge predictor's regret against full-feature retraining over every continuous completion of an interval-valued table. Our main theorem gives verifiable original-row residual conditions for a uniform error bound independent of nuisance-feature scale and rank, with an asymptotically sharp leading constant and quantitative leakage stability. Full-rank constructions separate certification dimension from feature dimension, including examples requiring directions beyond the candidate. Observed-row anchors make the conditions computable, while exact row geometry and global search return valid upper bounds and feasible original-table witnesses. A finite-scale contraction and common-domain moment fallback implement the structural result. Four real-world tasks retain all model features and certify twenty rank-three instances to width $10^{-5}$. Their primary median widths improve over centered moments. Joint residual refinement additionally certifies all twenty new response-independent masks to the same precision within 76.21 seconds. For a concrete fixed-point predictor contract, pure energy certifies thirteen of fifteen stopping decisions in ten seconds, versus five for moments. Refinement certifies all fifteen within 91.68 seconds at the same objective threshold.

</details>


### [54] [SparseGDN: Chunk Sparse in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41420)
*Jiedong Zhuang,Peng Zhao,Jialin Tang,Haoji Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SparseGDN 是一个硬件感知框架，通过块内结构化近似与输入依赖的循环事件选择，在保留逐 token 输出的前提下降低 GDN 长上下文预填充的计算、更新与访存开销，取得 1.50–1.94x 的 GDN 核心预填充加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDN 具备线性时间序列建模能力，但其长上下文预填充仍受限于稠密的块内计算、循环状态更新以及中间状态存储，导致成本高昂。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 块相邻求解器（block-adjacent solver）降低局部 Delta-solve 计算量；(2) 动态选择器在头对（head pairs）间分配循环更新预算，实现输入依赖的稀疏事件选择；(3) GPU 实现仅对选中事件实例化更新，并利用紧凑状态快照与组内局部交互重建 token 输出，从而减少计算与显存访问。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-4B 与 Qwen3.5-9B 上，于 RULER 任务及 4K–128K 上下文长度评测，平均保留约 29% 的循环事件，相较原生 FLA 内核获得 1.50–1.94x 的 GDN 核心预填充加速（含在线选择开销）。

Conclusion: 结构化块内近似与输入依赖的循环事件稀疏化可有效降低 GDN 长上下文预填充开销，且在大幅稀疏（~71% 事件被丢弃）下仍保持逐 token 输出完整性；结果提示该加速在长上下文范围内具备实用性，但摘要未报告精度损失细节。

Abstract: Gated Delta Networks (GDNs) offer linear-time sequence modeling, yet long-context prefill remains costly due to dense intra-chunk computation, recurrent-state updates, and intermediate state storage. We introduce SparseGDN, a hardware-aware framework that combines structured intra-chunk approximation with input-dependent recurrent-event selection while producing outputs for every token. A block-adjacent solver reduces local Delta-solve computation, while a dynamic selector allocates recurrent-update budgets across head pairs. The GPU implementation materializes updates only for selected events and reconstructs token outputs using compact state snapshots and group-local interactions, reducing computation and memory traffic. We evaluate SparseGDN on Qwen3.5-4B and Qwen3.5-9B across RULER tasks and context lengths from 4K to 128K. Retaining approximately 29% of recurrent events on average, SparseGDN achieves 1.50-1.94x GDN-core prefill speedups over native FLA kernels, including online selection overhead.

</details>


### [55] [A Unified XAI-NSAI Analytical Framework for Trustworthy IoT Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41424)
*Hugues Leger,Giovani R. Abuaitah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一分析框架，从五个互补维度联合审视可解释AI（XAI）与神经符号AI（NSAI）在IoT异常检测中的角色，用于刻画现有方法、识别能力缺口与设计权衡，并指导新系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备在关键环境中广泛部署，异常检测需同时具备准确性与可解释性，并满足领域知识融合、资源约束下运行及支持人类决策等实际需求。尽管已有工作分别引入XAI或NSAI，但二者常被独立看待，缺乏对其互补作用的统一理解。

Method: 提出一个围绕五个互补维度组织的统一分析框架，用于结构化地刻画现有XAI与NSAI方法、识别能力缺口与设计权衡。框架的分析效用通过应用于三项代表性研究加以验证，设计效用通过应用于一个医疗IoT异常检测系统加以说明。

Result: 框架能够系统刻画现有方法的能力边界与取舍关系；在三项代表性研究的分析中展示了其区分与比较能力；在医疗IoT异常检测系统的案例中展示了其指导系统设计的作用。

Conclusion: XAI与NSAI应被联合而非孤立地考虑，以支撑可信的IoT异常检测；所提五维框架可作为分析、比较与设计新系统的结构化工具。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of Internet of Things (IoT) devices in critical environments makes anomaly detection essential for identifying abnormal behaviors and supporting timely investigation and response. However, accurate detection alone is insufficient. Practical IoT deployments must also provide explanations, incorporate domain knowledge and reasoning, operate under IoT constraints, and support human decision making. Although recently proposed approaches increasingly incorporate Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) or Neuro-Symbolic Artificial Intelligence (NSAI), XAI and NSAI methods are often considered independently. We argue that they should be considered together to better understand their complementary roles in supporting trustworthy IoT anomaly detection. To address this gap, we propose a unified analytical framework organized around five complementary dimensions. The framework provides a structured basis for characterizing existing approaches, identifying capability gaps and design trade-offs, and guiding the design of new anomaly detection systems for different IoT applications. We demonstrate the framework's analytical utility by applying it to three representative studies and illustrate its design utility through its application to a Medical IoT anomaly detection system.

</details>


### [56] [From Representation to Readout: Mechanistic Analysis of Membership Signals in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41445)
*Wenxi Dai,Jingyu Hu,Yanting Yang,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从机制层面拆解大语言模型中的成员信号，提出DUG框架分别度量内部表示中可解码的成员信息与经读出层暴露的程度，发现SFT下成员效应同时体现在可解码信息与读出上，RL类方法下主要体现在读出上，激活修补将效应定位至中间层，并发现RLVR污染下行为变化可不伴随似然类成员推断信号增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成员推断中的"记忆"常被笼统描述，但其背后机制既涉及信息在内部如何表示，也涉及模型如何访问这些信息以产生行为，二者未被区分研究，因此需要机制性分析。

Method: 提出Decodable Information Score、Used Information Score与Readout Accessibility Gap（DUG）三项互补指标，分别量化可从内部表示解码的成员信息及经读出层暴露的程度；在SFT与基于RL的成员推断方法下开展对比分析，并结合激活修补（activation patching）定位效应层位；进一步考察可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）污染场景（训练奖励与参考解无关）。

Result: SFT下成员特异效应同时出现在可解码信息量与读出中；RL类成员推断方法下主要出现在读出中；激活修补将成员特异效应定位到中间层；RLVR污染场景中，行为变化可在似然类成员推断信号未增强的情况下出现。

Conclusion: 成员信号应被分解为"表示"与"读出"两个机制维度加以评估；仅依赖似然类信号的成员推断可能低估或遗漏训练数据影响，机制性度量对理解与检测LLM记忆更为充分。

Abstract: Membership inference aims to determine whether a particular example was included in a model's training data. Such membership signals are often broadly described as memorization, while the underlying mechanisms can involve both how information is represented internally and how it is accessed by the model to produce behaviors. We therefore present a mechanistic study of membership signals in large language models (LLMs). We introduce Decodable Information Score, Used Information Score, and Readout Accessibility Gap (DUG), a set of complementary measures that separately quantify membership information decodable from internal representations and the extent to which it is exposed through the model's readout. Across supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL)-based membership inference methods, membership-specific effects appear in both the amount of decodable information and readout under SFT, while under RL-based membership inference methods they appear primarily in readout. Activation patching further localizes this membership-specific effect to intermediate layers. To examine whether these findings extend to a more general RL setting, we further study reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) contamination, where the training reward is independent of the reference solution. In this setting, we find that behavioral changes can emerge without a corresponding increase in likelihood-based membership inference signals.

</details>


### [57] [When Everyone Can Build a Model: Mechanistic Inference in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41454)
*Marvin van Aalst,Adèle H. Ribeiro,Anna Matuszyńska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度学习与AI辅助代码生成降低了生物系统数学模型构建门槛，可构建"灰箱"半机制混合模型，但预测成功不等于机制正确。作者主张用因果推断处理变量间影响关系的可识别性，用集成分析处理响应函数的不确定性，将单一拟合转化为对"支持什么、开放什么、哪种实验可判定"的明确陈述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助建模使构建拟合复杂观测的半机制、混合（灰箱）模型变得容易，但可解释性并未随之提高：预测成功不能证明推断出的机制就是数据生成机制，学习组件会吸收机制部分的误差；许多生物学建模问题欠定，多个甚至无穷多个模型可同等拟合同一数据。这种歧义源于两类建模选择——变量间因果关系与响应函数形式——因此需要专门的方法来界定数据与先验知识支持的结论边界。

Method: 概念性/方法论框架：针对第一个选择（哪些变量影响哪些变量），采用因果推断框架，形式化判定哪些因果关系与干预效应可从数据和已有知识中识别；针对第二个选择（哪种响应函数描述机制），采用集成分析（ensemble analyses）量化不确定性，刻画同等拟合模型所允许的行为范围。

Result: 两类工具共同把"单个良好拟合"升级为显式说明：哪些结论有数据支持、哪些仍悬而未决、以及哪种实验能够作出判定。因果推断给出可识别性的形式保证，集成分析给出拟合等价模型的行为与不确定性范围。

Conclusion: AI降低建模门槛的同时加剧了可解释性与可识别性风险；仅有预测成功不足以确立机制。应将因果推断与集成分析纳入灰箱生物建模流程，以明确区分被支持的机制结论与开放问题，并指导后续实验设计。

Abstract: Modern deep learning and AI-assisted code generation are lowering the technical barrier to building mathematical models of biological systems that reproduce complex observations. This lets researchers with domain expertise build semi-mechanistic, hybrid models constrained by prior knowledge and augmented with learned structure without extensive training in modelling. However, easier construction does not make interpretation easier: predictive success of such so-called grey-box models does not establish that an inferred mechanism corresponds to the one that generated the data, and the learned component can absorb errors in the mechanistic part. Many biological modelling problems are underdetermined, so several, possibly infinitely many, models may reproduce the same data equally well. This ambiguity enters at two modelling choices: which variables can affect others, and which response function describes the underlying mechanism. For the first choice, causal inference provides a formal framework stating which causal relationships and intervention effects are identifiable from the data and available knowledge. For the second, ensemble analyses quantify the uncertainty and the range of behaviours still allowed by models that fit equally well. Together, they turn a single good fit into an explicit account of what is supported, what remains open, and which experiment would decide.

</details>


### [58] [Improving the Optimization Landscape of Matrix Completion with $ε$-close Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41455)
*Ziye Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在矩阵补全（MC）这一非凸恢复问题中，通过向观测掩码的零空间注入 ε 级噪声扰动，构造噪声矩阵感知替代问题，证明其解与真值保持 O(ε) 接近，并满足有效的 RIP 常数，从而获得无采样率与不相干性显式约束的定量恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸优化是机器学习与 AI 的核心，但相较于凸优化仍缺乏通用且原理化的理论框架。作者希望在经典矩阵补全设定下，从数学上阐明"噪声扰动 + 过参数化"相结合的重要性，并为噪声改善优化景观曲率这一经验现象提供理论依据。

Method: 在矩阵补全设定下（仅观测部分元素、需低秩约束恢复），向观测掩码的零空间注入 ε 级扰动，构造带噪的矩阵感知代理模型；证明在有效的受限等距性质（RIP）常数下，代理问题的解与真实矩阵保持 O(ε) 距离。RIP 的存在使张量方法框架可带理论保证地应用。假设各矩阵元素独立观测。

Result: 建立了定量恢复保证，且无需对采样率或不相干性（incoherence）作显式要求；解与真值的误差为 O(ε)，并可借助 RIP 引入张量框架的理论保证。文中亦讨论了额外假设如何进一步增强结论。

Conclusion: 将噪声扰动与过参数化在数学上结合，可为矩阵补全这类困难非凸问题提供原理化分析路径；随机性或噪声通过改善优化景观曲率，帮助过参数化大模型更好地区分信号与噪声，为更一般的非凸优化框架提供了思路。

Abstract: Non-convex optimization is central to machine learning and AI, yet unlike its convex counterpart, it still lacks a general and principled framework. In this work, we try to highlight the importance of combining noisy perturbations with over-parametrization mathematically under the classic setting of matrix completion (MC). Matrix completion is an important non-convex recovery problem in which only a subset of a matrix's entries is observed, and the remaining entries must be recovered using low-rank constraints. This problem is notoriously difficult because observations are limited. However, we show that by injecting $ε$-level perturbations into the nullspace of the observation mask, we can construct noisy matrix sensing surrogates whose solutions remain $\mathcal{O}(ε)$-close to the ground truth with valid restricted isometry property (RIP) constants. The existence of RIP further enables powerful tensor frameworks to be applied with theoretical guarantees. This approach reflects a broader empirical trend in which stochasticity or noise improves the curvature of the optimization landscape, allowing over-parameterized (a.k.a large) models to better separate signal from noise. Assuming each matrix entry is observed independently, we establish quantitative recovery guarantees without explicit requirements on sampling rate or incoherence, and discuss how such assumptions can further strengthen our results.

</details>


### [59] [NormDetect: Anchored Calibration for Low-False-Positive Detection of Machine-Generated Web Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41459)
*Hanshuai Cui,Fanshuai Meng,Zhiqing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 NormDetect：将按板块学习的检测头锚定到归一化零样本分数（Binoculars），在人类文本 FPR 上限约束下用训练折选择混合权重、仅用人类文本校准单一阈值，在 RAID 八个板块十一种攻击下，0.1% 校准 FPR 处召回较归一化 Binoculars 提升 5.0 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台需以极低误报率（FPR）筛查 LLM 生成文本，因为每次误报都伤及真实人类作者。零样本检测器（如 Binoculars）依赖冻结语言模型打分，但同形字等不可见编辑会破坏分词，使机器文本落入人类分数区间。Unicode 归一化可消除这类编辑，但仍有"无可清洗"的攻击导致严格 FPR 下漏检约一半机器文本；同时实验发现基于 token 统计的检测头在该 FPR 下召回低于归一化分数，且部分板块对人类文本误报过多。

Method: 1) 将学习到的"按板块"检测头锚定（anchor）到归一化零样本分数，形成两路分数；2) 在训练折上、于人类 FPR 上限约束下选择混合权重，融合两路分数；3) 仅使用人类文本校准单一判定阈值，不依赖机器文本标注。

Result: 在 RAID 基准八个板块、十一种攻击设置下，0.1% 校准 FPR 处召回较归一化 Binoculars 提升 5.0 个百分点；各板块人类 FPR 上界均不超过 1%；在该 FPR 约束下，无可比检测器取得更高召回。

Conclusion: 将板块级学习头与归一化零样本分数锚定融合，并以纯人类文本校准阈值，可在严格误报约束下显著提升低 FPR 区间的检测召回，且误报上界可控，适合真实平台部署场景。

Abstract: Web platforms screen posts for text written by large language models, and every false flag hits a human writer. Detectors must therefore run at a very low false positive rate (FPR). Zero-shot detectors such as Binoculars score text with frozen language models, but invisible edits such as homoglyphs shatter tokenization and push machine text into the human range. Unicode normalization undoes these edits, yet the normalized score still misses half of the machine text at a strict FPR, under attacks that leave nothing to clean. We find that with the Falcon-7B scoring pair, heads on token statistics recall less than the normalized score at this FPR, and several flag too many human posts in some sections. We propose NormDetect, which anchors a learned per-section head to the normalized zero-shot score. A blend weight chosen on training folds under a human FPR cap combines the two scores, and one threshold is calibrated on human text only. On eight sections of the RAID benchmark with eleven attacks, NormDetect raises recall at a 0.1% calibrated FPR by 5.0 points over normalized Binoculars. Its human FPR bound stays at most 1% in every section, and no other detector within this bound recalls as much.

</details>


### [60] [RetryRoute: Retry-Aware Model Routing for Agentic Image Editing Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41460)
*Hanshuai Cui,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Jun Luo,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RetryRoute：一个感知失败历史的"重试感知"路由器，为Web agent的图像编辑调用做重试/升级/放弃的规划，并把队列等待计入成本，从而在同等预算下显著提升成功率与SLO达标率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web agent 以"批评—重试"循环调用开放图像编辑模型，模型间成本差异超过一个数量级。每次编辑被拒后，服务需决定重试同一模型、升级到其他模型还是放弃。现有路由器是"每请求选一个模型"，级联则按固定顺序升级，二者都把重试当作独立抽样，忽略了失败的相关性。

Method: 作者首先识别并量化"粘性失败"（sticky failures）：在6个开放模型、3个基准、2个评判器上，失败后重试的成功率仅为首次尝试的17%–53%。据此提出RetryRoute：在失败状态空间上估计"给定请求失败历史时各模型的成功概率"，并在此状态上联合规划重试、升级与放弃；同时把实时队列等待计入成本，使同一规划成为负载感知的调度策略。

Result: 在主要预算档位下，RetryRoute 在循环内评判器下的成功率在8个实验组上平均超出11个最强基线中最佳者2.8个百分点；在GPU服务测试台上，各负载下均将满足SLO的编辑交付比例提升5.0–6.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 重试并非独立抽样而是强相关事件，显式建模失败历史可显著改善路由收益；将失败状态规划与队列等待统一进成本模型，可同时提升质量与服务水平，且优于固定级联与单次选模型的路由范式。

Abstract: Web agents increasingly edit images by calling open editing models in a critique-and-retry loop, and these models differ in cost by more than an order of magnitude. After every rejected edit, a service must decide whether to retry the same model, escalate to another or give up. Existing routers pick one model per request, and cascades escalate in a fixed order. However, both treat a retry as an independent draw. We identify sticky failures, meaning that a retry after a failure succeeds only 17-53% as often as a first try across six open models, three benchmarks and two judges. Based on this finding, we propose RetryRoute, a retry-aware router that estimates each model's chance of success given the request's failure history. It plans retries, escalations and abandonment over failure states and adds live queue waits to the cost, so the same plan becomes load-aware dispatch. At the main budget, RetryRoute's success under the in-loop judge exceeds the strongest of eleven prior baselines by 2.8 points on average over eight groups. On a GPU serving testbed it raises the share of edits delivered within the service-level objective by 5.0-6.3 points over the best prior baseline at every load.

</details>


### [61] [When Links Mislead: Label-Free Structural Polarity for Zero-Shot Classification of Web Pages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41464)
*Hanshuai Cui,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Weijia Jia,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结构极性（structural polarity）统计量，用其符号门控一步有符号标签传播，实现无需训练的零样本网页分类方法 ZeroPolar 及其增强版 ZeroPolar-T。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字图书馆、在线商店、高校站点等平台需在无人工标注前对新页面分类。LLM 可依据文本打标签，但标签噪声大；链接本可用于纠错，但仅在相连页面同类时有效。现有零样本方法要么忽略链接，要么像标签传播和 GNN 那样把节点拉向邻居，在异配（邻居类别不同）时反而有害；另有方法用 LLM 估计同配率来加权传播，需额外调用且随模型漂移。

Method: 定义"结构极性"：沿链接方向上 LLM 标签与文本原型预测（prototype scores）的一致超额量，无需真实标签即可读出。ZeroPolar 以该统计量作为门控，执行一步有符号传播，混合原型得分与任意数量的 LLM 标签；ZeroPolar-T 在半数以上页面带标签时，用文本分类器对标签去噪。

Result: 每页一个 GPT-4o 标签时，ZeroPolar-T 在 11 个图上取得最高平均准确率 73.99%，较 LLM-BP 高 6.01 个点，并显著优于全部 18 个基线；仅 5% 页面有 LLM 标签时，无训练的 ZeroPolar 比用这些标签训练的 GNN 高 10.80 个点。

Conclusion: 链接进入预测的符号（结构极性）可在无真标签条件下从 LLM 标签与文本原型的一致性中估计，据此门控的有符号单步传播能同时利用链接与 LLM 标签，在低标注乃至零标注场景下显著优于忽略链接、无符号传播及 GNN 类方法。

Abstract: Web platforms such as digital libraries, online stores and university sites must classify new pages before anyone labels them. Large language models (LLMs) can label each page from its text, but these labels are noisy, and links could correct them only where linked pages share classes. Existing zero-shot methods ignore the links, or pull a page toward its neighbours as label propagation and graph neural networks (GNNs) do, which hurts where linked pages differ in class. Others weight propagation by an LLM-estimated homophily that costs extra calls and drifts across LLMs. We find that the sign with which links should enter a prediction, which we call structural polarity, is readable without true labels. It is the excess agreement of LLM labels and text-prototype predictions along links. We thus propose ZeroPolar, which gates one signed propagation step by this statistic. Training-free ZeroPolar mixes prototype scores with any number of LLM labels, and ZeroPolar-T denoises the labels with a text classifier once half of the pages carry one. With one GPT-4o label per page, ZeroPolar-T reaches the highest mean accuracy on eleven graphs (73.99%), 6.01 points above LLM-BP and significantly above all eighteen baselines we compare. With LLM labels on only 5% of the pages, training-free ZeroPolar leads a GNN trained on these labels by 10.80 points.

</details>


### [62] [Spectral Haar Binarizer: Compact Boolean Contrast Representations for Hyperspectral Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41478)
*Michał Romaszewski,Przemysław Głomb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Spectral Haar Binarizer（SHB），用相邻光谱区间差分构造类 Haar 对比布尔谓词，经 AdaBoost 式选择得到紧凑判别性特征，在五个异质高光谱数据集上稳定优于逐波段二值化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsetlin Machine 可解释、易硬件实现，但处理连续高光谱数据需先转成布尔表示；传统逐波段独立离散化会丢失相邻波长间的关联信息。

Method: SHB 基于 Haar 型对比特征，在选定的光谱区间之间取差值构建布尔谓词，并用 AdaBoost 风格的迭代选择程序筛选出紧凑且具判别力的光谱谓词集合，接入 TM、RF 等分类器。

Result: 在涵盖法医血迹检测、农业与城市遥感、雨林树种分类的五个异质高光谱数据集上，二分类与多分类任务中 SHB 均一致优于逐波段二值化；二分类中 TM-SHB 平均性能最佳，多分类中 RF-SHB 最强；贝叶斯与秩检验分析表明优势稳健、非个别数据集驱动。

Conclusion: 连续高光谱测量的表示方式是高效分类的核心环节；基于对比的布尔特征可有效衔接信息丰富的传感器测量与紧凑的逻辑型处理。注意力应放在表示层而非仅分类器本身。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machines offer an interpretable, hardware-friendly alternative to conventional machine learning models, but applying them to continuous hyperspectral measurements requires converting spectral signals into Boolean representations. Standard binarisation methods typically discretise each spectral band independently, potentially discarding important relationships between neighbouring wavelengths. In this paper, we introduce the Spectral Haar Binarizer (SHB), a contrast-based spectral representation method that constructs Boolean predicates from differences between selected spectral intervals. Inspired by Haar-like contrast features, the method uses an AdaBoost-style selection procedure to obtain a compact set of discriminative spectral predicates. We evaluate SHB on five heterogeneous hyperspectral datasets covering forensic blood detection, agricultural and urban remote sensing, and rainforest tree-species classification. Across binary and multiclass tasks, SHB consistently outperforms standard per-band binarisation strategies and provides the strongest or near-strongest results across classifiers. In binary tasks, TM-SHB achieves the best average performance, while RF-SHB is strongest in multiclass settings. Bayesian and rank-based analyses confirm that SHB's advantage is robust across datasets and not driven by isolated cases. The results show that representing continuous hyperspectral measurements is a central processing step for efficient classification. That contrast-based Boolean features provide an effective bridge between information-rich sensor measurements and compact, logic-based processing.

</details>


### [63] [PCD: Predictive Coding Dynamics for Irregularly Sampled Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41506)
*Tianrui Gou,Jiayangmei Xu,Xingzhou Zhang,Lei Sha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预测编码动力学（PCD），一种用于不规则连续动态模型的残差反馈机制，通过将掩码有符号残差以能量惩罚形式注入状态识别过程，提升稀疏异步观测下的预测与轨迹恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景观测稀疏、不规则且异步，而动态系统连续演化，未来值依赖部分观测的轨迹，因此需要在观测间推断潜状态演化；现有连续动态模型缺乏对观测一致性的显式反馈利用。

Method: PCD 维护历史重建，由重建预测观测条目，计算掩码有符号残差，并通过能量惩罚将残差并入状态识别；基于 ODE-RNN 与 Latent ODE 实现，支持事件驱动的隐状态更新与潜状态识别。

Result: 在预测与轨迹恢复任务上，PCD 在稀疏、不规则采样条件下改进了连续动态骨干模型；结果表明预测误差反馈在观测对当前轨迹形成约束时提升了潜状态估计。

Conclusion: PCD 提供了一个残差反馈框架，在连续时间序列建模中同时支持轨迹生成、观测一致性检查与状态细化。

Abstract: Dynamic systems evolve continuously over time, whereas observations in real scenarios are often sparse, irregular, and asynchronous. Accurate prediction therefore requires inferring latent state evolution between observations, as future values depend on partially observed trajectories. We propose Predictive Coding Dynamics (PCD), a residual feedback mechanism for irregular continuous dynamic models. PCD maintains previous reconstructions, predicts observed entries from them, and incorporates masked signed residuals into state recognition through an energy penalty. The mechanism is implemented with ODE-RNN and Latent ODE, supporting event-based hidden updates and latent state recognition. Experiments on forecasting and trajectory recovery tasks show that PCD improves continuous dynamic backbones under sparse and irregular sampling. The results further indicate that predictive error feedback improves latent state estimation when observations constrain the current trajectory. These results suggest that PCD provides a residual feedback framework that jointly supports trajectory generation, observation consistency checking, and state refinement in continuous-time series modeling. The source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/PCD-for-paper-92F1/.

</details>


### [64] [Infinite ResNets are Gaussian Mean ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41514)
*Louis-Pierre Chaintron,Lénaïc Chizat,Javier Maass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对ResNet在标准形状缩放与1/depth分支缩放下的大规模极限训练动力学,提出以"神经均值ODE上的梯度下降"为核心的简洁刻画,避免DMFT的非马尔可夫复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作用动力学平均场理论(DMFT)描述深度网络大规模极限的训练动力学,得到非马尔可夫系统,难以分析且掩盖了原动力学的优化结构,因此需要更简单、更透明的极限描述。

Method: 对随机初始化、用梯度下降(GD)训练的ResNet,在宽度、深度及嵌入/输入/输出维度联合取大的极限下,证明极限动力学等价于在高斯初始权重下对Neural Mean ODE做GD;覆盖总体风险与经验风险,并推导依赖ResNet形状与数据协方差谱的近似缩放律;方法推广至MoE模型,得到嵌套均值ODE描述。

Result: 极限动力学被简化为Neural Mean ODE上的GD,并获得显式的近似缩放律,其依赖于ResNet形状及数据协方差谱;1/depth分支缩放可实现最大(局部)特征更新;MoE亦具有直观的嵌套Mean ODE形式。

Conclusion: 该框架给出大规模极限训练动力学的极简且可分析的优化视角,统一处理ResNet与MoE,并为理解缩放律与特征学习强度提供工具。

Abstract: Understanding the training dynamics of deep neural networks in large-scale limits is a question both of foundational and of practical importance. Prior works have obtained such descriptions through dynamical mean field theory (DMFT), yielding a system of non-Markovian dynamics that are challenging to analyze and that obscure the optimization structure of the original dynamics. In this paper, we obtain instead a simple description of the large-scale limit training dynamics for ResNets in standard shape scalings and in the $1/\text{depth}$ branch scaling that allows maximal (local) feature updates. For a model trained with gradient descent (GD) from a random initialization, the limit dynamics is simply GD on a Neural Mean ODE with Gaussian initial weights. Our analysis covers population or empirical risk GD in the joint limit of large width, depth and embedding, input, and output dimensions. We obtain approximation scaling laws that depend on the shape of the ResNet and the covariance spectrum of the data. We also illustrate the flexibility of our approach by discussing Mixture of Expert models, where likewise the limit admits an intuitive (nested) Mean ODE description.

</details>


### [65] [Enhancing Sentiment Classification on Small Datasets through Data Augmentation and Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41518)
*Mahmoud S. Mayaleh,Samer A. Mayaleh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在小规模情感分类任务上系统比较了三种数据增强策略（EDA、回译、上下文词替换）在传统机器学习模型（逻辑回归、随机森林）与BERT上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模情感分类面临数据稀缺问题，限制模型泛化能力；现有研究缺乏针对模型类型与数据集规模的数据增强选择的系统性对比指导。

Method: 采用低资源情感数据集，通过总结近期研究结果并结合针对性的头对头（head-to-head）实验，对EDA、回译与nlpaug式上下文词替换三种增强方法，在逻辑回归、随机森林和BERT上进行实证比较。

Result: 所有增强方法均带来性能提升；上下文增强对BERT模型增益最稳定，而EDA与回译对传统分类器收益更大。

Conclusion: 研究为按模型类型与数据规模选择合适的数据增强技术提供了指导，填补了小数据集情感分类数据增强研究的空白。

Abstract: Small-scale sentiment classification often suffers from data scarcity, which limits the generalization ability of the models. This study evaluates and compares the effectiveness of three data augmentation strategies: Easy Data Augmentation (EDA), back-translation, and contextual token substitution (nlpaug-style), with both traditional machine learning classifiers (Logistic Regression, Random Forest) and transformer-based models (BERT). We perform a comprehensive empirical comparison with low-resource sentiment datasets by summarizing the results of recent studies and performing targeted head-to-head experiments. Our findings indicate that all augmentation methods improve performance. Contextual augmentation yields the most consistent gains for BERT models, while EDA and back-translation provide greater benefits for traditional classifiers. These insights help guide the selection of data augmentation techniques tailored to model type and dataset size, filling a critical gap in research on data augmentation for sentiment classification on small datasets.

</details>


### [66] [FOCUS: First-Order Control Using Optimal Transport Supervision for Imitation from Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41540)
*João A. Cândido Ramos,Lionel Blondé,Hugues Vinzant,Jacopo Castellini,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOCUS：用局部最优传输对齐 + 短时域可微actor-critic，实现无需时间对齐的从观测模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从状态演示（state-only）模仿需在未知时间对应关系下恢复行为；可微模拟器虽提供路径wise策略梯度，但穿越长且富接触轨迹求导会导致优化不稳定。

Method: 将智能体 rollout 窗口与多个采样专家片段通过 soft best-of-K 目标做局部最优传输匹配，强调局部兼容匹配；由传输成本生成可微模仿奖励，并用学习到的 critic 在 rollout 时域之外做价值自举；无需时间对齐轨迹、专家对齐初始化或 teacher forcing。

Result: 在 DFlex 的富接触运动任务（含152个肌肉激活控制的肌肉骨骼人形）上，相比对抗式从观测模仿方法与先前可微模仿基线，样本效率与训练稳定性均提升。

Conclusion: 局部传输对齐与短时域可微学习的结合可有效缓解长轨迹求导的不稳定性，并为 state-only 模仿提供可扩展范式。

Abstract: Imitation learning from observations must recover behavior from state-only demonstrations without known temporal correspondence. Differentiable simulators provide pathwise policy gradients, but differentiating through long, contact-rich trajectories can make optimization unstable. We introduce FOCUS, which combines local optimal-transport alignment with short-horizon differentiable actor-critic learning. FOCUS matches agent rollout windows to multiple sampled expert segments using a soft best-of-$K$ objective that emphasizes locally compatible matches. The resulting transport-based costs provide differentiable imitation rewards, while a learned critic bootstraps value beyond the rollout horizon. FOCUS requires neither temporally aligned trajectories nor expert-aligned initialization or teacher forcing. Across contact-rich locomotion tasks in DFlex, including a musculoskeletal humanoid controlled by 152 muscle activations, FOCUS improves sample efficiency and training stability over adversarial imitation-from-observation methods and prior differentiable imitation baselines.

</details>


### [67] [ORION: An Outcome-Driven LLM Advisor for RL-Based Multi-Domain 6G Slice Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41563)
*Mahmoud S. Mayaleh,Fetia Bannour,Nour-El-Houda Yellas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层编排器ORION，用两个LLM智能体将业务意图转为结构化请求与抽象放置计划，MADRL协调跨域划分，并以结果驱动的情景记忆闭环提升方案层质量，准入率提升4.6–11.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络切片跨管理域部署时需在线联合决策VNF归属域、跨域流量路径与是否接纳，该问题在连续到达下为NP-hard；实验表明不存在跨所有拓扑族都占优的固定规划策略（排序随拓扑类型反转），且多数被拒请求存在可行放置但未被发现，说明"计划层"是决定性性能杠杆。

Method: ORION为分层编排架构：两个LLM智能体负责意图解析（转结构化请求）与生成抽象放置计划；协作式多智能体强化学习协调器选择跨域划分，并将计划作为持续软先验，各域智能体完成功能放置。核心贡献为计划层的结果驱动情景记忆：记录每次尝试的网络条件画像、所提计划及经验证的可行性结果，将已验证成功与带标签失败反馈给规划器，以部署可行性而非标量奖励闭合自适应回路；并采用重要性保留策略替代FIFO。

Result: 在固定下游选择与执行机制的受控设置下，于留出拓扑族上评估：带记忆的规划器较无记忆版本准入率提升4.6–11.5个百分点；其中基于重要性的保留策略贡献了相对FIFO存储的大部分增益。

Conclusion: 计划层是跨域切片编排的关键性能来源；以部署可行性为反馈信号的情景记忆可有效改善LLM规划器的抽象放置质量，且增益主要来自记忆的筛选保留机制而非简单累积。

Abstract: Deploying 6G network slices across administrative domains requires an online decision for each arriving request: which domain hosts each virtual network function, how traffic crosses domain boundaries, and whether the slice is admitted. This joint decision problem is NP-hard under continuous arrivals. Experiments on a parametrized family of multi-domain topologies show that no fixed planning strategy dominates across all regimes: the performance ranking reverses between topology classes, and feasible placements exist for most rejected requests but remain undiscovered. These findings identify the plan layer as the decisive performance lever. We introduce ORION, a hierarchical orchestrator in which two LLM agents translate service intents into structured requests and generate abstract placement plans. A cooperative multi-agent RL coordinator selects the cross-domain partition, treating the plan as a persistent soft prior, while per-domain agents place the functions. ORION main contribution lies at the plan layer: an outcome-driven episodic memory that records, for each completed attempt, the network-condition profile, the proposed plan, and its verified feasibility outcome. The memory feeds verified successes and labelled failures back to the planner, thereby closing the adaptation loop using deployment feasibility rather than a scalar reward assigned to the planner's own proposals. To isolate the effect of the plan quality, we evaluate the planner on held-out topology families while keeping the downstream selection and execution mechanisms fixed. Under this controlled setting, the proposed memory improves admission by $4.6$--$11.5$ percentage points over the same planner without memory, while importance-based retention accounts for most of the improvement over a first-in-first-out store.

</details>


### [68] [A Constructive Orbit-Based Theory of Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41641)
*Yunied Puig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出有限维轨道框架构建数据自适应字典、稀疏实现与预测扩展，核心构造CCDS可在最小偏差边界恢复MP/OMP行为，并允许MP/OMP不可达的可行内点路径，给出多项理论保证并在合成与分类基准上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将字典学习问题转化为可由轨道动力学刻画的三项显式任务（支架发现、稀疏实现、稀疏坐标预测），从而把稀疏近似、插值与有限维算子动力学统一起来，并获得可证明的稳定性与误差界。

Method: 利用wrapped weighted shifts生成轨道字典，并从独立轨道代表元构造局部插值映射；引入CCDS（偏差受控稀疏实现流程），在最小偏差边界处复现MP/OMP型行为，同时允许具有指定残差几何的可行内点路径。

Result: 证明一般轨道独立性、带范数控制的精确块插值、稀疏近似失真的可观测界、选定支撑上的稀疏实现保证，以及块插值在容许扰动下固定路径的稳定性；实验中选定轨道支架的匹配秩投影行为显著优于随机轨道与非结构化方案，CCDS在最小偏差边界实现近零实现误差，基于稀疏CCDS证书训练的预测器性能具竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过构造与分析数据诱导的轨道字典，将稀疏近似、插值与有限维算子动力学相连接，为数据自适应字典与稀疏预测提供具理论保证的可行框架。

Abstract: We develop a finite-dimensional orbit framework for constructing data-adapted dictionaries, sparse realizations, and predictive extensions. The method uses wrapped weighted shifts to generate orbit dictionaries and local interpolation maps from independent orbit representatives, turning the learning problem into three explicit tasks: scaffold discovery, sparse realization on the discovered dictionary, and prediction through learned sparse coordinates. We introduce CCDS as the main constructive ingredient: a deviation-controlled sparse realization procedure that recovers MP/OMP-type behavior at the minimal-deviation boundary while allowing feasible interior paths with prescribed residual geometry that is inaccessible to MP/OMP. We prove generic orbit independence, exact block interpolation with norm control, observable bounds on sparse approximation distortion, sparse realization guarantees on selected supports, and fixed-path stability under admissible perturbations of the block interpolants. These results connect sparse approximation, interpolation, and finite-dimensional operator dynamics through the construction and analysis of data-induced orbit dictionaries. Experiments on synthetic geometry, controlled realization tests, and standard classification benchmarks show that selected orbit scaffolds have substantially better matched-rank projection behavior than random orbit or unstructured alternatives, that CCDS attains near-zero realization error at the minimal-deviation boundary, and that predictors trained from the sparse CCDS certificates give competitive performance.

</details>


### [69] [On the Generation Order in GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41648)
*Timofei Gritsaev,Evgeny Katasonov,Ildus Sadrtdinov,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究GFlowNet中离散生成顺序对收敛的影响，提出Learning-Order GFlowNet在无顺序标注下学习可解释的生成顺序，并用梯度信噪比（SNR）解释顺序涌现机制。
