<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从由未归一化概率函数定义的离散分布采样是经典统计问题。GFlowNet按顺序逐次添加离散分量构造对象，顺序决定网络需学习的条件分布，部分顺序更易学习。但现有GFlowNet算法是否能发现可解释的生成顺序并在训练中加以利用尚属未知。

Method: 设计可控问题（controlled problems）以实验证明生成顺序影响收敛，且标准GFlowNet训练难以发现有用顺序；进而提出Learning-Order GFlowNet训练方案，在无顺序标注条件下学习可解释的生成顺序；最后以梯度信噪比（SNR）作为顺序涌现的可能解释进行分析。

Result: 实验表明生成顺序显著影响收敛速度；标准GFlowNet训练难以自行发现有用顺序；Learning-Order GFlowNet能学习到可解释顺序并改善收敛。收敛更快的顺序在可比验证损失下具有更高SNR。

Conclusion: 生成顺序是GFlowNet训练的关键但被忽视的因素；Learning-Order GFlowNet可无监督学习有效顺序。SNR既提供了有利于优良顺序涌现的机制解释，也给出了良好生成顺序的SNR刻画。

Abstract: Sampling from discrete distributions specified by unnormalised probability functions is a classical statistical problem. GFlowNets address this problem by constructing objects sequentially, adding one discrete component at a time in either a predefined or a learned order. The choice of order determines which conditional distributions the neural network must learn, some of which may be easier to learn than others. Yet whether existing GFlowNet algorithms discover interpretable generation orders and make use of them during training remains unexplored. We introduce controlled problems where generation order is experimentally shown to affect convergence and standard GFlowNet training struggles to discover useful orders. To address this difficulty, we develop Learning-Order GFlowNet, a training recipe that learns interpretable generation orders without order annotations and improves convergence on these problems. Finally, we investigate gradient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) as a possible explanation for order emergence: orders associated with faster convergence exhibit higher SNR at comparable validation loss, suggesting both a mechanism favouring their emergence and an SNR-based characterisation of a good generation order.

</details>


### [70] [Does Reasoning Make Language Models Agree?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41691)
*Mila Gorecki,Moritz Hardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 区分“推理作为干预”与“推理作为观测行为”：强制提高推理投入会增加模型间一致性，而观测到推理链更长的人群反而预测分歧更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型存在单文化（相关性错误）与多元性（准确率相当却决策冲突）两种对立担忧；推理模型的兴起使这一争论复杂化——推理可能因共享显著特征或相似推理路径而提高一致性，也可能因随机推理轨迹引入分支而增加多样性。论文旨在化解这一张力。

Method: 区分推理的两种角色：作为干预（controlled changes in reasoning effort）与作为观测行为（observed reasoning lengths）。在来自两家开发者的九个模型、三个分类任务上，将一致性测量结果与由模型准确率所预期的基线一致性进行对照，以排除准确率差异的混淆。

Result: 对同一批个体评估时，启用推理会提高模型间一致性；而诱发更长推理链的个体在不同模型间获得更不一致的预测。两种模式在控制准确率差异后依然成立。

Conclusion: 要求模型更多推理会促进一致性，而观测到模型推理更长则预示分歧；单文化与多元性之争需区分干预效应与选择/观测效应。

Abstract: Language models face two opposing concerns: monoculture, in which models err in correlated ways, and multiplicity, in which equally accurate models make conflicting decisions. The rise of reasoning models has complicated this debate: On the one hand, reasoning may increase model agreement if models anchor on the same salient features or follow similar reasoning paths. On the other hand, stochastic reasoning traces may introduce additional branch points leading to more diverse predictions. We resolve this tension by distinguishing reasoning as an intervention from reasoning as an observed behavior. We compare agreement under controlled changes in reasoning effort with agreement across observed reasoning lengths, measuring both against the agreement expected from model accuracy. Across nine models from two developers and three classification tasks, enabling reasoning increases agreement between models evaluated on the same individuals. Yet the individuals who elicit longer reasoning traces receive less consistent predictions across models. Both patterns persist after accounting for differences in accuracy. Asking models to reason more thus promotes agreement, while observing them reason longer signals disagreement.

</details>


### [71] [From WSIs to Bulk, Spatial and Single-Cell Transcriptomics via Multi-Scale Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41692)
*Miguel Andrade,José Almeida,Carlos Santiago,Catarina Barata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多尺度图神经网络（GNN），仅用全切片病理图像（WSI）即可同时预测bulk、空间与单细胞转录组，并验证其在下游临床任务中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组（ST）与单细胞转录组（scT）分辨率高但成本高、样本稀缺；而常规H&E全切片图像与bulk RNA测序数据广泛可得。故有必要从廉价易得的WSI中推断多尺度基因表达信息。

Method: 采用病理基础模型UNI2-h提取形态学特征；在patch级与cell级分别构建空间图，形成多尺度GNN以学习组织层次结构；在TCGA、Visium、Xenium三套数据（肺、肾、乳腺组织）上联合训练，并采用混合训练策略：直接监督（各数据源对应尺度）加弱监督一致性损失，保证跨生物学尺度的预测一致。

Result: 模型在切片（bulk）水平可准确预测基因表达；patch水平预测中等，其中在Xenium数据上表现最好；在最具挑战性的单细胞水平仅达中等相关性。下游任务（生存分析、空间风险图、基于分子签名的肿瘤分类）表明预测具有临床相关性。

Conclusion: 单一WSI输入的多尺度GNN可实现跨bulk、空间与单细胞三个尺度的基因表达预测，且预测结果可迁移至生存分析与肿瘤分类等临床任务；细胞级精度仍有限，是未来改进方向。

Abstract: Understanding the spatial distribution of gene expression is key to studying complex diseases like cancer. While Spatial Transcriptomics (ST) and Single-Cell Transcriptomics (scT) provide high-resolution insights, they remain expensive and scarce. In contrast, standard histopathology Whole-Slide Images (WSIs) paired with bulk RNA sequencing are widely available. This work introduces a Multi-Scale Graph Neural Network (GNN) capable of simultaneously predicting bulk, spatial, and single-cell transcriptomics directly from WSIs. By extracting morphological features using a Pathology Foundation Model (PFM) (UNI2-h) and constructing spatial graphs at both patch and cell levels, the model learns the hierarchical relationships within the tissue. The network is trained on a combined dataset of The Cancer Genome Atlas Program (TCGA), Visium, and Xenium samples across lung, kidney, and breast tissues. A hybrid training strategy is employed, leveraging direct supervision alongside weakly supervised consistency losses to ensure predictions align across biological scales. Results show the model accurately predicts gene expression at the slide level and moderately at the patch level (strongest on Xenium), and achieves moderate correlation at the challenging single-cell level. Furthermore, downstream tasks explore the clinical relevance of these predictions, demonstrating their utility in survival analysis, spatial hazard mapping, and tumor classification via molecular signatures.

</details>


### [72] [From fleet trajectories to single-cell promises: hierarchically weighted conformal prediction for battery SOH monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41696)
*Xiangyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示保形预测的"90%覆盖率"在嵌套电池数据上仅承诺车队平均而非单个电芯，提出三层（车队/批次/电芯）预注册报告协议量化该差距，并用分层加权保形引擎部分修复，最终交付一套可审计的单电芯区间报告协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测区间通常被解读为统一的名义覆盖率（如90%），但在嵌套结构的电池数据中，这种池化覆盖率掩盖了电芯个体层面的严重失效——最差电芯的实际覆盖可能极低。现有报告实践缺乏对"承诺对象是车队还是单电芯"的区分，导致单电芯区间声明在部署前无法审计。

Method: 在124个公开LFP电芯上采用预注册的三层报告协议（车队、批次、电芯），用精确二项检验对照名义覆盖水平量化池化与最差电芯间的差距；随后构建分层加权保形引擎，其权重仅使用预测时冻结的侧信息（签名特征、批次标签、循环索引），不触碰残差；并检验SOH协议漂移下的敏感性、校准池电池多样性边界及跨化学体系外推。

Result: 池化覆盖率看似健康（各划分0.875–0.906），但最差电芯仅被覆盖0.1%–2%。分层加权引擎满足预注册修复准则：第10百分位电芯覆盖率由0.750升至0.830（弥补53%差距），最差电芯由0.642升至0.694，零延迟且敏感性谱平坦。未修复部分被定位而非搁置：协议漂移下冻结权重在SOH尺度上单调恶化（结构性死路，不作调参掩盖）；修复能力受校准池电池多样性上界约束；跨化学体系外部集区间崩塌至0.286，而域内骨架可复现（池化0.933，最差电芯0.686）。

Conclusion: 池化覆盖率不能作为单电芯可靠性证据。论文交付的报告协议——锚定实测工况的三层承诺、最差电芯统计量、延迟占比——使单电芯区间声明在部署前可审计，并明确指出分层加权修复的适用边界（协议漂移、校准池多样性、跨化学体系外推）。

Abstract: Interval predictions from conformal prediction are usually read as "90% coverage", but on nested battery data that number promises the fleet average, not the individual cell. We measure the gap on 124 public LFP cells under a preregistered three-tier reporting protocol (fleet, batch, cell): pooled coverage looks healthy (0.875-0.906 across splits) while the worst cell is covered 0.1-2% of the time, with exact binomial tests against the nominal level. We then repair part of the gap with a hierarchically weighted conformal engine whose weights consume only side information frozen at prediction time - signature features, batch labels, cycle indices - and never touch residuals. The preregistered repair criterion passes: 10th-percentile cell coverage moves 0.750 to 0.830 (53% of the gap), the worst cell 0.642 to 0.694, with zero deferrals and a flat sensitivity spectrum. What the engine does not repair is localized rather than left open: under protocol drift the frozen weights harm monotonically on the SOH scale (the absolute scale is reported in full), a structural dead end we report instead of tuning away; and repair capacity is bounded by calibration-pool battery diversity, measured on both datasets. On a cross-chemistry external set the calibrated intervals collapse (0.286), while the within-domain skeleton replicates (pooled 0.933, worst cell 0.686). The deliverable is a reporting protocol - three tiers of promise anchored to measured regimes, worst-cell statistics, deferral share - that makes per-cell interval claims auditable before deployment.

</details>


### [73] [Hierarchical Residuals: A Minimalist Decoupling of Residual Width from Compute Width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41711)
*Ziteng Wang,Jianqiao Lu,Xinyu Ma,Jintao Zhang,Jun Zhu,Yiyuan Ma,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Hierarchical Residuals（HiRes），将残差宽度与每层计算宽度解耦：块内保持标准d维PreNorm层，块间用更宽的D维PostNorm外层残差连接，配合可学习的读写投影桥接两个空间。仅用标准算子即可提升FLOPs效率1.30倍，并在OLMoE-1B-7B上取得更低loss与更高下游精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准残差连接隐式地将随深度传递的残差宽度与层内计算宽度绑定为同一维度，限制了残差状态作为独立缩放变量的可能。作者希望在不引入专用kernel或基础设施改造的前提下，解耦这两个维度。

Method: HiRes将层分为若干块：块内为d维PreNorm标准Transformer层不变；块间引入更宽D维的PostNorm外层残差连接；在块边界用可学习的read/write投影在两个表示空间间转换。全部由稠密投影与归一化构成，无特殊kernel。

Result: 在不同模型规模与深度下稳定优于vanilla残差相加；拟合的scaling law显示FLOPs效率提升1.30倍；OLMoE-1B-7B在500B token预训练中训练loss降低0.0208，下游准确率提升1.14点；相比VWN、mHC、AttnRes等残差扩展方法具竞争力，且激活内存与I/O开销更低。

Conclusion: HiRes以极简架构改动将残差宽度暴露为实用的缩放变量，仅依赖标准算子即可获得效率与效果收益，具备良好的工程可部署性。

Abstract: Residual connections underpin Transformers, but they implicitly tie the residual width carried through depth to the compute width used inside each layer. We introduce Hierarchical Residuals (HiRes), a minimalist architectural change that decouples these dimensions, widening the residual state while keeping per-layer compute width fixed. HiRes groups layers into blocks: standard d-dimensional PreNorm layers operate inside each block, while a wider D-dimensional PostNorm outer residual connects blocks. Learned read/write projections bridge the two spaces at block boundaries, leaving individual Transformer layers unchanged. Across different model sizes and depths, HiRes consistently outperforms vanilla residual addition, with fitted scaling laws indicating a 1.30-fold improvement in FLOPs efficiency. On OLMoE-1B-7B pretraining for 500B tokens, HiRes achieves 0.0208 lower training loss and 1.14-point higher downstream accuracy. HiRes is also competitive with other residual expansion methods such as VWN, mHC and AttnRes with less activation memory and I/O costs. Built from only dense projections and normalizations, it requires neither specialized kernels nor infrastructure-level optimizations. HiRes thus exposes residual width as a practical scaling variable using only standard operators.

</details>


### [74] [UpLoop: Training Looped Transformers via Upcycling at No Extra Pretraining Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41720)
*Cheng Liang,Tengfei Zhang,Ya Zhang,Yanfeng Wang,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 UpLoop 预训练调度：用 upcycling 训练循环 Transformer，在最终参数量、训练 token 数与训练 FLOPs 均与标准稠密基线严格一致的前提下获得推理能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型通过共享权重的重复计算增强推理、在不增加独立参数的情况下提升执行深度，但每增加一次前向就增加训练 FLOPs，使性能增益代价高昂。作者希望在等计算预算下获得循环结构的收益。

Method: UpLoop 从浅层 Transformer 开始，通过复制已学层逐步加深深度（upcycling），并把前期节省下来的计算用于训练后段对共享中间 block 的重复执行。

Result: 跨模型规模实验中，语言建模与下游性能提升；1.3B 参数下 22 个下游任务平均分由 22.99 提升至 27.52；经监督微调后数学推理能力亦有增益。

Conclusion: 循环结构的好处可在不增加最终参数、token 与训练 FLOPs 的等预算约束下实现，UpLoop 是一种可扩展的等成本训练调度。

Abstract: Looped language models can strengthen reasoning through repeated computation with shared weights, increasing execution depth without adding independent parameters. However, each additional pass increases training floating-point operations (FLOPs), making these gains more expensive to obtain. To address this cost, we propose UpLoop, a pretraining schedule that uses upcycling to train looped Transformers while exactly matching a standard dense baseline in final parameter count, total training tokens, and training FLOPs. Specifically, UpLoop begins with a shallow Transformer, progressively grows its depth by copying learned layers, and uses the computation saved early to support repeated execution of a shared middle block later in training. Experiments across model scales show improvements in language modeling and downstream performance, with the average score across 22 downstream tasks increasing from 22.99 to 27.52 at 1.3B parameters. These gains also extend to mathematical reasoning after supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [75] [Leakage-Controlled Structure-Preserving Augmentation and Template-Aware Evaluation for Bangla Symptom-Based Disease Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41733)
*Sabah Nushra,Shovan Samanta Turzo,Moinul Hossain,Md Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验仅用训练集的非对称伯努利扰动（扩充）能否改善孟加拉语症状-疾病分类，并发现其增益高度依赖模型族与基准邻近度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有孟加拉语症状疾病预测常在小规模、类别不平衡的结构化基准上评测，且大量样本仅相差一两个症状，导致基准准确率可能高估真实泛化能力，需区分数据扩充与超参调优的作用并审计模板邻近性。

Method: 使用725个唯一档案、85种疾病、172个二值症状；采用10次重复真实档案留出与按父组分组的内层交叉验证；2×2设计分离扩充与调参；对照方法含精确复制、随机过采样、类别加权与SMOTEN；模型含BNB、XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost；并进行Hamming距离审计、同病1位净化挑战、缺失/伪症状鲁棒性测试及BNB解释忠实度评估。

Result: BNB平均准确率最高（97.50%），但相对调优原始训练的增益未通过Holm校正；提升树模型显著受益，XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost分别提升18.10、28.45、10.09个百分点；91.0%的标准留出档案与训练数据Hamming距离在1个症状位以内；同病1位净化挑战下准确率下降，但XGBoost与LightGBM仍保留大幅扩充增益。

Conclusion: 伯努利扰动对部分模型族有效（尤以提升树为著），但对BNB收益不稳健；基准准确率须结合模板邻近度共同解读，以避免因样本近似重复而高估性能。

Abstract: Bangla symptom-based disease prediction is commonly evaluated on a small, imbalanced structured benchmark containing many profiles that differ by only one or two symptoms. We test whether train-only asymmetric Bernoulli perturbation improves classification using 725 unique profiles spanning 85 diseases and 172 binary symptoms. Experiments use ten repeated real-profile holdouts and parent-grouped inner cross-validation. The 2 x 2 design separates augmentation from hyperparameter tuning, with exact duplication, random oversampling, class weighting, and SMOTEN as balancing controls. For Bernoulli Naive Bayes (BNB), the gain over tuned raw training does not withstand Holm correction, although BNB attains the highest mean accuracy (97.50%). The boosted-tree models benefit substantially: XGBoost, LightGBM, and CatBoost improve by 18.10, 28.45, and 10.09 percentage points, respectively. A Hamming-distance audit places 91.0% of standard held-out profiles within one symptom bit of the training data. Accuracy decreases on a same-disease 1-bit-purged challenge; nevertheless, XGBoost and LightGBM retain large augmentation gains. We also evaluate robustness to missing and spurious symptoms and the faithfulness of BNB explanations. Overall, Bernoulli perturbation is useful for some model families, but benchmark accuracy should be interpreted together with template proximity.

</details>


### [76] [Locality-Controlled Out-of-Distribution Guidance for Robust Regulatory DNA Sequence Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41736)
*Dabin Jeong,Marwane Bourdim,Mo Lotfollahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LOCOGEN，将调控DNA序列的条件生成中的引导重构为"局部性"问题：用序列级不变性学习得到motif掩码以保护motif位置，仅对背景位置施加OOD阻尼，在冻结的扩散骨干上采样时生效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计靶向基因表达控制的调控DNA是重要挑战，现有方法多将任务视为性质条件生成，用奖励微调或采样时引导。作者指出单一奖励评估会掩盖reward hacking；将评估分解为property、novelty、plausibility后，发现一个三者均满足但所有基线均失败的经验性未被探索区域。原因在于先前方法把引导主要当作"幅度"问题，而调控DNA的序列语法在空间上非均匀：motif位置对扰动敏感，背景区域则可更多探索。

Method: LOCOGEN将引导重新表述为局部性问题：通过序列级不变性学习学得一个不变motif掩码以保护motif位置，仅对背景位置施加OOD阻尼；在采样阶段作用于冻结的扩散骨干，无需重新训练骨干网络。

Result: 在HepG2、K562和SK-N-SH三个细胞系上，LOCOGEN达到property–novelty–plausibility空间中此前未被探索的区域（即三者同时满足的区域），而现有基线无法达到。

Conclusion: 单一奖励评估会掩盖reward hacking，应采用property、novelty、plausibility的分解评估；调控DNA的引导应视为空间局部性问题而非幅度问题，LOCOGEN以不变motif掩码+背景OOD阻尼、在冻结骨干上采样即可有效提升多目标权衡表现。

Abstract: Designing regulatory DNA sequences for targeted gene-expression control is an important challenge. Recent approaches frame this task as property-conditioned generation, where diffusion models trained on natural sequences are guided toward high predicted activity through reward-based fine-tuning or sampling-time guidance. We show that single-reward evaluation can obscure reward-hacking behavior. By decomposing evaluation into property, novelty, and plausibility, we identify an empirically underexplored region of the evaluation space in which all three objectives are satisfied, but no existing baseline succeeds. We postulate that this gap arises because prior methods treat guidance primarily as a matter of magnitude. In regulatory DNA, however, sequence grammar is spatially nonuniform: motif positions are sensitive to perturbation, while background regions allow more exploration. We therefore propose LOCOGEN by reformulating guidance as a problem of locality. LOCOGEN learns an invariant motif mask through sequence-level invariance learning to protect motif positions, while applying OOD damping only to background positions. It operates at sampling time on a frozen diffusion backbone, without retraining the backbone. Across HepG2, K562, and SK-N-SH, LOCOGEN reaches this previously underexplored region of the property-novelty-plausibility space. Code is available at https://github.com/Lotfollahi-lab/LocoGen.

</details>


### [77] [Task Vectors Can Be Linearly Decomposed into Interpretable and Controllable Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41743)
*Yukiya Horiba,Hakaze Cho,Takahiro Yakoh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出将任务向量（TV）分解为任务识别（TR）、任务学习（TL）与残差三个功能分量，并用贝叶斯优化调整TR/TL系数以实现更有效的干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务向量通常被视为从隐状态中提取的"原子化"方向，其内部功能结构缺乏理解；受将上下文学习分解为任务识别与任务学习的启发，作者追问单个TV能否同样被分解为功能上有意义的分量。

Method: 基于候选标签的unembedding构造TR/TL分解，将每个TV拆为TR、TL与残差分量；采用贝叶斯优化求解TR与TL系数以获得更有效的干预方向；在多种模型家族、分类任务以及指令型与示例型TV上进行评测。

Result: TR/TL优化得到的干预方向优于原始TV；随机方向优化虽也能提升性能，但TR/TL搜索的增益更稳定，尤其在指令型TV上；对分解分量单独干预会产生与其功能角色一致且可区分的logit层面效应。

Conclusion: 任务向量并非单一的整体引导方向，而是可分解为兼具可解释性与可控性的功能分量，支持对其内部结构进行解释与操控。

Abstract: Task Vectors (TVs) provide a compact way to steer large language models, but they are typically treated as atomized directions merely extracted from hidden states, leaving their internal functional structure poorly understood. Motivated by prior work that decomposes in-context learning into Task Recognition (TR) and Task Learning (TL), we ask whether a single TV can likewise be decomposed into functionally meaningful components. We formulate a TR/TL-based decomposition from candidate-label unembeddings, separating each TV into TR, TL, and residual components, and try to optimize the TR and TL coefficients using Bayesian optimization for more effective intervention. We evaluate this formulation across multiple model families, classification tasks, and both instruction- and demonstration-based TVs. Across these settings, TR/TL-based optimization identifies intervention directions that outperform the original TVs. Although optimization over random directions can also improve performance, TR/TL-based search yields more reliable gains, particularly for instruction-based TVs. Moreover, isolated interventions on the decomposed components produce distinct logit-level effects that are consistent with their intended functional roles. Together, these results suggest that TVs are not monolithic steering directions, but can be decomposed into functionally meaningful components that support both interpretation and control.

</details>


### [78] [Do LLMs Know When They Are Wrong About Molecules? In-Context Property Prediction with Self-Reported Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41745)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Tamadur Albaraghtheh,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler,Christian J. Cyron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 评估7个前沿LLM在4个分子性质数据集上的预测能力与自报不确定性，并做数据污染筛查与经典基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在诸多领域广泛应用，但其分子性质预测能力仍缺乏系统探索；同时需排除训练数据记忆（污染）对评估结果的干扰。

Method: 选取7个前沿LLM，基于in-context示例进行分子性质预测并自报不确定性；使用4个数据集，逐个筛查LLM训练数据污染（3个干净、ESOL被多数模型记忆作为对照）；与在同批分子上训练的经典基线比较；考察上下文示例数量（如30例）与推理努力程度的影响，并用Spearman秩相关评估不确定性对误差的排序能力。

Result: 在干净数据集上，所有LLM随上下文示例增加而提升，30例时最优模型超过所有基线；推理努力程度对准确率影响很小；自报不确定性对预测误差的排序能力较弱（Spearman最高约0.46），与基线不确定性水平相当；结合不确定性与预测，在lipophilicity小上下文下最优LLM最确定的一半最准确，在BACE、antiviral及大上下文lipophilicity上最优LLM与最佳基线持平；在受污染的ESOL上，LLM准确率不随上下文规模提升，且对可召回分子的自报不确定性更低。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM在干净数据上借助上下文示例可达到甚至超越经典基线，但自报不确定性校准较弱；ESOL上的行为模式（无上下文增益、记忆分子不确定性降低）提示需严格进行数据污染筛查，LLM的分子性质预测潜力与不确定性可靠性需分别审慎评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used in many domains, but molecular property prediction with them is still little explored. In this work we evaluate the capability of 7 frontier LLMs to predict molecular properties together with a self-reported uncertainty from in-context examples, and compare them with classical baselines trained on the same molecules. We use 4 datasets and screen each for contamination of the training data of the LLMs: three are clean, and the fourth, ESOL, is memorised by most of the models and serves as a comparison. On the clean datasets every LLM improves with the context, at 30 examples the most accurate one beats every baseline. The requested reasoning effort changes accuracy little. The self-reported uncertainty ranks the prediction errors weakly, with Spearman rank correlations of up to 0.46, about the level reached by the uncertainties of the baselines. Combining uncertainty and prediction, on lipophilicity the most certain half of the best LLM is the most accurate at small context sizes; on BACE and antiviral, and on lipophilicity at large context sizes, the best LLMs are on par with the best baseline. On the contaminated ESOL dataset the LLMs show no improvement with the context size, as expected for a memorised dataset, and their stated uncertainty is smaller on the molecules whose value they recall.

</details>


### [79] [Latent-Lens: Visual Perception in Small Language Models Through Latent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41751)
*Mo Shahdloo,Faegheh Sardari,Mathew Salvaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Latent-Lens用一个约160万参数（占组合模型0.2%）的轻量编解码器，在冻结的VLM与冻结的纯文本SLM之间实现纯隐状态通信，显著优于同预算下的离散文本通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化多智能体中，VLM与SLM通常以离散文本交换信息，形成有损瓶颈，尤其损失空间与细粒度细节；受限于容量，SLM对输入信号质量更为敏感。已有隐空间通信方法多用于语言模型之间，而VLM如何将视觉信息传递给单独预训练的纯文本SLM尚属空白。

Method: 提出Latent-Lens编解码器：域编码器将VLM隐状态投影到SLM嵌入空间；以LoRA形式化的注意力解码器教SLM关注这些视觉前缀。VLM与SLM均保持冻结，仅训练约1.6M参数。

Result: 视觉问答：CLEVR 81.0±7.9%、GQA 65.4±5.4%，对比同LoRA预算的文本通信58.3±5.7%与61.8±1.0%（相对提升＋30.2–69.8个百分点）；图像描述Flickr8k上较同基线提升+16.5（较冻结核+18.8）。并在TinyLlama-1.1B与InternVL2-1B（架构、词表、隐维度均不同）上完成验证。

Conclusion: 隐空间通信可有效替代离散文本作为跨模型信息通道，在小参数开销下显著提升下游任务表现，并具备跨架构、跨词表的泛化性。

Abstract: Bridging independent vision-language models (VLMs) and small language models (SLMs) in a modular multi-agent system typically forces them to exchange information as discrete text, which creates a lossy bottleneck for spatial and fine-grained details. This constraint is especially acute for SLMs, whose limited capacity makes them more reliant on the quality of the input signal. Recent latent-communication methods exchange states between language models. How a VLM can pass visual information to a separately pretrained text-only SLM though remains unexplored. We propose \textbf{Latent-Lens}, a lightweight codec that bridges the representation gap between a frozen VLM and a frozen SLM directly in latent space, enabling them to communicate purely through hidden states. The codec consists of a domain encoder that projects hidden states from the VLM into the SLM's embedding space, and an attention decoder formalised as a LoRA that teaches the SLM to attend to these visual prefixes, while training only ${\sim}1.6$M parameters (0.2\% of the combined model size). On visual question answering (CLEVR, GQA), Latent-Lens achieves $81.0\pm7.9$\% and $65.4\pm5.4$\%, compared with $58.3\pm5.7$\% and $61.8\pm1.0$\% for text communication with the same LoRA budget (+30.2--69.8pp relative to frozen text communication), while on image captioning (Flickr8k) it improves over the same baseline by +16.5 (+18.8 frozen). We also evaluate Latent-Lens with TinyLlama-1.1B and InternVL2-1B, which use different architectures, vocabularies, and hidden dimensions.

</details>


### [80] [Implicit Assumptions About Tasks in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41792)
*Kathrin Korte,Christian Metzner,Akshay Bedhotiya,Viet Anh Khoa Tran,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文区分了持续学习中"数据层面、划分层面、学习器层面"三类因素，明确每个报告指标究竟对应哪一层，并通过可控的教师-学生实验证明：划分粒度、呈现顺序与数据相似结构都会改变测量结果；对代表性基准的审查表明，许多被讨论的属性并未被显式声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习通常把数据切分为任务并顺序呈现，其性能被归因于任务间相似性、数据变化速率与呈现顺序等因素。但这些因素可在底层数据、对数据施加的划分、以及学习器三个层面分别定义，三者并不必然一致；文献通常未说明所报告的数值究竟指哪一层，导致结果解释与比较含混。作者希望把这三个层面分开，明确哪些属性在哪个层面才有取值。

Method: 作者从概念上分离数据层、划分层与学习器层，识别各层上有定义的属性。随后设计可控的教师-学生（teacher–student）实验：对同一条底层数据轨迹在不同粒度上做划分、改变呈现顺序、并改变底层数据的相似性结构，观察测量结果的变化。此外，系统审查若干代表性持续学习基准，核查这些属性是否被明确陈述。对于无法检视底层数据的基础模型，作者提出把数据属性归入模型初始状态，并描述其上层训练。

Result: 教师-学生实验显示：仅改变同一底层轨迹的划分粒度、呈现顺序或相似性结构，就会产生不同的测量结果，说明报告数值强烈依赖于层面的选择而非仅由任务本身决定。基准审查显示，代表性基准往往并未显式说明这些属性的所属层面。

Conclusion: 持续学习的测量与解释必须显式标注属性所属层面（数据/划分/学习器），否则跨研究比较缺乏可比性。在底层数据不可检视的基础模型场景下，数据属性应由模型初始状态继承，并可通过描述其训练过程来刻画。

Abstract: Continual learning is the study of how a model accumulates knowledge from data received over time, rather than all at once. In practice, this is typically achieved by dividing a dataset into tasks and presenting them sequentially. Explanations of the resulting measurements take into account factors such as the similarity between segments, the rate at which the data changes and the order of presentation. Each of these factors can be defined in terms of the underlying data, the partition imposed on it, or the learner that encounters it. These levels do not necessarily coincide, and it is usually left implicit which level a reported quantity refers to. We separate these and identify which properties acquire a value at each level. In controlled teacher--student experiments, we demonstrate that partitioning the same underlying trajectory at different levels of granularity, changing the order of presentation, and varying the similarity structure of the underlying data all result in different measured outcomes. We review representative benchmarks to determine which of these properties are stated. Where the underlying data cannot be inspected, as in foundation models built on undisclosed pre-training corpora, its properties are inherited by the model's initial state, and the training applied on top of it can be described.

</details>


### [81] [Accurate and Auditable ECG Classification via Visibility Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41795)
*Artur Xavier,Guilherme Henrique Gomes Evangelista,Aitazaz Khan,Antonio Luiz Pinho Ribeiro,Mohammed J. Zaki,Srinivasan Parthasarathy,Wagner Meira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LYNX-ECG，将每个心搏表示为基于时间戳转折点的稀疏可见图，在CODE-Test上macro F1达0.889，并可删除特定波形区域或改变心搏时序以审计预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医生依据波形形态与时间关系判读心电图，但现有深度学习分类器准确率虽高，却很少说明是哪些波形区域驱动了预测，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 构建图神经网络LYNX-ECG：把每个心搏建模为稀疏可见图，节点为带时间戳的转折点，使每个节点对应具体的心电时间点，从而支持区域删除与时序扰动实验。

Result: CODE-Test上macro F1为0.889，优于含ECGFounder基础模型在内的五个基线；图存储量约为逐样本建图的14%。传导障碍中，临床预期区域在90%以上正确预测中为最强证据；心动过缓/心动过速随心率变化（传导间期固定）；去除逐搏变异后房颤预测消失。

Conclusion: 稀疏可见图在保持准确分类的同时提供了临床可落地、可检验的解释性，实现性能与可解释性的兼顾。

Abstract: Physicians read electrocardiograms (ECGs) from waveform shape and timing, yet accurate deep-learning classifiers rarely show which waveform regions drive their predictions. We introduce LYNX-ECG, a graph neural network that represents each heartbeat as a sparse visibility graph of timestamped turning points. On CODE-Test, LYNX-ECG reaches a macro F1 score of 0.889, outperforming five baselines including the ECGFounder foundation model, while its graphs need about 14% of the storage of graphs built from every sample. Because each node maps to a specific ECG time, we can delete clinically defined waveform regions and alter beat timing to audit predictions. For conduction disorders, the clinically expected region was the strongest evidence in more than 90% of correct predictions. Bradycardia and tachycardia calls follow heart rate with conduction intervals held fixed, and atrial fibrillation calls vanish when beat-to-beat variation is removed. Sparse visibility graphs thus pair accurate ECG classification with clinically grounded, testable explanations.

</details>


### [82] [On-device Detection of Wearer-induced NLOS on Chest-worn UWB Tags: Machine Learning, Link Indicators, or Both?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41799)
*Markus Gallacher,Vu Anh Minh Le,Matteo Trobinger,Davide Vecchia,Carlo Alberto Boano,Gian Pietro Picco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对胸戴UWB标签的人体遮挡NLOS（WNLOS）问题，系统评测了21种端侧分类器（ML、无线链路指标LI及混合方法），发现精度与信道/PHY配置强相关，且能效差异极大（最高达39.6倍），并开源数据集与分类器推荐工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UWB虽可实现高精度测距，但在非视距（NLOS）下性能退化；当标签穿戴于人体时，人体不可避免地造成佩戴者诱发的NLOS（WNLOS），误差显著，但该场景研究不足。同时WNLOS检测需在标签端完成，受严格运行时间与能耗约束，传统ML分类器难以兼顾精度与效率；而轻量快速的UWB链路指标（LI）分类器鲜有探索。

Method: 聚焦胸戴标签，在涵盖多信道、PHY配置、环境、受试者与朝向的150万样本数据集上，分析并对比21种分类器：既有端侧ML分类器与LI分类器，以及利用二者互补性的新型混合分类器。

Result: 所有分类器均能达到较好的分类精度，但相对性能（含误分类导致的测距误差）高度依赖信道与PHY配置；运行时间与能效差异极大，部分方法仅带来边际精度提升却消耗高达39.6倍能量。

Conclusion: WNLOS下分类器选择需结合信道/PHY配置与资源约束综合权衡，单一追求精度并不经济。作者提出一个推荐工具，用于在用户自定义约束下识别最优分类器，并与数据集一同公开。

Abstract: Ultra-wideband (UWB) enables high-accuracy distance estimation (ranging), but performance degrades in non-line-of-sight (NLOS) conditions. When UWB tags are worn, the human body inevitably causes a wearer-induced NLOS (WNLOS), yielding significant errors; still, despite its relevance, WNLOS remains understudied. Moreover, WNLOS detection typically must occur on-device at the tag, requiring classification accuracy to be balanced against tight runtime and energy constraints often hard to meet with machine learning (ML) -- the dominating NLOS classification approach. Meanwhile, UWB radio link indicators (LI) offer a faster and lightweight alternative that has rarely been explored. We study existing on-device ML and LI classifiers when applied to WNLOS, and propose novel hybrid ones exploiting their complementarity. We focus on chest-worn tags and analyze 21 classifiers on a dataset of 1.5 million samples across channels, PHY settings, environments, subjects, and orientations. Results show that while all of them achieve good classification accuracy, relative performance, including ranging errors due to misclassification, depends heavily on channel and PHY configuration. Runtime and energy efficiency also vary dramatically; some techniques offer marginal accuracy gains while consuming up to 39.6x more energy. Therefore, we also introduce a recommender tool to identify optimal classifiers under user-defined constraints, publicly released with our dataset.

</details>


### [83] [Towards Out-of-Distribution Uncertainty Quantification for Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials via Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41801)
*Stefan P. Schmid,Tobias Setzer,Maximilian Scheurer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在大型多样材料科学数据集上预训练MLIP的不确定性估计器，实现零样本可用、微调后与误差正相关的UQ模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP实际部署需要可靠的不确定性量化（UQ），但现有事后UQ方法多在小数据/窄域上训练，适用域受限；受基础MLIP启发，作者希望通过大规模预训练扩展UQ的适用范围。

Method: 在大型材料科学数据集上预训练多个UQ模型，用于量化不同MLIP的不确定性，并在分布内与分布外（OOD）吸附能预测任务上评估其性能，比较零样本与微调两种设定。

Result: 预训练UQ估计器可在多个数据集上提供可行的零样本不确定性估计；微调后在预测不确定性与预测误差之间稳定呈现正相关，即使在最具挑战性的数据集上亦然。

Conclusion: 结果为面向MLIP的“基础UQ模型”奠定基础，可为用户提供模型可靠性与适用域的先验信息，促进MLIP在实际场景中的部署。

Abstract: Informative uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the practical deployment of machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs), as it provides insight into their reliability across chemical space. Although numerous post-hoc uncertainty estimation methods for MLIPs have recently been proposed, most have been trained in low-data regimes, restricting their applicability to narrow domains of interest. Inspired by the emergence of foundational MLIPs, we propose pre-training uncertainty estimators on large, diverse datasets to enable broader applicability. We pre-train several UQ models on large materials science datasets to quantify the uncertainty of different MLIPs, and evaluate their performance on both in-distribution and out-of-domain (OOD) adsorption-energy predictions. We find that pre-trained uncertainty estimators provide viable zero-shot uncertainty estimates across multiple datasets, while fine-tuning consistently yields positive correlations between predicted uncertainty and prediction error, even on the most challenging datasets. These results lay the groundwork for foundational UQ models for MLIPs, ultimately facilitating their deployment in practical settings by providing users with a priori information about model reliability and applicability.

</details>


### [84] [Backpropagation-free Deep Learning for Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41805)
*Miha Kapš,Blaž Bertalanič,Carolina Fortuna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次系统评估DFA、DRTP、NoProp三种免反向传播学习器在112个UCR单变量时间序列分类数据集上的表现，平均F1为0.76–0.79，低于ROCKET(0.83)与InceptionTime(0.84)；类内时间变异性是与差距最一致相关的数据属性，且池化实验显示DFA/DRTP卷积特征近似随机，NoProp则与BP相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免反向传播学习规则（如DFA、DRTP、NoProp）可缓解更新锁与权重传输问题，但此前几乎只在2D图像基准上评估，缺乏对一维时间序列分类的系统性证据。

Method: 将三种学习器适配到1D卷积架构的单变量TSC任务，每种方法仅用一套超参数优化配置，在UCR档案112个数据集上评测；提出分析框架，将逐数据集相对InceptionTime的差距与数据属性做（偏）Spearman相关分析；并用全局平均池化替换flatten读出，以同一架构下反向传播为对照，通过双重差分（DD）检验卷积特征学习质量。

Result: 优化后平均F1为0.76–0.79，低于ROCKET(0.83)与InceptionTime(0.84)。类内时间变异性与差距关联最稳定（偏Spearman ρ=0.28–0.40）。池化对DFA与DRTP的损害大于BP（DD分别为−0.025与−0.055），NoProp则可容忍池化（DD +0.013）。计算层面：DRTP较BP减少33%训练FLOPs，但仅在序列长度约700以上时训练更快；DFA无计算收益；NoProp峰值内存降低38%，代价是训练时间延长48%。

Conclusion: 免反向传播学习器在当前1D卷积TSC设定下精度仍不及强基线，且DFA/DRTP未能学到有效的卷积特征（表现为对池化敏感），NoProp在特征质量与内存效率上更接近BP。类内时间变异性可作为预判方法适用性的数据属性指标。

Abstract: Deep neural networks for time series classification (TSC) are trained almost exclusively by error backpropagation, which imposes update locking and the weight-transport problem. Backpropagation-free learning rules such as Direct Feedback Alignment (DFA), Direct Random Target Projection (DRTP), and the diffusion-inspired NoProp relax these constraints but have been evaluated almost exclusively on 2D image benchmarks. We adapt these three learners to univariate TSC with 1D convolutional architectures and present the first systematic study of them across 112 datasets from the UCR time series archive, with one hyperparameter-optimized configuration per method. The optimized learners reach mean F1 scores of 0.76 to 0.79, below ROCKET (0.83) and InceptionTime (0.84). We propose an analysis framework that relates the per-dataset gap to InceptionTime to dataset properties and tests convolutional feature learning by replacing the flatten readout with global average pooling, measured as a difference-in-differences (DD) against a backpropagation control on the identical architecture. Intra-class temporal variability is the dataset property most consistently associated with the gap for all three learners (partial Spearman $ρ$ from $0.28$ to $0.40$). Pooling harms DFA and DRTP more than backpropagation (DD $-0.025$ and $-0.055$), consistent with near-random convolutional features, whereas NoProp tolerates pooling as backpropagation does (DD $+0.013$). Relative to backpropagation on the same architectures, DRTP reduces training floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 33% and trains faster only on series longer than about 700 time steps, DFA yields no computational reduction, and NoProp reduces peak memory by 38% at the cost of 48% longer training.

</details>


### [85] [Exploring Bad Minima in Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41807)
*Mina Remeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过三类对抗攻击构造反例，检验"源任务泛化好是否必然带来迁移性好"这一假设，但实证结果反而显示源任务泛化性与可迁移性存在强相关，未能否定其作为迁移充分条件的可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习是深度学习中极为成功的学习范式，可复用已有任务知识；但其成功所需的条件（尤其是可迁移性的必要条件）尚不清晰。作者希望厘清：源任务上的良好泛化是否必然意味着对相关目标任务的可迁移性。

Method: 提出"否"的假设，并尝试在迁移学习流程中通过对抗攻击寻找反例。具体设计三类对抗攻击，针对源任务泛化与迁移性之间的关系进行实证检验。

Result: 三类对抗攻击的实验均未找到推翻假设的反例，反而显示源任务泛化性与可迁移性之间存在强相关性。因此，无法排除"源任务泛化性是成功迁移的充分条件"这一可能。

Conclusion: 原假设（源任务泛化好不必然可迁移）未被支持；源任务泛化表现与可迁移性高度相关，不能否定其作为迁移充分条件的地位。可迁移性的必要条件仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a highly successful learning paradigm in deep learning which leverages knowledge from one task and applies it to another. Despite its success, the necessary conditions for knowledge transfer are unclear and require further research. In this dissertation, our aim is to understand transferability a little better by answering the following question: Does good generalization on a source task necessarily imply transferability to a related target task? We hypothesize the answer is negative, and attempt to find counterexamples via adversarial attacks on the transfer learning procedure. Over three types of adversarial attacks, our empirical results show a strong relationship between generalization on the source task and transferability. These results do not rule out generalization on the source task as a sufficient condition for successful transfer.

</details>


### [86] [Continual Reinforcement Learning Is an Integration Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41809)
*Nis Meinert,Jakob Gawlikowski,Edward Grant,Max Cyprys,Nathan Schurr,Neil Boston,Alfredo Ibias,Milind Tambe,Janusz Marecki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出十项"持续性终身强化学习智能体"设计诉求（desiderata），以此审视现有代表性方法，并给出一个紧凑的持久世界场景作为配套基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身/持续强化学习方法与基准分散地推进不同性质，缺少证据表明十项性质能在单一智能体的单一连续生命周期内共存，需要统一的设计与评估标尺。

Method: 以观点性（opinionated）方式定义十项desiderata，作为设计与评估准则；据此对现有代表性方法进行对照分析，并考察代表性benchmark各自覆盖的目标侧面；另提出一个紧凑的持久世界（persistent-world）可交互场景及相应基准方案。

Result: 对现有方法的对照显示进展多为单项性质的局部推进，未见公开评估能证明十项诉求在同一智能体内集成；现有benchmark分别探测目标的不同部分，分离评估无法确立性质共存。所提持久世界场景用于在单一生命周期内综合考察相关行为能力的广泛集合。

Conclusion: 单项desiderata的进展有价值，但长期目标是将十项整合于单一智能体；为此需要一个能在同一连续生命周期内联合检验多能力的基准，作者以紧凑持久世界场景作为该方向的初步方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an opinionated set of ten desiderata intended to inform the design and evaluation of reinforcement-learning agents that learn continually throughout a continuing lifetime. We use these desiderata to examine a representative selection of existing approaches. Progress on individual desiderata is valuable, but the longer-term aim should be to integrate all ten within a single agent. To our knowledge, no published evaluation has yet provided evidence for such an integration. Likewise, the representative benchmarks we review probe different parts of this target, but separate evaluations do not establish that the corresponding properties coexist within one agent throughout a continuing lifetime. As a secondary contribution, we therefore present a compact persistent-world scenario as a playable companion to the desiderata and outline a proposed benchmark that examines a broad range of associated behavioral capabilities together within one such lifetime.

</details>


### [87] [DIFFNAS: Predictor-Free Neural Architecture Search via Diffusion Model and Zero-Cost Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41818)
*Yize Sun,Hanzhang Ma,Zixin Wu,Xuechun Li,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DIFFNAS：无性能预测器的NAS框架，用零成本代理引导离散图扩散模型生成架构，在NAS-Bench-201上无预训练预测器即找到CIFAR-10/100最优架构（94.37%/73.51%），搜索成本较DiffusionNAG降低17.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS依赖模型训练或学习型性能预测器，计算开销大；已有基于扩散模型的NAS方法仍需训练性能预测器，进一步增加成本。作者希望构建无需预测器、借助免训练指标引导的生成式NAS。

Method: 提出DIFFNAS：采用离散图扩散模型作为架构生成器，在生成过程中以零成本（免训练）性能代理（如MeCo、SWAP）作为条件引导，替代学习型性能预测器，实现低开销的架构搜索。

Result: 在NAS-Bench-201上，无需预训练预测器即找到CIFAR-10与CIFAR-100最优架构，准确率分别为94.37%和73.51%；搜索成本相比DiffusionNAG最多降低17.5倍。在MobileNetV3搜索空间上准确率低于DiffusionNAG。

Conclusion: 扩散模型与零成本代理相结合是可行且有前景的可扩展NAS路径；但其在大搜索空间（如MobileNetV3）上的泛化与精度仍逊于含预测器的基线，为后续改进方向。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has advanced the automated design of deep networks, but remains computationally expensive due to reliance on model training or performance predictors. We introduce DIFFNAS, a predictor-free NAS framework that leverages a discrete graph diffusion model guided by zero-cost performance proxies. Unlike prior diffusion-based NAS methods that require learned performance predictors, DIFFNAS conditions the generative process on training-free metrics such as MeCo and SWAP, enabling efficient architecture search with minimal computational overhead. Experiments on NAS-Bench-201 show that DIFFNAS finds the optimal architectures for CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 (94.37% and 73.51%) without a pretrained predictor, while reducing search cost by up to 17.5x compared to DiffusionNAG. On the MobileNetV3 search space, its accuracy is lower than that of DiffusionNAG. These results highlight the promise of diffusion models combined with zero-cost proxies for scalable and effective architecture search.

</details>


### [88] [ElTab: Elastically Looped Tabular Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41819)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ElTab：通过随机早退监督训练单一弹性循环表格Transformer，在不同算力预算下匹配专用模型，降低训练与推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN等表格Transformer预测性能强但推理成本高，难以在资源受限场景部署；循环（looped）Transformer虽通过权重共享降低参数量，但过早停止（early exit）会显著损失预测能力，缺少能在不同计算预算下自适应的单一模型。

Method: 提出Elastically Looped Tabular Transformer（ElTab）：在循环Transformer架构基础上，对随机采样的早退层施加监督，使单一模型在多个计算预算（深度）下均可运行，实现弹性深度训练。

Result: 在30个OpenML分类数据集上，单个ElTab模型与6个特定深度的循环模型性能相当，训练成本降低一半以上；与12层TabPFN式基线性能相当，推理FLOPs减少25%。

Conclusion: 弹性深度训练可得到单一模型，能根据部署时的算力预算自适应，在预测性能、存储与计算成本之间取得有利权衡。

Abstract: Tabular transformer models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur substantial inference costs, making them difficult to deploy in resource-constrained settings. While looped transformers reduce parameter count through weight sharing, they can lose significant predictive power when stopped early. To address this challenge, we introduce the Elastically Looped Tabular Transformer (ElTab), a single model trained to operate across different computational budgets through supervision at randomly sampled early exits. Across 30 OpenML classification datasets, a single ElTab model matches six depth-specific looped models while cutting training cost by more than half, and matches a 12-layer TabPFN-style baseline with 25% fewer inference FLOPs. These results show that elastic-depth training yields a single model that adapts to the deployment budget at hand, offering a favorable trade-off among predictive performance, storage, and computational cost.

</details>


### [89] [Trainable Vector Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41822)
*Anastasiia Prutianova,Alina Bogdanova,Mikhail Shutov,Dmitrii Poimanov,Anatoly Gorkov,Ilia Zharikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TVQ leverages straight-through estimation to enable gradient-based optimization of vector-quantized LLM weights, combined with block-wise knowledge distillation, achieving state-of-the-art low-bit post-training quantization quality.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQ is effective for compressing LLM weights, but extreme compression degrades performance, and recovery is hard due to the discrete nature of VQ parameters. Existing training-free methods only minimize layer-wise reconstruction loss, which is insufficient.

Method: Proposes TVQ, which uses straight-through estimation to make vector-quantized LLMs trainable via trainable latent weights, enabling gradient-descent optimization toward arbitrary objectives. Applies TVQ within a block-wise knowledge distillation paradigm (balancing quality and training efficiency) and introduces practical solutions to accelerate training.

Result: TVQ is compatible with modern VQ schemes and achieves state-of-the-art quality in low-bit post-training quantization under modest training budgets with straightforward PyTorch implementation. E.g., near-lossless 2-bit quantization of LLaMA2-70B with only 1% accuracy drop on popular QA benchmarks.

Conclusion: By enabling gradient-based optimization of VQ parameters through straight-through estimation and block-wise distillation, TVQ overcomes the limitations of training-free layer-wise reconstruction, delivering strong low-bit quantization performance efficiently.

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) is a rapidly evolving technique for compressing Large Language Model (LLM) weights, reducing memory usage, and speeding up inference. However, extreme compression deteriorates model performance, which is challenging to recover due to the discrete nature of VQ parameters. Existing methods employ training-free approaches that are limited to layer-wise reconstruction loss minimization. To address this limitation, we propose TVQ, a novel method that leverages straight-through estimation to enable gradient descent-based optimization of vector-quantized LLMs. By reformulating VQ LLM optimization with trainable latent weights, TVQ enables arbitrary objective minimization. We constitute our approach by applying TVQ in a block-wise knowledge distillation paradigm, which balances quality and training efficiency, and propose several practical solutions to accelerate training. In experiments on modern LLMs, we demonstrate that TVQ is compatible with modern VQ schemes. As a result, TVQ achieves state-of-the-art quality in low-bit post-training quantization settings with a modest training budget and straightforward PyTorch implementation. For instance, TVQ achieves near-lossless 2-bit quantization for LLaMA2-70B with only 1% accuracy drop on popular Question-Answering benchmarks.

</details>


### [90] [On k-nearest-neighbor regression in metric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41896)
*László Györfi,Vladimir G. Pestov,Harro Walk,Roi Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究度量空间取值特征下k近邻回归的均方一致性，用"邻居选择密度"分离近似误差与条件方差误差，给出弱于Stone支配条件的充分条件，并构造反例说明σ-有限Nagata维不保证无界响应下的均方一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有k-NN一致性理论多依赖Stone支配条件，且在一般度量空间中对无界响应、有限高阶矩情形缺乏精确刻画；分类中的σ-有限Nagata维结论能否推广到回归尚不清楚。

Method: 以原子无条件距离分布的固定特征分布μ为框架，通过独立查询点均匀邻居的密度来刻画邻居选择对概率的重分配；据此分别给出近似误差与条件响应方差的独立条件，并以固定总体质量邻域形式表述条件，结合有限四阶响应矩；最后构造度量空间反例。

Result: 当k_n→∞、k_n/n→0且在有限四阶矩下选择密度具一致L²(μ)界时即可保证均方一致性，弱于Stone支配；固定质量邻域条件可回收欧氏密度界；有限全局Nagata维蕴含Stone支配；构造出σ-有限Nagata维空间上一个具全部有限矩的无噪响应，其均方误差发散。

Conclusion: 邻居选择密度为度量空间k-NN回归一致性提供了统一且更精细的分析工具，明确了矩条件与维数条件的作用边界；σ-有限Nagata维对分类的普适一致性不足以保证无界响应回归的均方一致性。

Abstract: We study mean squared consistency of k-nearest-neighbor regression for metric space valued features. For a fixed feature distribution mu with atomless distance distributions, we describe how neighbor selection redistributes probability through the density of a uniformly chosen neighbor of an independent query. This gives separate conditions for the approximation error and the conditional response variance, and weakens Stone's domination condition when stronger response moments are available. In particular, when k_n tends to infinity and k_n/n tends to zero, a uniform L2(mu) bound on the selection densities suffices under a finite fourth response moment. We give a corresponding condition in terms of neighborhoods of fixed population mass and recover the Euclidean density bound. Finite global Nagata dimension gives Stone's domination. While sigma-finite Nagata dimensionality is known to ensure universal consistency of k-NN classification in complete separable spaces, we show that this is not the case for mean squared consistency with unbounded responses: we construct a space in this class on which one fixed noiseless response with all finite moments has diverging mean squared error.

</details>


### [91] [Binary Sparse Codes: Where are we on Energy Efficient Representations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41933)
*Jose Miguel Ramos,Luis SaCouto,Andreas Wichert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出以"相似性保持"形式化稀疏码的信息保持，并构建可复用评价框架，在匹配条件下比较四类二值稀疏编码方法，揭示稀疏性与信息保持之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经系统在严格代谢约束下依靠稀疏皮层表征实现强大计算，激发了人工系统对稀疏编码的兴趣；但"信息保持"缺乏公认的定量定义，使不同方法之间无法进行有原则的比较。

Method: 将信息保持形式化为相似性保持，即二值稀疏码对输入空间关系几何的还原程度，据此提出一组度量指标，并在匹配条件下比较四类代表性方法：基于有效编码的理论方法、经反向传播训练的深度学习方法、基于局部Hebbian规则的生物可行方法（含Competitive Sparse Codes）。

Result: 实验显示一致权衡：高信息保持的模型以牺牲稀疏性为代价，而接近最优随机稀疏码均匀性的模型往往损失信息保持；Competitive Sparse Codes是唯一在接近最大稀疏的同时仍保有有意义信息保持的架构。

Conclusion: 同时满足高稀疏性与高信息保持仍是该领域的开放挑战；本文提供的评价框架可作为衡量进展的可复用工具。

Abstract: Biological neural systems achieve impressive computational capabilities under tight metabolic constraints, relying on sparse cortical representations that minimise total neural activity. This combination of efficiency and representational power has motivated growing interest in sparse coding as a computational strategy for artificial systems. Artificial models designed to produce binary sparse codes span three broad paradigms, theoretical methods grounded in efficient coding, deep learning methods trained via backpropagation, and biologically plausible methods that learn through local Hebbian rules, each yielding codes whose quality depends not only on achieving low activation density but also on preserving the informational structure of the input. The latter property, however, lacks an agreed-upon quantitative definition, leaving the field without a principled basis for comparing methods. We propose a set of metrics that formalise information preservation as similarity preservation, measuring how faithfully a binary sparse code reproduces the relational geometry of the input space, and apply this framework to compare four representative methods under matched conditions. The results reveal a consistent trade-off in which models that achieve high information preservation do so at the cost of sparsity, while models approaching the uniformity of an optimal random sparse code tend to sacrifice it. Competitive Sparse Codes, a biologically plausible approach, is the only architecture to occupy a region of near-maximal sparsity while retaining meaningful information preservation. These findings frame the simultaneous satisfaction of both factors as an open challenge for the field and provide a reusable evaluation framework for measuring progress toward it.

</details>


### [92] [Observable-Specific Validity Tests for Scientific AI: Controlled Approximations and Intervention Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41939)
*Xiaoshn Nee,Haobo Zhong,Xiaomin Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出“可观测对象特定的有效性记录”，把近似模型与其参考模型、误差度量、容差、参数域及变换/干预绑定，从而对科学AI实施可独立检验的认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似模型可能复现能量却无法复现其响应，或满足误差容限却未被给定界认证；仅凭单一标量指标的验证不足以保证科学AI在不同可观测量上的有效性。

Method: 构建有效性记录框架；对奇异性微扰振子给出显式位移与速度证书；用精确Hubbard二聚体关系区分能量判据与响应判据接受的耦合域；进行16次重复神经拟合并施加量纲齐性约束；采用成对变换检验标度关系；给出机制判别中固定控制查询需求随x^{-2k}的精确标度并数值验证成功曲线；提供256任务评测接口。

Result: 位移与速度证书恢复出不同收敛阶并给出充分有效性保证；能量与响应判据接受的耦合域不同；量纲齐性使能量尺度迁移误差由52.3%降至0.0768%，同时满足能隙与响应容差的中位比例由0升至100%；成对变换验证受保护的标度关系，耦合交叉点揭示响应迁移所需额外信息；因果模型在强负观测关联下恢复出正的干预响应；固定控制查询需求精确标度为x^{-2k}并获数值验证。

Conclusion: 以可观测对象为中心的有效性记录与配套认证方法，为科学AI中的受控近似、物理迁移与机制判别提供了可独立检验的测试基础。

Abstract: An approximation can reproduce an energy while missing its response, or satisfy an error tolerance without being certified by a given bound. We formulate observable-specific validity records for scientific artificial intelligence (AI) that link a reduction to its reference model, error measure, tolerance, parameter domain, and transformations or interventions. Explicit displacement and velocity certificates for a singularly perturbed oscillator recover distinct convergence orders and provide sufficient validity guarantees. Exact Hubbard dimer relations resolve the different coupling domains accepted by energy and response criteria. Across 16 repeated neural fits, imposing dimensional homogeneity reduces energy scale transfer error from 52.3% to 0.0768% and raises the median fraction jointly satisfying gap and response tolerances from zero to 100%. Paired transformations verify the protected scaling relation, while a coupling crossover identifies the additional information needed for response transfer. A causal model recovers a positive intervention response beneath a strong negative observational association. For two mechanisms observed with Gaussian noise whose responses first differ at order $x^k$, we derive an exact fixed control query requirement scaling as $x^{-2k}$ and verify its full success curve numerically. Together with a 256-task evaluation interface, these results provide independently checkable tests of controlled approximation, physical transfer, and mechanism discrimination in scientific AI.

</details>


### [93] [Chronos-2: A foundation model for multivariate time series forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41956)
*Abdul Fatir Ansari,Oleksandr Shchur,Jaris Küken,Andreas Auer,Boran Han,Pedro Mercado,Syama Sundar Rangapuram,Huibin Shen,Lorenzo Stella,Xiyuan Zhang,Mononito Goswami,Shubham Kapoor,Danielle C. Maddix,Pablo Guerron,Florian Saupe,Markus Reichstein,Tony Hu,Junming Yin,Nick Erickson,Prateek Mutalik Desai,Hao Wang,Huzefa Rangwala,George Karypis,Yuyang Wang,Michael Bohlke-Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chronos-2 是一个零样本时间序列基础模型，可直接从历史上下文推断多变量时间序列与异构协变量之间的关系，生成概率预测，并在多领域基准与科学任务上匹配或超越专用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型通常需按领域数据集单独开发与训练；已有时间序列基础模型（TSFM）大多将每条序列独立处理，无法建模序列间交互与外部驱动因素，难以满足科学应用中多变量、含协变量的预测需求。

Method: 提出 Chronos-2：通过大规模预训练获得零样本预测能力；模型不从任务特定结构假设出发，而是直接从历史上下文推断序列间关系，统一处理多变量时间序列与异构协变量，并输出概率预测，无需任务特定处理。

Result: 多类基准上平均达到 SOTA：技能分数较最强基线提升 5.2 个百分点，胜率提升 10 个百分点；计算高效，单块中端 GPU 上 100 条序列预测耗时不足 1 秒，端到端分别比最佳任务特定点预测器与概率预测器快 50 倍和 1000 倍；在传染病预测、宏观经济建模、碳通量预测与径流模拟中匹配或超越各领域专用方法。

Conclusion: 单一预训练模型即可捕获跨时间系统共享的结构，表明基础模型范式可替代逐领域定制建模，适用于科学预测场景。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting with uncertainty quantification is critical in a variety of scientific applications ranging from epidemiology and economics to energy systems and earth sciences. Yet most forecasting models still need to be developed and trained separately for each domain-specific dataset. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) aim to remove this limitation and enable the application of the same model across domains through large-scale pretraining. However, most existing TSFMs treat each time series independently and therefore cannot model interactions among time series and external drivers. Here, we introduce Chronos-2, a foundation model for zero-shot forecasting that models the interactions between multivariate time series and heterogeneous covariates, which commonly arise in scientific applications. The model infers relationships among time series directly from historical context and generates probabilistic forecasts without task-specific treatment. Across various benchmarks, Chronos-2 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on average by improving skill scores by 5.2 percentage points relative to the strongest baselines and increasing win rates by 10 percentage points. Chronos-2 is also computationally efficient, generating forecasts for 100 time series in under one second on a single mid-range GPU, 50x and 1000x faster end-to-end than the best task-specific point and probabilistic forecasters, respectively. In addition, we demonstrate its effectiveness in diverse scientific settings -- including infectious-disease forecasting, macroeconomic modeling, carbon-flux prediction, and runoff simulation -- where the model matches or surpasses specialized methods developed for each domain. These results show that a single pretrained model can capture structure shared across temporal systems.

</details>


### [94] [Robust Learning of Multi-Index Models under Logconcave Distributions via Weak Partial Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41961)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Daniel M. Kane,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于"弱部分学习器"的boosting框架，在对数凹分布下高效学习布尔概念类（多指标模型、半空间函数、多分类线性分类器等），并可扩展至部分分类与可测试学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典boosting依赖分布无关弱学习器（常不可行）或仅给出弱近似保证；需要在对数凹边缘分布下为宽泛的多指标模型族获得更快、更鲁棒的学习器。

Method: 1）以弱部分学习器（标签可在部分域上未定义）实现强不可知学习；2）针对boosting所需的条件分布非对数凹问题，设计递归的矩匹配划分：将对数凹X与条件X'的空间划分为多项式多个凸区域，使多数区域上低阶矩近似一致；3）在"好"区域上，良态多指标模型与低阶多项式相关，构成弱假设；4）核心组件是矩差异检测：结合矩张量的谱分析与对数凹测度的等周不等式，寻找捕捉X与X'非平凡差异的低维子空间。

Result: 得到比已有方法显著更快的鲁棒学习器；覆盖多指标模型、半空间函数、多分类线性分类器；支持部分分类与可测试学习；矩差异检测方法具独立价值。

Conclusion: 弱部分学习器足以支撑强不可知学习；矩匹配划分与矩差异检测使boosting在条件分布非对数凹时依然可行，从而统一改进多类基本概念类的学习效率。

Abstract: We develop a new algorithmic framework for learning Boolean concept classes under logconcave distributions. Our framework applies to broad families of multi-index models and yields significantly faster robust learners than were previously known in these settings. It extends to partial classification and testable learning. Consequently, we obtain improved learners for several fundamental concept classes, including functions of halfspaces and multiclass linear classifiers, under logconcave marginals.
  Our starting point is a new boosting framework based on weak partial learners. Classical boosting approaches require often-intractable distribution-free weak learners or yield weak approximation guarantees. We show instead that weak learners for partial classification, where labels may be undefined on part of the domain, suffice for strong agnostic learning. The key challenge is that boosting requires a weak learner that succeeds under conditioned distributions that are not guaranteed to be logconcave. To address this, we develop a recursive moment-based partitioning procedure: given a logconcave distribution $X$ and a conditional version $X'$, we partition the space into polynomially many convex regions so that, on most regions, the low-degree moments of $X$ and $X'$ approximately agree. On each good region, a well-behaved multi-index model correlates with a low-degree polynomial, giving a weak hypothesis. Combining these hypotheses yields the required weak learner.
  A key ingredient of our approach is an efficient method for detecting moment discrepancy. Specifically, we show how to find a low-dimensional subspace that captures any non-trivial discrepancy between $X$ and $X'$, if one exists. Our approach combines spectral analysis of moment tensors with recent progress on isoperimetry for logconcave measures. This discrepancy-finding procedure may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [95] [New Lower Bounds for Private PAC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41980)
*Prathamesh Dharangutte,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究近似差分隐私下可实现PAC学习的样本复杂度下界框架，提出独立类打包（ICP）参数，证明VC维和Littlestone维不能决定私有样本复杂度，并用ICP给出多个假设类的紧或改进下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在(ε,δ)-差分隐私与(α,β)-PAC的可实现学习设定中，传统组合维度（VC维、Littlestone维）是否足以刻画样本复杂度尚不清楚，需要建立统一的下界证明框架并厘清维度与私有样本复杂度之间的关系。

Method: 构造具有相同VC维和Littlestone维、但在共同隐私与精度参数下私有样本复杂度不同的类，说明二者不能刻画复杂度（即使对非真学习器亦然）；引入Independent Class Packing (ICP)参数，即类族G中的类可被独立打包进H的最大总样本复杂度，并据此证明任何(ε,δ)-DP、(α,β)-PAC学习器（可非真）需Ω(ICP(H,G)/(αε))个样本。

Result: 利用ICP框架，为轴平行矩形、区间并、线性子空间得到紧下界，为半空间与凸多边形得到改进下界；对乘积类，以及更一般地当同一类族既能打包进H又能覆盖其定义域从而使目标标签可直接使用时，ICP在对数因子内刻画私有PAC样本复杂度。

Conclusion: VC维与Littlestone维不足以决定近似差分隐私可实现PAC学习的样本复杂度；ICP参数为证明私有样本复杂度下界提供了通用框架，并在若干类上达到紧或改进结果，在乘积类等情形下还能刻画复杂度至对数因子。

Abstract: We study realizable PAC learning under approximate differential privacy, and give a framework for proving lower bounds on its sample complexity. We construct classes with identical VC and Littlestone dimensions but different private sample complexities at common privacy and accuracy parameters, showing that these dimensions do not determine the sample complexity, even for improper learners. To address this we introduce the Independent Class Packing (ICP) parameter, the largest total sample complexity of classes in $\mathcal{G}$ that can be packed independently into a class $\mathcal{H}$, and prove that every $(\varepsilon, δ)$-DP, $(α, β)$-PAC learner for $\mathcal{H}$, possibly improper, uses $Ω\left(\mathrm{ICP}(\mathcal{H}, \mathcal{G})/(α\varepsilon)\right)$ examples. Using this framework we prove tight lower bounds for axis-aligned rectangles, union of intervals and linear subspaces and improved lower bounds for halfspaces and convex polygons. Finally we also show that for product classes, and more generally when the same classes pack into $\mathcal{H}$ and cover its domain so that target labels can be used directly, ICP characterizes private PAC sample complexity up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [96] [Hear No Evil: Detecting Gradient Leakage by Malicious Servers in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20651)
*Fei Wang,Baochun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从防御者视角系统分析联邦学习中的恶意梯度泄漏攻击及其模型操纵手段，揭示"攻击有效性"与"隐蔽性"之间的根本权衡，并提出一种轻量客户端侧检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明联邦学习中的梯度更新可能泄露客户端本地敏感数据，而恶意服务器通过操纵全局模型可诱发信息量更大的更新，使风险加剧。作者希望从防御者角度全面审视这类攻击的实际威胁程度。

Method: 首次综合分析恶意梯度泄漏攻击及支撑它们的模型操纵技术，并在引入常见归一化技术和联邦平均的真实FL设定下评估攻击表现；据此提出一种简单的客户端侧检测机制，在本地训练开始前标记可疑的模型更新。

Result: 发现核心权衡：此类攻击无法同时做到高重构效果与足够隐蔽以规避检测，尤其在有归一化和联邦平均的真实FL场景中；攻击在实践中本质受限，且通常可通过基础监控被发现。

Conclusion: 恶意梯度泄漏攻击虽在理论上值得关注，但实际威胁有限；所提轻量检测机制以极小开销进一步佐证了防御可行性，为注重隐私的联邦学习系统提供可部署的防护手段。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that gradient updates in federated learning (FL) can unintentionally reveal sensitive information about a client's local data. This risk becomes significantly greater when a malicious server manipulates the global model to provoke information-rich updates from clients. In this paper, we adopt a defender's perspective to provide the first comprehensive analysis of malicious gradient leakage attacks and the model manipulation techniques that enable them. Our investigation reveals a core trade-off: these attacks cannot be both highly effective in reconstructing private data and sufficiently stealthy to evade detection -- especially in realistic FL settings that incorporate common normalization techniques and federated averaging.
  Building on this insight, we argue that malicious gradient leakage attacks, while theoretically concerning, are inherently limited in practice and often detectable through basic monitoring. As a complementary contribution, we propose a simple, lightweight, and broadly applicable client-side detection mechanism that flags suspicious model updates before local training begins, despite the fact that such detection may not be strictly necessary in realistic FL settings. This mechanism further underscores the feasibility of defending against these attacks with minimal overhead, offering a deployable safeguard for privacy-conscious federated learning systems.

</details>


### [97] [Less Loss, Fixed Weights: Logit Corrections for Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42000)
*Hanyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文检验能否用简单的固定词表偏置（或加温度）修正来逆转语言模型在指令微调/继续预训练后对标准网页文本的"遗忘"，结论是大部分损失无法由此类受限拟合恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调或领域继续预训练会降低模型对通用网页文本的预测能力（遗忘）。作者希望验证这种能力损失是否只是输出层一个简单的、固定不变的偏移问题，从而可由极简后处理恢复。

Method: 在14对基座与派生模型（1B–7B）上，为每个词元拟合固定的奖励/惩罚（词表偏置），单独使用或与温度参数联合；在未参与拟合的留出文本上评估恢复效果（以top-4K词表偏置为主，损失在保留词表条件下计算）；并设计随机破坏的受控模拟作为对照。

Result: 留出集上，最大top-4K偏置恢复估计不足10%；偏置加温度最多恢复36%；数学继续预训练的模型损失最大且几乎无法恢复；在无植入偏置/温度变化的随机损坏模拟中，偏置+温度仍能"恢复"29%，提示该指标存在人为增益。

Conclusion: 真实模型的损失增量大多超出固定偏置与温度这类受限拟合的可达范围，遗忘并非简单的输出层平移；此类修正的恢复量还包含模拟中即可复现的非因果成分，需谨慎解读。

Abstract: When a language model is fine-tuned to follow instructions, or further pretrained on a new subject, it often gets worse at predicting standard web text. This is known as "forgetting." We ask whether this loss can be undone by simple, fixed adjustments to the model's final token predictions. Across 14 pairs of base and derived models, from 1B to 7B parameters, we fitted a fixed bonus or penalty for each token (a vocabulary bias), alone or together with a temperature. On text held out from correction fitting, the largest top-4K bias recovery estimate is under 10%, with loss conditional on the retained vocabulary. Combining the bias with a temperature recovers up to 36%. Models further pretrained on math lose the most and recover almost nothing. Finally, in a controlled simulation where a model is damaged at random, with no planted bias or temperature change, the bias-plus-temperature fix still recovers 29%. Most of the real-model loss increase stays out of reach of our restricted fits.

</details>


### [98] [Leaner Training, Lower Leakage: Revisiting Memorization in LLM Fine-Tuning with LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20856)
*Fei Wang,Baochun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视微调阶段的记忆化问题，发现LoRA微调与全量微调在记忆化规律上存在显著分歧：模型规模、数据重复等因素对LoRA影响不同；基于更宽松的相似度记忆化指标，LoRA在保持任务性能的同时显著降低记忆化风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练阶段的记忆化已被充分研究，但微调阶段（尤其广泛采用的LoRA参数高效微调）中的记忆化影响研究较少，而记忆化会使LLM面临数据抽取攻击风险，因此需明确不同微调策略下的记忆化特性。

Method: 重新审视微调中的记忆化，对比LoRA微调与全量微调在模型规模、数据重复等影响记忆化的因素上的表现差异；并采用更宽松的基于相似度的记忆化度量指标进行评估。

Result: 在预训练和全量微调中强烈影响记忆化的模型规模与数据重复等因素，在LoRA微调中并不遵循相同趋势；相似度记忆化指标显示LoRA相较全量微调显著降低记忆化风险，同时仍保持较强的任务性能。

Conclusion: LoRA微调在记忆化行为上不同于全量微调，能有效缓解记忆化带来的数据抽取风险，且不牺牲任务性能，为参数高效微调的隐私安全性提供了新证据。

Abstract: Memorization in large language models (LLMs) makes them vulnerable to data extraction attacks. While pre-training memorization has been extensively studied, fewer works have explored its impact in fine-tuning, particularly for LoRA fine-tuning, a widely adopted parameter-efficient method.
  In this work, we re-examine memorization in fine-tuning and uncover a surprising divergence from prior findings across different fine-tuning strategies. Factors such as model scale and data duplication, which strongly influence memorization in pre-training and full fine-tuning, do not follow the same trend in LoRA fine-tuning. Using a more relaxed similarity-based memorization metric, we demonstrate that LoRA significantly reduces memorization risks compared to full fine-tuning, while still maintaining strong task performance.

</details>


### [99] [When Does Jacobian Fine-Tuning Stabilize Neural PDE Emulators?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42013)
*Shreeyam Bangera,Manya Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一阶误差分解与"放大比"(amplification ratio)诊断量，将混沌PDE神经仿真器的自回归误差增长拆分为模型自身动力学放大与物理混沌放大两部分；雅可比微调的收益约等于其单步精度代价除以放大比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经PDE仿真器以单步训练、自回归部署，误差会累积。基于雅可比的正则化常被用来抑制误差增长，但在混沌系统中部分误差增长是物理上固有的、无法通过训练消除，因此需要区分"可训练消除"与"不可消除"的误差来源，以理解并预判这类方法何时有效。

Method: 提出一阶误差分解：将仿真器误差与一个反事实轨迹对比——把相同的单步误差通过求解器雅可比传播，从而定义放大比（模型自身动力学放大其误差的倍数）。在四个PDE、五种架构、两种模型规模（8–10k与33k参数）、共21种配置上，于一个Lyapunov时间处测量误差；并比较雅可比微调、沿模型自身误差方向匹配求解器雅可比（EDJM）与随机方向、输入噪声注入等方案，统计35个随机种子。

Result: 雅可比微调在放大比≤1.25的34个种子中仅1个误差下降超过10%；而在放大比≥4的20个种子中全部下降，几何平均误差分别降低约8倍（小模型）与55倍（33k模型）。增益约等于微调的单步精度代价除以放大比。EDJM在35个种子中的28个上优于同代价的随机方向；输入噪声注入效果与EDJM相当。

Conclusion: 放大比是一个可预测雅可比正则化收益的诊断指标：只有当模型自身动力学显著放大误差时，雅可比微调才带来大幅提升，且收益被单步精度代价与放大比定量刻画。匹配误差方向的雅可比约束比随机方向更有效，输入噪声注入是等价且更简单的替代方案。

Abstract: Neural emulators of chaotic PDEs are trained on one-step prediction but deployed autoregressively, where their errors compound. Jacobian-based regularisers aim to curb this growth, but in a chaotic system part of the growth is physical and cannot be trained away. We separate the two sources of this error with a first-order decomposition. We compare the emulator's error with a counterfactual that propagates the same one-step mistakes through the solver's Jacobian, giving an amplification ratio: the factor by which the model's own dynamics amplify its errors. We study 21 configurations across four PDEs, five architectures and two model sizes (8-10k and 33k parameters), with errors measured at one Lyapunov time. Jacobian fine-tuning reduced the error by more than 10% for 1 of 34 seeds whose amplification ratio was at most 1.25, and for all 20 seeds whose ratio was at least four; for the latter, the error fell by a factor of 8 (small models) and 55 (33k models) in geometric mean over seeds. The gain approximately equals the one-step accuracy cost of fine-tuning divided by the amplification ratio. Matching the solver's Jacobian along the model's own error direction (EDJM) outperformed random directions at the same cost on 28 of 35 seeds; input-noise injection performed comparably to EDJM.

</details>


### [100] [Know When the Equation Is Wrong: Structural Uncertainty for LLM-Guided Symbolic Regression under Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42024)
*Mostafa Cham,Bayu Adhi Tama,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-SR-UQ框架，保留多个竞争的方程结构，用结构间分歧刻画函数形式不确定性，并将其扩展为结构一致性区间，以改善符号回归在外推下的区间覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导的符号回归方法多只输出单个最优方程，缺乏对备选函数形式的不确定性量化；不同方程可同样拟合观测数据却在外推时表现迥异，标准保形区间在LLM-SRBench上分布内覆盖达标但外推时经常失效。

Method: 保留多个竞争方程结构，以其预测分歧表征函数形式层面的不确定性；将标准定宽保形方法扩展为"结构区间"（structural intervals），度量候选方程间分歧并定位预测区间应加宽的区域。

Result: 大量实验表明，引入结构分歧后覆盖率显著提升，同时保持分布内覆盖；方法在应用于其他符号回归方法时也表现出良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 以方程结构分歧为核心的不确定性量化可有效弥补单一最优方程在分布外覆盖上的不足，且框架具通用性，可迁移至其他符号回归方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can help discover mathematical equations from data, but most LLM-guided symbolic regression methods focus on a single best equation without quantifying uncertainty over alternative functional forms. This is challenging because distinct equations can fit the observed data similarly while producing substantially different extrapolations. Our experiments on the LLM-SRBench dataset show that standard conformal intervals achieve nominal in-distribution coverage but frequently fail under extrapolation. To tackle this challenge, we introduce LLM-SR-UQ, a framework that retains competing equation structures and uses their disagreement to characterize uncertainty about the underlying functional form. We extend the standard fixed-width conformal approach to quantify structural intervals, which measure disagreement among candidate equations and identify where prediction intervals should widen. Extensive experiments show our framework substantially improves coverage attainment by incorporating structural disagreement while preserving in-distribution coverage. Our approach also shows good generalizability when it is applied to other symbolic-regression methods.

</details>


### [101] [GhostPrefix: Synchronization-Free Universal Prefix Attacks for ASR Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42026)
*Shengding Liu,Ashwin Innuganti,Qiben Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GhostPrefix 是首个针对 ASR 的免同步通用对抗前缀攻击：学习一段可复用的短音频前缀，前置到任意实时语音即可实时操控识别输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频对抗攻击多为逐条语音定制、需与受害者波形同步、或仅针对狭窄目标，难以在未知且不断变化的实时语音上通用实施。

Method: 核心挑战是跨未知语音上下文保持稳定对抗效果。方法结合 token 级损失与新颖的表征级目标以获得输入无关的稳定效果；支持三种模式：提示型前缀（语义引导）、目标型前缀（转写劫持）、非目标型前缀（转写降质）。并以良性环境声分布正则化提升感知隐蔽性，同时增强对空中传输失真的鲁棒性。

Result: 在四大系列共 13 个开源 ASR 模型上评测：数字域提示型引导成功率 97.6%、目标劫持 93.4%，非目标攻击将 WER 推高至 1.18；空中传输下两模式成功率分别为 91.5% 和 89.5%。攻击可传播至语音助手、AI 笔记等下游管线，造成系统性语义误读。

Conclusion: GhostPrefix 揭示了免同步通用前缀是对真实世界 ASR 及其下游应用的一种实用且尚未充分探索的威胁。

Abstract: We present GhostPrefix, the first synchronization-free universal adversarial prefix attack against automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems. Unlike prior audio attacks, which are typically crafted per utterance, require synchronization with the victim waveform, or target narrow objectives, GhostPrefix learns a short, reusable audio segment prepended to arbitrary live speech to exert real-time control over ASR outputs without knowledge of the future utterance or temporal alignment. A key challenge is maintaining stable adversarial effects across unknown, evolving speech contexts. GhostPrefix combines token-level losses with a novel representation-level objective for stable, input-agnostic effects. GhostPrefix supports three attack modes: (i) prompted prefix attacks for semantic steering, (ii) targeted prefix attacks for transcript hijacking, and (iii) untargeted prefix attacks for transcription degradation. To remain perceptually inconspicuous, we regularize prefixes toward benign environmental-sound distributions and improve robustness against over-the-air distortions. We evaluate GhostPrefix across 13 open-source ASR models from four families. In digital settings, it achieves average attack success rates of 97.6% for prompted steering and 93.4% for targeted hijacking, while driving word error rate to 1.18 under untargeted attacks. Over the air, it achieves success rates of 91.5% and 89.5% for prompted and targeted modes, respectively. These manipulations propagate into downstream AI-assisted pipelines, including voice assistants and AI note-taking tools, causing systematic semantic misinterpretations. GhostPrefix exposes synchronization-free universal prefixes as a practical, under-explored threat to real-world ASR and downstream applications. Artifacts and demos are available at https://ghostprefix.github.io/.

</details>


### [102] [CausalXRL: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Causal Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42028)
*Yanming Zhang,Eric Papenhausen,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CausalXRL框架，利用因果图推理为强化学习智能体提供透明、结构化、多层次的决策解释，并通过定量实验与专家定性研究验证其解释保真度与可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在复杂环境中性能优异，但以可解释性为代价，导致信任度下降、调试与改进智能体行为困难，因此需要可解释强化学习（XRL）方法。

Method: CausalXRL框架的核心是因果图推理（causal graph reasoning），以生成透明、结构化、多层次的智能体决策解释；验证采用案例研究加两部分评估：（1）在基准RL环境中定量分析解释保真度与因果结构学习效率；（2）在RTS基准MicroRTS上开展定性专家研究评估可解释性。

Result: 定量结果表明CausalXRL能在高效学习因果结构的同时提供保真解释；定性专家研究显示参与者认为该框架有助于审视高层RTS策略。

Conclusion: 因果图推理是一种可行的XRL路径，可兼顾解释保真度与结构学习效率，并在真实策略类任务中获得专家认可。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a powerful paradigm for training autonomous agents and has achieved impressive performance in complex environments. However, this success often comes at the cost of interpretability, diminishing trust and complicating efforts to debug and improve agent behavior. To address these challenges, we introduce CausalXRL, a novel framework for explainable reinforcement learning (XRL). A key feature of CausalXRL is its use of causal graph reasoning, which provides transparent, structured, multi-level explanations of agent decision-making. We validate CausalXRL through comprehensive case studies and a two-part evaluation: (1) a quantitative analysis of explanation fidelity and causal-structure learning efficiency in benchmark RL environments, and (2) a qualitative expert study assessing explainability in the real-time strategy (RTS) benchmark MicroRTS. The quantitative results show that CausalXRL can provide faithful explanations while efficiently learning causal structures, and the qualitative expert study suggests that participants found CausalXRL useful for inspecting high-level RTS strategies.

</details>


### [103] [There Is No Free Loop: When Does Looping a Trained Model Pay for Its Inference Compute?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42030)
*Rhea Sanjay Sukthanker,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验"预训练后给模型加循环(looping)"是否值得其额外推理成本：在 Qwen3/Qwen3.5 0.8B–9B 上，仅 0.8B 循环模型同时胜过等参数与等FLOPs两个基线，4B/9B 连等参数无循环基线都胜不过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加循环不改变参数量、磁盘大小、显存占用与训练成本，因此很有吸引力；但每个生成token需多付约 (N+m)/N 倍的推理代价。大规模预训练中学到的循环能证明该代价合理，而预训练后再加循环是否值得尚不清楚，论文即针对此问题做受控比较。

Method: 重复应用 N 层中的 m 层构成循环。设两个对照基线：(1) 同模型、同训练方式但无循环（等参数）；(2) 在相同推理 FLOPs 下无循环模型的最佳配置（等FLOPs）。在 Qwen3 与 Qwen3.5、0.8B 至 9B 参数规模上系统对比。

Result: 仅 0.8B 循环模型同时胜过两个基线；在 4B 与 9B 上，循环模型未胜过等参数的无循环基线。该结果并非因基准过于简单：同一基准上无循环 9B 模型仍比 4B 模型高出最多 13 分，说明仍有充足提升空间。

Conclusion: 在已预训练模型上引入循环，只在"固定参数预算的小模型"场景下划算；对大模型加循环不值得其额外推理开销。

Abstract: Looping re-applies a block of layers so that each token passes through the block more than once. Adding a loop to a trained model is attractive because it leaves the parameter count, and hence the disk size, the memory footprint and the training cost unchanged. Unfortunately, every generated token pays for the loop. Re-applying m of N layers makes each token about (N+m)/N times as expensive. Loops that a model learns during large-scale pretraining justify this price, but whether loops added after pretraining justify this cost remains unclear. We compare a looped model with two simple baselines. We first compare it with the same model trained identically without the loop (equal parameters). Secondly, we compare it with the best unlooped use of the same inference floating-point operations (equal FLOPs). Across Qwen3 and Qwen3.5 models from 0.8B to 9B parameters, only the looped 0.8B model beats both baselines. At 4B and 9B, the looped model does not beat the first baseline, i.e. the same model trained without the loop. This is not because the benchmarks are too easy, since on the same benchmarks the unlooped 9B model still scores up to 13 points higher than the 4B model, showing that there is ample room for improvement. Introducing loops in an already pretrained model therefore only pays for small models with a fixed parameter budget.

</details>


### [104] [A richness-sensitive Acoustic Biodiversity Index from species-level detections: classifier benchmarking, false-positive control and validation against standard soundscape indices in an African savanna and an urban zoo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42042)
*Muhammad Saeed,Dmitry Mikhaylov,Mohamed H. Alhosani,Yasser F. Al Wahedi,Rory Pilossof,Ahmed Arabi,Walid Shaaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并检验基于物种级检测的声学生物多样性指数ABI_E（检测计数香农多样性除以区域物种池对数），在津巴布韦与阿布扎比录音中与物种丰富度强相关（rho=0.93），优于六种传统声学指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声学指数仅概括声能分布而非发声物种，常与物种丰富度相关性弱，需构建可解释、可控假阳性的物种级多样性指标。

Method: 先在有强标注的热带数据集（1216段鸣声、24类）上以按录音分组交叉验证比较四种分类器；再以带地点季节过滤的BirdNET v2.4处理津巴布韦赞比西河谷稀树草原与阿布扎比动物园三生境录音，追加严格事后地点过滤（剔除9个不合理物种的517个高置信检测），并按昼夜时段匹配稀释至等努力量（6小时）后计算ABI_E与稳定性T。

Result: BirdNET嵌入+逻辑回归最佳（macro-F1=0.750±0.029）；随机帧级划分使准确率由0.41虚高至0.70。等努力下稀树草原21种（1D=9.83，ABI_E=0.38），动物园1.3–13种（ABI_E=0.03–0.21）。149录音小时内ABI_E与丰富度Spearman rho=0.93，六种标准指数最高仅ACI的0.48。稳定性T稀树草原0.39低于动物园0.65–0.67，显示其对自然昼夜节律的惩罚。

Conclusion: 具备显式假阳性控制、明确区域参考池与努力量标准化的物种级指数，比基于声能的声学指数更具可解释性与生态意义；但稳定性项会惩罚自然昼夜活动节律，宜作为独立分量报告。

Abstract: Acoustic indices are widely used as rapid proxies for biodiversity, but they summarise the distribution of sound energy rather than the species producing it and often track richness poorly. We revise and test an Acoustic Biodiversity Index (ABI) built from species-level detections. Its primary form, ABI_E, is Shannon diversity of detections normalised by the regional species pool expected at the site and season (H / ln S_ref); temporal stability T is reported as a separate component. We first benchmarked four classifiers on a strongly labelled tropical dataset (1,216 calls, 24 classes) with recording-grouped cross-validation. BirdNET embeddings + logistic regression performed best (macro-F1 0.750 +/- 0.029), and random frame-level splits inflated accuracy from 0.41 to 0.70. We then applied BirdNET v2.4 with a location and season filter to recordings from a riverine savanna in the Zambezi Valley, Zimbabwe, and from three habitats at Emirates Park Zoo and Resort, Abu Dhabi, United Arab Emirates. A stricter post-hoc location filter removed 517 high-confidence detections of 9 implausible species, all at the zoo. After diel-matched rarefaction to equal effort (6 h), the savanna site held 21 species (Hill number 1D = 9.83; ABI_E = 0.38) against 1.3-13 species (ABI_E = 0.03-0.21) at the zoo sites. Across 149 recorded hours, ABI_E correlated strongly with richness (Spearman rho = 0.93), whereas six standard indices did not (best: ACI, rho = 0.48). The stability term was lower at the savanna site (0.39) than at the zoo (0.65-0.67), showing that it penalises the natural diel cycle of bird activity. Species-level indices with explicit false-positive control, a declared regional reference pool and effort standardisation provide a more interpretable biodiversity indicator than energy-based acoustic indices.

</details>


### [105] [Differentially Private Synthetic Data from Sliced Chebyshev Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42081)
*Lucas Rosenblatt,R. Teal Witter,Christopher Musco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPLICE：基于噪声切片Chebyshev多项式矩匹配的高维差分隐私合成数据算法，Sliced Wasserstein-1误差为 Õ(1/√n)，且与维度 d 无关，并在基准上达到SOTA。
