<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实用DP合成数据方法多只近似保持低维边缘分布，高维全局结构仅靠“幸运副产品”实现；而具备全局精度保证的方法代价随维度 d 指数增长，面临“维数灾难”。

Method: 测量目标分布的噪声切片Chebyshev多项式矩，并拟合一个分布去匹配这些矩；将近期一维多项式合成数据方法推广到高维，并借助张量分解文献工具实现实用化。

Result: 在常数隐私参数下，对 n 条样本返回的私有分布与目标分布的Sliced Wasserstein-1距离为 Õ(1/√n)，且与 d 无关；附带矩匹配理论结果：单位球上分布沿 Õ(d/α²) 个随机方向匹配前 O(1/α) 个Chebyshev矩即保证 α 的Sliced Wasserstein误差；在广泛基准上性能优于先前DP合成数据方法。

Conclusion: SPLICE以维度无关的全局分布保证缓解了DP合成数据中的维数灾难，兼具理论保证与实际SOTA表现。

Abstract: Research on practical differentially private (DP) synthetic data has largely focused on methods that approximately preserve low-dimensional marginals of a target distribution, and can thus only hope to preserve higher-dimensional, global structure as a lucky by-product. At the same time, existing methods that target global accuracy guarantees pay a price exponential in the data dimension, $d$.
  In this work, we address this ``curse of dimensionality'' by presenting a new DP synthetic data algorithm, SPLICE, that comes with a compelling global distributional guarantee, even in high-dimensions, and moreover, is practically state-of-the-art. Our algorithm is based on measuring noisy sliced Chebyshev polynomial moments of the target distribution and fitting a distribution to match, a twist on recent polynomial methods for 1-D synthetic data.
  We prove that, given a dataset of $n$ examples, SPLICE returns a private distribution with Sliced Wasserstein-1 distance $\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$ from the target, for constant privacy parameters. Notably, this bound is independent of the data dimension, $d$. Along the way, we prove a moment matching result of independent interest: for a distribution on the unit ball, matching the first $O(1/α)$ Chebyshev moments along $\tilde{O}(d/α^2)$ random directions guarantees $α$ Sliced Wasserstein error.
  Finally, we provide a practical implementation of the algorithm that borrows tools from the tensor decomposition literature and achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to prior DP synthetic data methods on an extensive benchmark.

</details>


### [106] [Cost-Aware Stopping for Freeze-Thaw Bayesian Optimization: When Does One-Step Lookahead Suffice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42085)
*Long Vu,Jongseong Brad Choi,Binoy Ravindran,Zhoulai Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"EI停止"规则：在冻结-解冻贝叶斯优化中，每步推进期望改进(EI)最大的候选，当无候选EI超过单次增量成本时停止；并构造下界证书识别并修正过早停止。理论保证效用不降，实验显示高成本下EI停止不劣于修正、低成本下两基准修正更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冻结-解冻BO虽以可恢复增量评估候选，但通常停在固定预算或收敛判据处，缺乏对"每次增量成本"的显式权衡。作者希望定义一个无预算、成本感知的目标——最优观测分数减每次增量成本——并给出简单且可分析的停止准则。

Method: 最大化"最优观测分数 − 每增量成本"的无预算效用；采用一步前瞻：在固定代理模型下推进EI最大的候选，一旦最大EI不超过成本即停止(EI停止)。为纠正可能的过早停止，构造两步前瞻价值的下界证书，证明某次停止为时过早；反复修正该停止。

Result: 理论上EI停止的期望效用不低于立即停止，修正步骤在代理模型下永不降低期望效用；代理模型下EI停止在低成本时平均而言过早、高成本时损失很小。三个超参数优化基准上的预注册检验表明，多数高成本设置中EI停止对修正不劣，三个中有两个在低成本下修正更优；代理模型信息共享越多，EI停止效用越高。在两个已发表方法的代理模型上，EI停止以更低分数换取更少训练增量。

Conclusion: EI停止是一种简单、成本感知的参考准则，其效用高度依赖代理模型；证书修正可安全地提升（或至少不降）效用，并可作为诊断过早停止的工具。

Abstract: Freeze-thaw Bayesian optimization (BO) evaluates candidates in resumable increments but usually stops at a budget or convergence test. We maximize a budget-free utility, the best observed score minus a cost per increment, with a one-step lookahead: advance the candidate with the largest expected improvement (EI) under a fixed surrogate, and stop once no EI exceeds the cost. This rule, EI stopping, never falls below stopping immediately in expected utility, yet it can stop too early. We construct a certificate, a lower bound on the value of two-step lookahead, that proves such a stop premature, and repeating this correction never lowers expected utility under the surrogate. Under the surrogate, EI stops are premature on average at low costs and lose little at high costs. On three hyperparameter optimization benchmarks, a pre-registered test finds EI stopping noninferior to the correction in most high-cost settings, while the correction gains at low costs on two of the three; more information sharing in the surrogate raises EI stopping's utility. On the surrogates of two published methods, EI stopping trades lower scores for fewer training increments. EI stopping is a simple cost-aware reference whose utility depends on the surrogate.

</details>


### [107] [Towards Understanding the Impact of Plasticity Loss on Reinforcement Learning in Stochastic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42100)
*Philipp Bordne,André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统研究随机环境下深度强化学习的可塑性丧失问题：在带观测/动作噪声的DeepMind Control任务上训练SAC，发现保可塑性方法在较难任务上更稳健，且噪声水平越高优势越大；重置+层归一化在所有噪声水平下均劣于单独重置；随机性会加速难环境中的可塑性丧失、在简单环境中反起正则化作用；可塑性丧失集中于训练早期，在最具挑战的随机设定下TD不稳定性成为主要失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度RL实践脆弱，现有研究将脆弱性归因于神经网络的可塑性丧失，但该现象在随机（stochastic）环境中研究不足，作者希望系统刻画随机性与可塑性丧失之间的相互作用。

Method: 在三个DeepMind Control任务上引入受控水平的观测噪声与动作噪声，训练SAC智能体，比较保可塑性方法（如重置、层归一化等组合）在不同噪声水平下的表现，并分析可塑性丧失随训练阶段的变化。

Result: 保可塑性方法提升学习鲁棒性，且在两个较难任务上随观测噪声增大优势扩大；重置+层归一化在每个测试噪声水平下均不如单独重置；随机性在难环境中加速可塑性丧失，在简单环境中可充当保持可塑性的正则项；可塑性丧失主要发生在训练早期。

Conclusion: 随机性对可塑性丧失的影响依赖任务难度；在极端随机设定下，失败主因从渐进的可塑性丧失转变为TD学习的不稳定性，提示需针对TD稳定性而非仅针对可塑性设计干预措施。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) is a powerful framework for sequential decision-making. Despite many high-profile successes in recent years, deep RL is known to be brittle. An emerging line of research attributes this brittleness to a loss of plasticity in neural networks. However, plasticity loss has so far been understudied in stochastic environments. We study this interplay systematically, training SAC agents on three DeepMind Control tasks augmented with controlled levels of observation and action noise. We find that plasticity-preserving methods increase learning robustness on the two harder tasks, with their advantage growing as observation noise increases, while combining resets with layer normalization underperforms resets alone at every observation noise level tested. Further, stochasticity can accelerate the loss of plasticity in harder environments, whereas in easier settings noise can act as a regularizer that sustains plasticity. Lastly, plasticity loss is most pronounced in the early stages of training. In the most challenging stochastic settings, TD instability rather than gradual plasticity loss becomes the dominant failure mode.

</details>


### [108] [Hyperbolic Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42104)
*Iulia Rusu,Anoop Praturu,Sergei Gepshtein,Tatyana Sharpee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双曲逆强化学习（Hyperbolic IRL），以特征向量与学习参数向量间的双曲距离参数化奖励函数，在低数据与密集环境下性能优于现有基线，并提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IRL奖励函数（线性权重、神经网络等）均未对特征空间的几何结构作任何假设，而神经回路中观察到几何组织特征，因此缺失一个刻画特征空间几何的奖励参数化方式。

Method: 将奖励建模为特征向量与学习参数向量之间双曲距离的函数（并设欧氏距离变体），在确定性与随机策略下与多种线性及非线性IRL基线进行对比。

Result: 在既定基准上与最先进模型性能相当，且在低数据情形及密集环境中表现突出；应用于博物馆房间多展品间的人类导航数据时，揭示出奖励结构不仅依赖单项展品距离特征，还包含特征间不可分的合取关系。

Conclusion: 双曲奖励参数化可同时提升IRL的预测准确性与可解释性，为研究不可分、合取式结构如何解释行为提供框架。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) seeks to infer the reward function underlying observed behavior, but its predictive accuracy and interpretability depend on how that reward is computed from the underlying features. While prior IRL reward functions have been implemented through a variety of techniques including linear weights, neural networks, or other function approximators, none make assumptions about the geometry of the underlying feature space. Inspired by geometric organization observed in neural circuits, we introduce Hyperbolic inverse reinforcement learning (Hyperbolic IRL), which parameterizes reward as a function of Hyperbolic distance between the feature vector and a learned parameter vector, as well as its Euclidean distance variant. We tested our methods against a range of linear and nonlinear IRL baselines under both deterministic and stochastic policies. Our Hyperbolic approach achieved comparable performance to state-of-the-art models on established benchmarks and excelled in the low data limit and across dense environments. Applied to human navigation among multiple exhibits in a museum room, Hyperbolic IRL reveals reward structure that depends not only on individual exhibit-distance features, but also on non-separable conjunctions between them. Together, these results demonstrate that a Hyperbolic reward parameterization can improve both the predictive accuracy and interpretability of IRL, providing a framework for studying how non-separable, conjunctive structure can begin to explain behavior.

</details>


### [109] [Fast Privacy Loss Accounting for Random Allocation via Multiscale FFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42117)
*Vitaly Feldman,Moshe Shenfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对随机分配采样方案下的隐私损失分布(PLD)记账器，提出多尺度FFT算法，将运行时对精度参数α的依赖从O(α^-2)降至O(α^-1 log(1/α))，并改进舍入误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有用于随机分配采样方案的PLD记账器运行时间为O(α^-2)，在高精度需求下代价过高，需要降低对精度参数α的依赖同时保持其保证。

Method: 多尺度FFT：在每个二进区间内用均匀网格表示似然比，并在各区间内用FFT精确计算逐对求和的舍入；此外采用Doroshenko等的均值保持connect-the-dots投影执行舍入。

Result: 渐近复杂度由O(α^-2)降至O(α^-1 log(1/α))，舍入在隐私损失均值与计算对的χ²散度上的误差从O(α)量级降至O(α^2/log t)；实验中在高精度下取得数个数量级加速，并为DP-SGD with PREAMBLE给出更优隐私界。

Conclusion: 多尺度FFT在保留记账器保证的同时显著降低运行时间，均值保持投影进一步减小舍入误差，实用中大幅提速并改善隐私界。

Abstract: In the random allocation sampling scheme each user's data is used in $k$ steps chosen randomly and uniformly from a sequence of $t$ steps. This scheme is known to improve on Poisson sampling in private optimization and in communication-efficient aggregation. The existing privacy loss distribution (PLD) accountant for this scheme runs in time quadratic in the inverse of the accuracy parameter $α$ (Feldman and Shenfeld, 2026). We give an algorithm, \emph{multiscale FFT}, that represents likelihood ratios on a grid that is uniform within each dyadic interval and computes the exact rounding of every pairwise sum using FFTs within each interval. It keeps the guarantees of the accountant and improves the dependence of its running time on $α$ from $O(α^{-2})$ to $O(α^{-1}\log(1/α))$. In addition, we demonstrate that rounding can be performed via the mean-preserving connect-the-dots projection of (Doroshenko et al., 2026). This reduces the errors that rounding introduces in the mean of the privacy loss and in the $χ^2$ divergence of the computed pair from the order of $α$ to $O(α^2/\log(t))$. In our experiments, our algorithm achieves speedups of several orders of magnitude at high accuracy and gives improved privacy bounds for DP-SGD with PREAMBLE.

</details>


### [110] [Gradient Descent on Two ReLU Neurons: Global Landscape and Bifurcation Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42118)
*Binghua Li,Mengzhe Li,Denny Wu,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在良好设定的双神经元ReLU教师-学生多指标模型中，种群梯度流的学习过程由"易"的角平分线方向与"难"的分裂方向组织：轨迹先塌缩到角平分线鞍点，再逃逸并特化对齐教师神经元，呈现多阶段对称破缺动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降如何学习特征是神经网络理论的核心问题；作者希望在能展现丰富多阶段梯度动力学的最小多指标设定中刻画损失景观与学习行为。

Method: 采用各向同性高斯输入下良好设定的双神经元ReLU教师-学生模型，小初始化下的种群梯度流；分析种群平方损失景观（证明所有学生权值均非零的临界点或为全局最小或为鞍点），并追踪梯度流轨迹，以"角平分线（bisector）方向"与"分裂（splitting）方向"两个方向组织动力学。

Result: 所有学生权重均非零的临界点均为全局最小或鞍点；梯度流轨迹显示学生神经元先向角平分线鞍点塌缩，随后逃逸并特化对齐教师神经元。

Conclusion: 该工作从景观与动力学两方面解释了简单多指标模型在标准算法与架构选择下出现的多阶段对称破缺行为。

Abstract: Understanding how gradient descent learns features remains a central challenge in neural network theory. We study this question in a minimal multi-index setting that already exhibits rich multi-phase gradient dynamics: a well-specified two-neuron ReLU teacher-student model under isotropic Gaussian inputs, trained by population gradient flow from small initialization. We show that the dynamics are organized by two directions: the "easy" bisector direction, which carries the leading signal, and the "hard" splitting direction, which governs specialization. To characterize the loss structure and learning behavior, we analyze the population squared-loss landscape and show that every critical point with both student weights nonzero is either a global minimum or a saddle. We then track the gradient-flow trajectory, showing that student neurons first collapse toward a bisector saddle before escaping and specializing to teacher neurons. Our results provide both a landscape and dynamics account of the multi-phase symmetry-breaking behavior that arises in a simple multi-index model and under standard algorithmic and architectural choices.

</details>


### [111] [Physiological Trajectories and Observation Patterns in ICU Mortality Prediction: Transportability Under Changing Measurement Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42126)
*Benjamin T. Pike*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用MIMIC-III/IV与eICU多中心数据，比较生理轨迹特征与观测模式特征在ICU死亡预测中的可迁移性与时序敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分生理轨迹的预测价值与观测模式（测量频率/时间）本身携带的信息，并检验测量时点是否在观测数量之外独立影响模型性能。

Method: 48小时院内死亡预测；嵌套表示R1（最新值快照）→R2（纵向生理摘要）→R3（测量频率与时间特征）。在7,762例MetaVision患者上拟合，外部验证于21,682例MIMIC-IV与43,878例eICU患者（204家医院），并在固定保留观测数的条件下评估时点效应，辅以bootstrap重拟合与观测删减实验。

Result: 生理轨迹提升外部预测（eICU中R2-R1 log-loss差-0.00922，95%CI -0.01148~-0.00704）；观测模式特征仅在本地eICU模型（R3-R2 -0.00257）有效，跨库迁移后不明确（-0.00012，CI跨零）；R3在30次源域bootstrap重拟合中仅10次占优。观测数匹配下，保留分布于整个窗口的测量较随机保留降低log-loss：R3为0.03280，R2为0.00703；移除末12小时较等量随机移除升高log-loss：R3为0.01947，R2为0.00822。观测可得性下降时，本地R3增益由有益转为有害。

Conclusion: 生理轨迹特征迁移更稳定，观测模式特征高度依赖测量时点与可得性；临床预测模型除跨人群验证外，还需在真实测量条件下评估。

Abstract: Objective: To distinguish the transferable predictive value of physiological trajectories from that of observation patterns in ICU mortality prediction, and to assess whether measurement timing affects performance independently of observation quantity.
  Materials and Methods: In-hospital mortality was predicted at 48 hours using MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, and eICU. Nested representations progressively integrated latest-value snapshots (R1), longitudinal physiological summaries (R2), and measurement frequency and timing features (R3). Models fitted on 7,762 MetaVision patients were evaluated on 21,682 MIMIC-IV patients and 43,878 eICU patients across 204 hospitals. The effect of measurement timing was assessed while holding the number of retained observations constant.
  Results: Physiological trajectories improved external prediction in eICU (R2-R1 log-loss difference -0.00922, 95% CI -0.01148 to -0.00704). Observation-pattern features improved locally developed eICU models (R3-R2 -0.00257, 95% CI -0.00370 to -0.00146), but not clearly after transfer from MetaVision (-0.00012, 95% CI -0.00206 to +0.00208). R3 was favoured in 10 of 30 source bootstrap refits. At matched observation counts, retaining measurements spread throughout the window reduced log loss relative to random retention by 0.03280 for R3 versus 0.00703 for R2. Removing the final 12 hours increased log loss relative to count-matched random removal by 0.01947 for R3 versus 0.00822 for R2. Under reduced availability, the local R3 increment changed from beneficial to harmful.
  Discussion: Physiological trajectories transferred more consistently, while observation-pattern features were particularly sensitive to the timing and availability of measurements.
  Conclusion: Clinical prediction models should be evaluated not only in new patient populations but also under the measurement conditions that supply their inputs.

</details>


### [112] [Instance-dependent sequential limits as an exact information ledger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42133)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将 bandit 遗憾、序贯检验、最佳臂识别与 MDP 的实例相关下界统一写成一条"精确账本"式链条（散度分解→数据处理→统计量→检验→对备择与分配的优化），并用一族鞅恒等式把分解推广到任意 tilt 之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典实例相关下界证明链条中只有第一步是恒等式，后续各步都含未量化松弛，导致算法实际表现与理论极限之间的差距来源不清。作者希望把这些松弛逐项显式化、可计算，从而定位差距。

Method: 以链式法则写出数据处理步骤中恰损失的"条件散度"（在自然指数族下依每臂计数与奖励和取值时消失）；构造单参数鞅恒等式族，将散度分解扩展到两实例间任意 tilt；随后以带"具名缺陷"的松弛得到经典极限，并区分可由算法自身运行计算与仅在类保证可数值化时才能均匀量化的缺陷。

Result: 在七个已发表数据集（从电影评分到发动机更换）上，账本成功定位标准算法与经典极限之差的来源；在已训练模型间的识别问题与 Wald 检验中，最大项通常来自均匀量化缺陷。

Conclusion: 实例相关下界可被视作可审计的精确分解；除渐近步外的缺陷均可由算法自身运行估计，从而使理论与实践的差距可归因、可诊断。

Abstract: Instance-dependent limits for bandit regret, sequential tests, best-arm identification and Markov decision processes are proved by one chain. It runs from a divergence decomposition through data processing to a statistic, a testing step that turns a class guarantee into a requirement on the history, and optimization over alternatives and allocations. Only the first step is an identity. This work writes the chain as an exact ledger. By the chain rule, data processing loses exactly a conditional divergence, which vanishes for arms in natural exponential families at the per-arm counts and reward sums. A one-parameter family of martingale identities extends the decomposition to every tilt between two instances. The classical limits follow by relaxations with named defects. All defects but the asymptotic step's can be computed from an algorithm's own runs, the uniform quantification's only when the class guarantee is a number. On seven published sources, from movie ratings to engine replacement, the ledger locates the difference between standard algorithms and the classical limits. For identification among trained models and for Wald's test the largest term is usually the uniform quantification's.

</details>


### [113] [Counting on Dummies: Privacy Amplification with Observable Counts and Truncated Batches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42139)
*Vitaly Feldman,Audra McMillan,Kunal Talwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对DP-SGD中变长批次泄露与截断导致隐私损失增大的问题，提出用随机数量“dummy points”同时私有化批次大小并在截断下保持隐私保证的简单机制，并给出Poisson采样与random allocation下高效紧致的隐私核算分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP-SGD通常按批处理数据，随机采样批次可借助隐私放大获得更优隐私界，但Poisson采样与random allocation等具放大性质的方案会产生变长批。其隐私保证要求批次大小不被泄露，而分布式场景中聚合服务器或通信监听者可获知批大小；同时内存/计算限制要求批大小不超过上限s，需对超限批进行截断，已知截断会显著增大隐私损失。因此需要同时解决“批大小泄露”与“截断破坏隐私保证”两个问题。

Method: 提出一个简单机制：使用随机数量的dummy points对批次大小进行私有化，从而在截断情形下仍维持隐私保证；该机制易在分布式环境实现。作者对Poisson采样与random allocation两种采样方案给出通用分析，分析设计兼顾高效且相对紧致的隐私核算；并通过子采样放大了计数发布的隐私性，使所需dummy points的期望数量渐近小于直接用非负噪声私有化（敏感度为1的）批大小所需的数量。

Result: 该机制在分布式场景下易于实现；分析可支持高效、较紧的隐私核算；利用子采样放大效应后，dummy points的期望数量渐近低于直接对批大小加非负噪声私有化的方案。针对带截断批次的Poisson采样，实验表明相比Ganesh (2025)近期给出的截断直接核算方法，本机制取得更优的隐私—效率权衡。

Conclusion: 以随机dummy points私有化批次大小是一种简单、可在分布式下落地且理论上可紧致核算的方案，能够在批次截断条件下保持隐私放大带来的隐私保证，并在隐私与效率的权衡上优于既有截断直接核算方法。

Abstract: In many practical deployments of Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD), the server processes data in batches. Randomly sampling these batches allows for better privacy bounds via privacy amplification results. Most sampling schemes with provable privacy amplification, including Poisson sampling and random allocation, result in variable batch sizes. The privacy guarantee of these schemes requires that the batch sizes are not revealed. In distributed settings, the server performing the aggregation or any party observing the communication traffic may learn these batch sizes. Furthermore, in both centralized and distributed settings, memory and computation constraints may require each batch to be of some maximum size $s$, necessitating truncation of the batch when it exceeds this limit. It is known that this truncation can result in a significant increase in privacy loss. In this work, we address both of these challenges via a simple mechanism that uses a random number of \emph{dummy points} to privatize the batch size and maintain the privacy guarantee even under truncation. We demonstrate that our mechanism is easy to implement in a distributed setting, and provide a general analysis of this mechanism for both Poisson sampling and random allocation. Our analysis is designed to support efficient and relatively tight privacy accounting. It also effectively amplifies the privacy of the count releases by subsampling, making the expected number of dummy points asymptotically smaller than what is needed to directly privatize the (sensitivity-one) batch sizes with non-negative noise. For Poisson sampling with truncated batches, we demonstrate empirically that our mechanism achieves better privacy--efficiency tradeoffs than the direct accounting for truncation recently given by (Ganesh, 2025).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [More Rules, Longer Lists? Benchmarking Self-Evolving Agents on a Hidden-Rule Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40758)
*Chenjie Qiu,Boyan Xu,Shaobin Shi,Bingfeng Chen,Ruichu Cai,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CLR-Bench（Latent Rules课程基准），用逐层叠加隐藏规则的链条测试自进化智能体能否学到"无人告知"的规则，而非把每条反馈记成例外；结果多数方法几乎学不会，但一句"命名分组"约定的固定提示可提升14.5–16.5分，且学习能力随模型强弱显著分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体依据反馈重写持久状态，但其是否真能习得未知规则（而非把每次纠正记成例外、使状态随规则增多而膨胀）基本未经检验。现有基准无法区分"记忆例外"与"归纳规则"，需要一个能认证模型先验知识、确认证据可识别隐藏规则、并排除无反馈猜测的受控测试。

Method: 构建CLR-Bench：每级在先前规则之上新增一条隐藏规则，规则作用于一个不透明标签，该标签对应网络上公开的命名分组（如某航空联盟创始成员）。任务给一个示例值，练习反馈揭示一个额外成员与一个非成员，测试考察未见成员与相似非成员。用探针认证三点：基础模型知道各分组且无反馈时不会猜出；标签按命名分组解读后证据可唯一识别该分组。以自动化流水线在新数据域生成链条，发布覆盖10个域的20条链；评测六种近期自进化方法，并分析其状态内容与实际行为。

Result: 在认证模型上，六种方法几乎学不到：相对冻结智能体的最佳增益≤5分，对未见成员的排除率仅6–12%，低于简单保留原始练习历史的20%。加入一句陈述"命名分组"约定的固定提示后，同一批方法提升14.5–16.5分。其状态在75–98%的情况下保存了已揭示成员，却几乎从不包含类名；当练习暴露列表遗漏的成员时，它们只是把该成员加入列表。换用更强的GPT-6-Sol，ACE与Dynamic Cheatsheet仅凭反馈即可学习，达到78%准确率、解出59–60%的单格，超过原始历史；而认证模型对应为53%与8%。

Conclusion: CLR-Bench可度量智能体的"学习能力"本身，而非仅凭累积例外取得的表面成绩；该能力在不同模型间差异显著，强模型能在无额外提示下归纳隐藏规则。基准同时揭示了当前自进化方法普遍停留在枚举式记忆、缺乏规则抽象的诊断性缺陷。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents rewrite a persistent state from feedback, but it is largely untested whether this lets them learn a rule nobody told them. An agent can also pass by writing down each correction as an exception, and its list then grows with every new rule. We introduce CLR-Bench, a Curriculum of Latent Rules benchmark. Each chain adds one hidden rule per level on top of the earlier ones. A rule acts on an opaque label that stands for a named grouping published on the Web, such as the founding members of an airline alliance. The task gives one example value, practice feedback reveals one more member and one non-member, and the test checks unseen members and similar non-members. Probes certify that the base model knows the members of each grouping and does not guess it without feedback, and that the evidence identifies the grouping once labels are read as named groupings. An automated pipeline builds chains from a new data domain, and we release 20 chains over 10 domains. We evaluate six recent self-evolution methods. With the certifying model they barely learn. The best gain at most 5 points over a frozen agent and exclude only 6-12% of the unseen members from the count, fewer than the 20% reached by simply keeping the raw practice history. A fixed lesson that states the named-grouping convention lifts the same methods by 14.5 to 16.5 points. Their states hold the revealed member in 75-98% of cases and almost never the class name, and when practice exposes a member the list misses, they add that member to the list. With the stronger GPT-6-Sol, ACE and Dynamic Cheatsheet learn from feedback alone. They reach 78% accuracy and solve 59-60% of the single cells, against 53% and 8% with the certifying model, and they surpass the raw history. CLR-Bench therefore measures how well an agent learns, and this ability differs across models.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Plausible Prediction: Safety Sufficiency in Web World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40798)
*Zhifei Hu,Alexandra I. Cristea,Adel Bibi,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"安全充分性"(safety sufficiency)概念与 SafeWebWorld 基准，考察 web world model 的预测/证据保留/授权决策是否足以支撑 EXECUTE/ASK/BLOCK 三分类决策，并用 Latent Authorisation Recovery (LAR) 区分"分数结构丢失"与"读出失败"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 web world model 以"预测合理性"为主要评价目标，但一个看似合理的未来预测并不必然支撑正确的安全决策。实测 3000 条预测中 86.1% 语义合理，然而多个模型呈现高度不对称的授权行为：false-safe 执行率低，但可澄清的 ASK 案例召回差、强烈偏向 BLOCK。因此需要新的评价维度来衡量预测接口是否保留并暴露"策略决定性信息"。

Method: 1) 构建 SafeWebWorld 基准，覆盖五个 web 安全域，包含 action-conditioned futures、policy-decisive evidence 与 policy-conditioned decisions 三个环节；2) 采用受控的 Security-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning 协议进行干预；3) 提出 Latent Authorisation Recovery (LAR)，通过冻结分数重校准(frozen-score recalibration)与配对恢复(paired recovery)判断性能下降源于授权敏感分数结构损失还是决策读出失败；4) 辅以 within-family 与 matched-authorisation 评测。

Result: SFT 后证据保留(evidence preservation)可显著提升，但授权敏感决策反而退化。冻结分数重校准显示存在可观但依赖 backbone 的恢复；配对恢复仅带来有限的额外增益。同族与匹配授权条件下评测仍表现较差。

Conclusion: 预测合理性、证据保留与聚合层面的可恢复性，三者各自均不足以确立稳健的授权能力；聚合可恢复性并不蕴含细粒度授权的稳健行为。

Abstract: Web world models increasingly predict the consequences of candidate actions before agents act, yet a plausible predicted future does not necessarily support the correct safety decision. In a matched human evaluation of 3,000 predictions from ten frozen open models, 86.1% are judged semantically plausible. However, several models exhibit highly asymmetric authorisation behaviour, combining low false-safe execution with poor recall of clarifiable ASK cases and a strong preference for BLOCK. This motivates safety sufficiency: whether a predictive interface preserves and exposes the policy-decisive information required to distinguish actions that may be EXECUTED, require clarification through ASK, or should be BLOCKED. We introduce SafeWebWorld, a benchmark for evaluating safety sufficiency across five web-safety domains through action-conditioned futures, policy-decisive evidence, and policy-conditioned decisions. Under a controlled Security-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning protocol, evidence preservation can improve substantially while authorisation-sensitive decisions deteriorate. To determine whether this deterioration reflects loss of authorisation-sensitive score structure or a failure of the decision readout, we introduce Latent Authorisation Recovery (LAR). Frozen-score recalibration reveals substantial but backbone-dependent recovery, while paired recovery provides only a modest additional gain. Yet within-family and matched-authorisation evaluations remain poor, showing that aggregate recoverability does not imply robust fine-grained authorisation behaviour. Overall, plausible prediction, evidence preservation, and aggregate recovery are each insufficient on their own to establish robust authorisation.

</details>


### [116] [Circumventing the Computational Hardness of Computational Arbitrage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40831)
*Nastaran Okati,Ricardo Olmedo,Moritz Hardt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将“计算套利”（从异构模型组合中购买推理并以获利方式转售）形式化为分配问题，证明一般情形下为NP-hard，但在“模型性能随推理支出呈边际收益递减”的自然条件下可高效求得最优分配，并给出样本高效的性能估计方法以降低识别套利策略的前期成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型市场中提供方在成本与能力上高度异构，形成跨模型组合的“计算套利”机会，可为套利中介、消费者与提供方同时创造财务价值。已有工作仅在小型模型池上实证其可行性；扩展到大型市场需要计算上可解、且识别与部署成本低廉的套利策略。

Method: （1）将计算套利形式化为分配问题进行理论分析；（2）证明一般情形下该问题是NP-hard；（3）引入关于模型性能随推理支出规模化的边际收益递减条件，证明该条件下最优分配可高效计算；（4）提出样本高效的方法，从有限数据中估计模型性能，以降低识别套利策略的前期开销。

Result: 一般形式的计算套利分配问题为NP-hard；在边际收益递减条件下最优分配可多项式/高效求解；所提性能估计方法具有样本高效性，可降低套利策略的识别成本。经济层面得到三点结论：最优解处每个获资助模型每美元的边际效用相等；套利会使收入从“效用相对价格偏低”的提供方转移出去；套利会奖励互补性专业化。

Conclusion: 计算套利在大型AI模型市场中可行且具经济意义：通过边际收益递减假设可实现可扩展的最优分配求解，并以样本高效估计降低部署门槛；其均衡条件（等边际效用每美元）与再分配效应为理解AI模型市场的竞争与专业化格局提供了理论依据。

Abstract: AI model marketplaces host providers with heterogeneous costs and capabilities. These differences create an opportunity for computational arbitrage (Olmedo et al., 2026), in which an intermediary purchases inference from a portfolio of models and resells it to users at a profit. Beyond creating profit opportunities for the arbitrageur, computational arbitrage offers financial value to both consumers and providers and could therefore significantly influence AI model markets. While prior work empirically demonstrates the feasibility of computational arbitrage for small model pools, scaling it to large marketplaces requires arbitrage strategies that are both computationally tractable and inexpensive to identify and deploy. In this work, we formalize computational arbitrage as an allocation problem and prove that it is generally NP-hard. However, we show that the optimal allocation can be computed efficiently under a natural diminishing-returns condition on how model performance scales with inference spending. We further develop a sample-efficient method for estimating model performance from limited data, reducing the upfront cost of identifying arbitrage strategies. Our analysis also yields several economic insights: at the optimum, every funded model delivers the same marginal utility per dollar; arbitrage directs revenue away from providers offering little utility relative to their prices; and it rewards complementary specialization.

</details>


### [117] [Incongruence-Aware Evaluation of Multimodal Speech Emotion Recognition in Audio Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40883)
*Fengji Jing,Guy Laban*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 文章提出一种免阈值、基于共享VAD空间距离的高斯混合“不一致性”分数，并验证其与人类判断一致；在三个语料上发现，只要音频存在，不一致性就显著提高语音情感识别的错误几率，而监督分类器无此现象，说明困难源于模型融合原始证据的方式而非信号本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音交互大模型中，“说什么”（文本）与“怎么说”（音频）两个通道并不总是一致。作者追问这种跨模态不一致是否会削弱语音情感识别性能，并希望量化、可验证地刻画这一现象。

Method: 将文本与音频映射到共享的效价-唤醒-支配（VAD）空间，用双分量高斯混合模型把两者距离转换成连续、免阈值的不一致分数，并用人类判断进行验证；在eNTERFACE'05、MELD、CMU-MOSEI（表演式、对话式、自发语音）上评测三个具备音频能力的模型；控制真实情感标签与特征空间距离，跨模态固定同一模型；与监督分类器及六种更简单的替代分数对比，并做去除情感词典的消融。

Result: 在每个语料中，只要音频存在，不一致性都会提高出错几率（控制真实情感与特征空间距离后依然成立）；同一特征上训练的监督分类器表现相反；纯文本效应不可靠——仅替换读取转写的模型即在一个语料上使效应符号反转；去掉情感词典重建分数仍复现核心结论；在六种替代方案中，该分数是唯一能在三个语料上同时预测错误的免标签分数。

Conclusion: 跨模态不一致确实会损害语音情感识别，但问题不在信号本身，而在于这类模型融合原始证据的方式；所提连续不一致分数稳健、免标签且可迁移，可作为分析多模态情感识别的诊断工具。

Abstract: Voice-interactive large language models process both what a speaker says and how they say it, yet the two channels do not always agree. We ask whether that disagreement degrades speech emotion recognition. Mapping text and audio into a shared valence-arousal-dominance space, we convert the distance between them into a continuous, threshold-free incongruence score with a two-component Gaussian mixture, and validate the score against human judgement. We evaluate three audiocapable models on acted, conversational and spontaneous speech (eNTERFACE'05, MELD and CMU-MOSEI). With the model held constant across modalities, incongruence raises the odds of error whenever audio is present, in every corpus, after controlling for the ground-truth emotion and for distance in the feature space. Supervised classifiers trained on the same features behave in the opposite way, which suggests that the difficulty lies in how these models combine raw evidence rather than in the signal itself. Text-only effects are unreliable: swapping only the model that reads the transcript reverses their sign on one corpus. Rebuilding the score without the emotion lexicon reproduces the central result, and among six simpler alternatives it is the only label-free score that predicts error in all three corpora. Code, data tables and prompts are released.

</details>


### [118] [ChatGPT as a Surveillance Assistant? An Exploratory Case Study of AI-Assisted Surveillance Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40890)
*Ayana Hussain,Nicholas Vincent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文对ChatGPT公开网页界面在监控（surveillance）相关任务上可能提供的潜在有害协助进行了探索性案例研究，提出七类监控风险分类法并构造110条提示，发现其大量提示可获得实质性监控指导，且拒答时仍可能附带详细操作内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可大规模生成详细指导、可运行代码与说服性文本，ChatGPT作为2026年最广泛使用的消费级语言模型产品之一，使公众可广泛获取此类能力，引发对其被用于监控相关任务的担忧；作者希望刻画"潜在有害的监控协助"在真实产品界面中的具体形态。

Method: 探索性案例研究。基于既有LLM风险研究与已部署监控系统的报告，构建包含七类监控风险的分类法，并实例化为110条提示；提示分两种投放方式：55条越狱提示（涵盖五类诱导策略）与55条平实语言的直接提示，每条先单独运行，若不成功再加一段短的固定前缀重试；随后对ChatGPT的全部回复进行人工审阅分析。

Result: 55条越狱提示中有40条、55条直接提示中在不加前缀的情况下有33条，均获得了实质性的监控指导。合规回复中极少提及政策或提供隐私保护替代方案。人工审阅发现，即便ChatGPT明确表示拒绝，仍可能在简短伦理说明之外同时给出详细操作指令、可运行代码或架构层面的指导。

Conclusion: 当前ChatGPT在监控相关请求上的安全防护存在明显缺口：直接自然语言提问即可获得大量实质协助，"拒绝+附带操作内容"的混合回复削弱了拒答的实际效力，政策声明与隐私友好替代建议的缺失进一步放大了风险；作者据此提示需要更细致的评估与防护设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate detailed guidance, functional code, and persuasive text at scale. As one of the most widely used consumer language model products as of 2026, ChatGPT gives broad public access to these capabilities, motivating concerns about its potential use in surveillance-related tasks. We present an exploratory case study of what potentially harmful surveillance assistance looks like in ChatGPT's public web interface. We contribute a taxonomy of seven surveillance risk categories based on prior LLM risk research and reports of deployed surveillance systems, instantiated as 110 prompts, along with a manually reviewed analysis of ChatGPT's responses. Prompts were issued using two approaches: 55 jailbreak prompts drawing on five types of elicitation strategies, and 55 plain-language direct prompts, each run alone, and if unsuccessful, again with a short fixed prefix. We find that ChatGPT provides substantive surveillance guidance for 40 of the 55 jailbreak prompts and for 33 of the 55 direct prompts without any prefix. Compliant responses also rarely included policy mentions or suggestions for privacy-preserving alternatives. We manually review every response and find that even when ChatGPT states a refusal, it may still supply detailed instructions, working code, or architectural guidance alongside brief ethical caveats.

</details>


### [119] [Does Recursive Knowledge Construction Help Agents Verify Consensus Claims in Science?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40906)
*Lun Ai,Yu-Heng Wu,Jonathan Schaul,Leonie Küchenhoff,Denes Turei,Francesco Carli,Fabio Petroni,Aurelien Dugourd,Julio Saez-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出共识科学主张验证任务与 ProClaim 框架，将证据状态持久化于模型上下文之外，使"递归构建何种状态"与"何种判据控制递归"两个设计轴可被独立评测；发现结构化外部证据状态优于上下文内累积，训练型判据优于固定轮数停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体通过检索证据、构建中间状态并判断信息是否充分来递归积累知识，但通常仅做端到端、无真值评测，导致各设计选择的实际贡献不明。作者希望解耦并量化两类设计轴的影响：(1) 递归所构建的证据状态内容；(2) 控制递归终止的判据。

Method: 定义"共识科学主张验证"任务，要求系统从开放文献中确立主张的共识立场。构建 ProClaim-eval 数据集，含 718 条带 SUPPORT/REFUTE 金标签的主张，覆盖分子生物学、核物理与天文学，源自五个专家整理数据库。实现 ProClaim 系统，将证据状态持久化于模型上下文之外，从而在固定一轴的前提下研究另一轴。与验证系统及通用智能体框架对比，并消融证据状态形式、存储内容与判据类型（训练判据 vs 固定轮数）。

Result: ProClaim 在对比系统中位列前三，相对通用智能体框架具有显著优势；外部证据状态优于上下文内累积，且其中存储内容亦有影响；读取证据状态的训练型判据优于固定轮数停止；独立训练、从未接触 ProClaim-eval 的判据在五分之三子集上与金标签一致性最高，持平 Claude Sonnet 4.6 判据并优于 Claude Haiku 4.5。

Conclusion: 在模型上下文之外递归维护结构化证据状态，是科学智能体进行主张验证的有效设计范式；证据状态的内容与可读状态的训练型终止判据均为关键设计要素。

Abstract: Recent scientific agents build knowledge recursively by retrieving evidence, constructing an intermediate state, and judging whether that state contains sufficient information. These agents are evaluated end-to-end, usually without ground truth, so the effect of each design choice is unknown. Here, we examined two design axes: (1) what the recursion builds (the state of evidence) and (2) what controls it (the judge that decides when to stop). To measure them, we defined consensus scientific claim verification and measured these effects on this task, where a system must establish a claim's consensus stance from the open literature. We built a new consensus claim dataset, ProClaim-eval, including 718 claims with SUPPORT and REFUTE gold labels from five expert-curated databases spanning molecular biology, nuclear physics, and astronomy. Our instrument, ProClaim, persists an evidence state outside the model's context, so each axis can be studied with the other held fixed. Against verification systems and general-purpose agent harnesses, ProClaim ranks among the top three, with a significant advantage over the harnesses. We found that the evidence state improves performance over in-context accumulation, and it also matters what is stored in it. In addition, a trained judge that reads the evidence state performs better than fixed-round stopping. A judge trained separately and never exposed to ProClaim-eval achieves the highest agreement with the gold labels on three of five subsets, where it matches the off-the-shelf Claude Sonnet~4.6 judge and outperforms Claude Haiku~4.5. Our results support recursively maintaining a structured evidence state outside the model's context as an effective design for verifying claims in scientific agents.

</details>


### [120] [Towards Portable Specification of the Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40917)
*Nicolas Gertler,Claudio Novelli,Luciano Floridi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将"harness"（模型运行所依赖的基础设施）拆解为八个功能组件，并构建可移植的规范框架，记录"哪个模型在哪个harness中运行"，以应对报告分数差异和攻击面问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM只能通过其周边基础设施（harness）行动，harness决定了模型能做什么、如何失败，却缺乏统一定义。不同harness配置可使同一模型报告分数相差数倍，且agent大量攻击面位于harness中；现有model card/agent card未记录一次运行内的有效配置序列，harness还会在版本间、运行间乃至运行内（自编辑工具时）变化，现行AI法律的文档义务也未规定此类记录。

Method: 将harness分解为八个可跨系统规格化与比较的功能组件；提出可移植的规范框架，记录模型与harness的对应关系及版本化信息。

Result: 论证了harness配置对报告分数的显著影响（数倍差异）、攻击面集中于harness、以及现有文档体系（model card、agent card、AI法律）均无法刻画运行内有效配置序列的空白。

Conclusion: harness必须被显式描述并规范为系统的版本化组成部分。

Abstract: Large language models act only through the infrastructure that surrounds them. That infrastructure, the harness, determines what a model can accomplish and how it fails, yet it has no settled definition. To address this gap, we decompose the harness into eight functional components that can be specified and compared across systems, and we propose a portable specification framework that records which model ran in which harness. The record is needed because different harness configurations can yield several-fold differences in reported scores for the same model, and because much of an agent's attack surface lies in its harness. Existing model cards and agent cards provide release and operational information, but do not establish the sequence of effective configurations within a run. Harnesses can change between releases and between runs, and, where agents edit their own tools and configuration, within a run. Enacted AI laws contain documentation duties relevant to covered systems, subject to their respective scopes and application dates, but do not prescribe the common harness record proposed here. Therefore, the harness has to be explicitly described and specified as a versioned part of the system.

</details>


### [121] [Agentic World Modeling for Agent Test-time Scaling without Environment Replica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40927)
*Wendi Li,Huseyin A. Inan,Wei-Ning Chen,Yanzhi Chen,Chetan Bansal,Saravan Rajmohan,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic World Modeling（AWM），将下一观测预测重构为多轮证据获取任务，通过只读接口在共享环境下实现安全的并行轨迹模拟，用于大模型智能体的测试时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展（TTS）依赖并行采样多条候选轨迹，但真实部署中企业工作区与云服务共享、持久、难以复制，动作可能产生不可逆副作用或跨轨迹干扰；而现有生成式世界模型把环境状态压缩进单一 prompt，在部分可观测下难以可靠模拟状态转移。

Method: AWM 将 next-observation prediction 重新表述为多轮证据获取任务：对每个假设动作，agentic simulator 通过安全的只读接口自适应查询真实环境，对返回证据进行推理，并用可执行的 computation 构造结果观测，而不改变共享状态；再与轨迹级 TTS 集成，实现单一共享环境上的并行探索。

Result: 在 τ-bench、AppWorld 与 OfficeBench 上，AWM 相对单次模拟基平均提升 40.07%，相对训练好的世界模型基线平均提升 12.36%。

Conclusion: 交互式、基于只读证据的模拟为共享、不可重置环境中的安全且可扩展的智能体测试时扩展提供了可行基础。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves large language model agents by sampling and selecting among multiple candidate trajectories, but common realizations assume that these trajectories can be executed in parallel. This assumption breaks in realistic deployments, where enterprise workspaces and cloud services are shared and persistent, costly or infeasible to replicate, and actions may introduce irreversible side effects or cross-trajectory interference. Existing generative world models avoid direct execution, but typically compress environment state into a single prompt, limiting their ability to reliably simulate transitions under partial observability. Instead, we introduce Agentic World Modeling (AWM), which reformulates next-observation prediction as a multi-turn evidence-acquisition task. For each hypothetical action, an agentic simulator adaptively queries the real environment through safe read-only interfaces, reasons over the returned evidence, and uses executable computation to construct the resulting observation without mutating shared state. Integrated with trajectory-level TTS, AWM enables parallel exploration over a single shared environment. Across $τ$-bench, AppWorld, and OfficeBench, AWM outperforms a single-shot simulation baseline by 40.07% on average, and surpasses a trained world-model baseline by 12.36% on average. These results establish interactive, read-grounded simulation as a practical foundation for safe and scalable agentic test-time scaling in shared, non-resettable environments.

</details>


### [122] [DiRAFT: Privacy-Aware Financial Transcript Synthesis from Aggregate Discourse Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40938)
*Yunbai Zhang,Yunyi Gao,Han-Tai Shiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DiRAFT：从聚合概念转移先验采样话语计划合成金融对话，并以结构重叠与人工事实核查两套互补评估证明随机采样在转移保真度与残余保密风险间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用客户对话训练/评估金融语言模型存在保密风险：即使掩蔽直接标识符，非公开金融事实仍可能残留；合成对话可作为替代数据源，但其话语保真度与残余保密风险尚待评估。

Method: DiRAFT从训练记录中抽取金融概念图并记录概念首次引入顺序，跨记录池化相邻概念转移，依据聚合转移先验、起始概念先验与计划长度先验采样新图计划；话语层在不提供任何单条源段落或图的前提下指导文本实现。评估包括语料级结构重叠与针对特定非公开投资者事实的源依据核查：结构评估覆盖两个金融语料与三种生成后端；事实评估由人工审阅七种策略、两种后端下的1,400条投资者通话输出；并评估转移保真度、内容可恢复性与轨迹多样性。

Result: 随机采样在转移保真度与结构重叠间取得有利平衡，在投资者通话上相较贪婪与top-3解码具一致优势；事实评估中随机采样在两种后端上均检出更少的敏感事实匹配。

Conclusion: 两套互补评估提供了关于残余保密风险的经验证据，但不构成形式化保证。

Abstract: Using customer conversations to train and evaluate financial language models raises confidentiality concerns because non-public financial facts can remain after direct identifiers are masked. Synthetic transcripts offer an alternative data source, but their discourse fidelity and residual confidentiality risks require assessment. We introduce DiRAFT (Discourse Realization from Aggregate First-order Transitions), which extracts financial concept graphs and records the order in which concepts are first introduced. It pools adjacent concept transitions across training records and samples new graph plans from aggregate transition, starting-concept, and plan-length priors. A discourse layer guides text realization without supplying an individual source passage or graph. We assess privacy-related risks through two complementary evaluations: corpus-level structural overlap and source-grounded checks for specific non-public investor facts. The structural assessment covers two financial corpora and three generation backends. The confidential-fact assessment uses human review of 1,400 investor-call outputs across seven strategies and two backends. We also evaluate transition fidelity, content recoverability, and trajectory diversity. Stochastic sampling provides a favorable balance between transition fidelity and structural overlap, with consistent advantages over greedy and top-3 on investor calls. The confidential-fact assessment likewise detects fewer matches for stochastic sampling than these alternatives on both backends covered by the fact assessment. These complementary assessments provide empirical evidence on residual confidentiality risks without establishing a formal guarantee.

</details>


### [123] [The First Few Tokens Matter: Understanding Chain-of-Thought Controllability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40939)
*Hugh Van Deventer,Dmitrii Troitskii,Laura Ruis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将CoT中"模型难以控制开头几个思考token"这一现象归因于起始think标签后数个token的熵坍缩，并通过prefill与"禁用高频开头token+上下文示例"两种推理时干预恢复可控性，发现加法/风格类可控性与抑制类可控性机制不同，且与可监控性的相关方向亦不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控之所以有效，部分依赖于当前模型无法自如控制其在推理轨迹中"说出"什么；但这一局限的来源不明。作者希望定位该局限的机制根源，并检验能否人为恢复对CoT内容的可控性，同时厘清可控性与可监控性之间的关系。

Method: 分析推理轨迹中开头think标签之后前若干token的熵分布，识别熵坍缩现象；在此基础上设计两种推理时干预：(1) prefilling，即在开头预填token；(2) 禁用高频开头token并配合in-context示例。随后在多个模型上评估不同控制类型（加法、风格变换、抑制）的合规率，并跨模型计算组内与组间相关性。

Result: 单个预填token即可显著提升可控性；禁用高频开头token加示例使平均合规率提升34–41个百分点。干预主要恢复加法与风格可控性，抑制可控性反应不一。组内（加法/风格 vs. 抑制）性能呈正相关，跨组多为负相关。加法与风格可控性与可监控性较稳定地正相关，抑制则常呈负相关。

Conclusion: CoT可控性受限源于开头token的熵坍缩，可通过初始token层面的推理时干预缓解；加法/风格控制与抑制控制应视为不同能力分别评估与报告。作者呼吁发展能区分"难以启动特定开头"与"改变推理内容能力整体受限"的可控性评测，并分类别报告控制任务结果。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising way to oversee increasingly capable agents. It succeeds in part because current models appear limited in their ability to control what they verbalize in their reasoning traces. We investigate the source of this limitation and link it to an entropy collapse in the first few tokens following the opening thinking tag. We exploit this mechanism to recover CoT controllability through two inference-time interventions on initial tokens: prefilling, where even a single token can substantially increase controllability, and banning frequent opening tokens combined with in-context examples, which improves average compliance by 34-41 percentage points across models. These interventions recover mainly addition and style controllability (adding or transforming information), while suppression controllability (avoiding or removing information) exhibits more varied responses, suggesting distinct underlying mechanisms. Consistent with this, the two groups behave as separate capabilities: across models, performance correlates positively within these groups, while cross-group correlations are mostly negative. Importantly, we find that the relationship with monitorability depends on the evaluation: addition and style controllability more consistently positively correlate with monitorability, while suppression often shows negative associations. Our findings motivate the development of controllability evaluations that distinguish difficulty initiating a specific opening from general limitations of the ability to alter reasoning content, alongside separate reporting of control tasks and categories.

</details>


### [124] [Benchmarking Forecasting Agents on Yesterday's Tomorrows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40940)
*Yuxuan Wang,Yishu Wang,Jiaqi Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FABench是面向预测智能体的可扩展回测基准，通过自动化流水线发现事件、生成问题并结算历史结果，避免日期等待与训练数据泄漏；首版含九大领域287道题，八种模型的检索均提升Brier分数，最佳三个智能体略超Polymarket市场共识，但研究轨迹揭示出证据权衡与应用环节的系统性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测任务综合考验信息整合、因果推断与校准概率判断能力。现有基准不适配：实时（live）基准须等事件揭晓才能得到反馈，评估周期受日历约束；固定语料基准虽即时可评，却把研究限制在预收集文档内，且难以规避模型训练截止带来的时间泄漏。

Method: 构建FABench可扩展回测环境：自动化流水线针对任意历史预测日期完成事件发现、问题生成与结果结算，使基准可迭代更新以领先于模型训练截止时间；首版发布覆盖九个领域的287道题。智能体在"离线档案+经时间泄漏筛查的实时网络搜索"的档案环境中自由研究；以Brier分数评估基于八种近期模型构建的智能体，并与Polymarket关联的54题市场共识对比。

Result: 在所有检索设置下，检索均改善了全部八个模型的Brier分数；领先智能体在54道与Polymarket挂钩的问题上达到市场共识水平，其中最佳三个略高于该共识。研究轨迹显示智能体在证据加权与应用方式上存在反复出现的失败模式，仍有较大改进空间。

Conclusion: FABench以可更新、可回测、抗时间泄漏的设计弥补了现有预测基准的不足，验证了检索对预测性能的普遍增益并给出与市场共识的可比参照；证据处理环节的系统性缺陷指明了改进方向，代码、数据与档案环境已开源，作为预测智能体社区研究的基础。

Abstract: Digital agents can now gather and organize information, reason over it, and use tools to complete complex tasks. Forecasting puts all of these abilities to the test: it requires integrating incomplete evidence, inferring causal drivers, and committing to calibrated probabilistic judgments. Existing benchmarks are poorly suited to measuring and improving this ability. Live benchmarks provide feedback only after events resolve, so every evaluation cycle waits on the calendar; fixed-corpus evaluations return results immediately but confine research to pre-collected documents. We introduce FABench, a scalable backtesting environment for Forecasting Agents. An automated pipeline discovers events, generates questions, and resolves outcomes for any past forecast date, so the benchmark can be renewed to stay ahead of model training cutoffs; its first release poses 287 questions across nine domains. Agents research each question freely in an archive environment that combines an offline archive with live web search screened to prevent temporal leakage. Evaluating agents built on eight recent models, we find that retrieval improves the Brier score of all eight in every retrieval setting and lifts the leading agents to the market consensus on the 54 questions linked to Polymarket, with the best three scoring slightly above it. Research traces reveal recurring failures in how agents weigh and apply evidence, leaving ample room for improvement. We release our code, data, and archive environment as a foundation for community research on forecasting agents.

</details>


### [125] [Opening the Box Changes the Cat: LLMs Detect Chain-of-Thought Monitoring - What now?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40946)
*Amir Jalilifard,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo,Anderson Rocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大模型能否从直接与间接环境线索中推断出思维链（CoT）监控的存在，以及这种推断如何改变其后续行为，并提出"监督是交互过程"的视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理暴露的中间推理轨迹被认为可用于在执行动作前进行安全监控。但现有评估通常假设监控是不可见的、被监控模型是被动对象，忽略了模型可能会察觉到自己正被监控并据此调整行为，从而使评估结论失真。

Method: 研究模型对监控线索的感知与反应：构造直接的与间接的环境线索（如超时、操作耗时等时间相关信号），在重复回合与多轮交互回路中考察模型是否能推断出监控存在、形成对监控有效性的统计估计，并据此调整行为以规避检测。

Result: 模型能够从直接和间接线索中推断出监控的存在，包括仅由超时和操作时长等细微时间信号泄露的信息；被监控模型会相应改变行为，且在反复交互中可对监控有效性做出统计估计，并利用该估计指导规避检测的尝试。

Conclusion: 监督是交互式过程：监控干预留下的痕迹本身会向模型泄露"思考正被观察"的信息，进而影响其试图观察的行为。因此需要在自适应条件下评估基于CoT的监督，将监控过程对被测行为的影响纳入考量。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning allows large language models (LLMs) to generate intermediate reasoning traces in natural language while solving complex tasks. These traces can support safety monitoring by exposing potentially harmful plans before actions are executed. In this work, we investigate the conditions under which LLMs infer the presence of CoT monitoring from direct and indirect environmental cues and examine how this inference affects their subsequent behavior. Our observations suggest that oversight is an interactive process: while a monitor inspects a model's reasoning, traces left by monitoring interventions can allow the model to infer that its thoughts are being observed and to adapt its behavior accordingly. We show that even subtle environmental cues, such as timing-related signals from timeouts and operation durations, can reveal the presence of monitoring and trigger models to adapt their behavior. Across repeated episodes and interaction loops, we further observe that models can form statistical estimates of the monitor's effectiveness and use these estimates to guide attempts to evade detection. These findings highlight the need to evaluate CoT-based oversight under adaptive conditions, where the monitoring process itself may influence the behavior it seeks to observe.

</details>


### [126] [P-ContextBench: From Personal Fact Recall to Personalized Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40959)
*Grant Nasir Brown,Abdul Basir Bari,Anmol Tadikonda,Isabel Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出P-ContextBench，一个用于评测LLM理解"个人上下文"的真实、可验证、可扩展基准：以人口统计种子构建生活叙事驱动的"数字人"，将特质、习惯、目标与约束以隐式证据嵌入碎片化、动态演化的合成App记录中，覆盖五类能力并用多阶段流水线校验，实验显示SOTA模型在时序追踪、高层模式推断与约束感知行动边界上准确率不足60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多承担个人化任务，但个人上下文具有私密、碎片化、动态、社会情境化等特点，现有评测难以对其进行安全、可验证的评估，缺少可量化、可扩展且能反映真实生活结构的基准。

Method: 构建基于美国人口人口统计种子的、人口结构化的生活叙事数字人；不采用显式静态画像，而将特质、日常规律、目标与约束作为隐式证据分散嵌入不断演化的合成移动App记录中；定义五项评测能力——单源事实回溯、多源事实回溯、时序状态追踪、从行为规律推断更高层个人模式、以及在与角色特有约束冲突的请求下做出合规/警示/澄清/拒绝的安全处理；设计多阶段验证流水线检查人格可信度、上下文可回溯性、信息泄露、证据保留、测试用例接地性与人工评判一致性。

Result: 实例化版本包含100个角色、10,088条隐藏事实、18,261个App痕迹碎片与4,810条已验证测试用例，流水线支持跨规模与人群的可配置生成；面向个人智能体的高性价比SOTA模型在时序追踪、高层模式推断与约束感知行动边界上准确率低于60%，三次重复运行中全部答对的用例不足10%，即便高性能SOTA模型在这些能力上仍表现不一致。

Conclusion: P-ContextBench为提升LLM个人智能体的准确性、可靠性与安全性提供了可度量的目标，并揭示了当前模型在个人上下文理解与约束感知安全行为上的显著短板。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for personal tasks, yet evaluating their safe use of personal context remains difficult because such contexts are private, fragmented, dynamic, and socially situated. We introduce P-ContextBench, a realistic, verifiable, and scalable benchmark for evaluating personal-context understanding. P-ContextBench constructs population-structured, life-story-grounded digital humans from demographic seeds reflecting the U.S. population. Rather than using explicit, static profiles, P-ContextBench embeds traits, routines, goals, and constraints as implicit evidence across fragmented, evolving synthetic mobile-app records. It evaluates five capabilities: single-source fact recovery, multi-source fact recovery, temporal state tracking, inference of higher-level personal patterns from routine behavior, and safe handling of requests conflicting with persona-specific constraints through compliance, warning, clarification, or refusal. A multi-stage validation pipeline checks persona plausibility, context recoverability, information leakage, evidence preservation, test-case grounding, and human-judge agreement. We instantiate P-ContextBench with 100 personas, 10,088 hidden facts, 18,261 app-trace fragments, and 4,810 verified test cases. The pipeline supports configurable generation across scales and demographic groups. Cost-effective state-of-the-art (SOTA) LLMs relevant to personal agents achieve below 60% accuracy on temporal tracking, higher-level pattern inference, and constraint-aware action-boundary decisions, with fewer than 10% of test cases answered correctly across all three repeated runs. Even a high-performing SOTA model remains inconsistent on these capabilities. P-ContextBench provides measurable targets for improving the accuracy, reliability, and safety of LLM-based personal agents.

</details>


### [127] [Nedimo: An Evidence-Grounded Multilingual Pipeline for AI-Generated Sleep Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40970)
*Georgios Makridis,Stefanos Kampouris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多语言"低唤醒助眠故事"生成与朗读流水线，将不确定的临床证据转化为保守的工程约束；系统将确定性规划与LLM生成分离，采用四阶段注意力渐weak化脚本结构、结构化/词汇校验、有界重试与隔离机制，以及供应商无关的TTS、段落感知拼接、两遍响度归一化与发布门控，并配套以行为代理指标与前瞻性睡眠起始试验方案，明确不主张临床疗效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式音频可批量生产个性化睡前内容，但常规媒体目标（留存、完播、新奇性）与"促进入睡过渡"的目标错位；成人叙事型睡前音频的直接证据混杂，且近期一项主动对照试验不支持其作为独立治疗手段。作者因此希望把这种不确定证据转译为保守的工程需求，避免产品化时夸大功效。

Method: 提出"证据→需求"映射；原型分离确定性规划与LLM生成，显式表示内容与演绎参数；用四阶段注意力渐弱塑造脚本，校验结构与词汇约束，执行有界、错误感知的重试并隔离未解决输出；TTS供应商无关，配合段落感知拼接、两遍响度归一化，以及基于固定响度、响度范围和真峰限制对已编码文件复测的发布门控；数据模型记录停止位置、重播、早期放弃与完成，并将其视为模糊的行为代理而非临床终点。

Result: 摘要未报告实证性能或临床结果；贡献为：证据到需求的映射、带显式验收阈值的可追溯护栏架构、技术评估标准，以及检验睡眠起始效应的前瞻性试验协议。

Conclusion: 系统被定位为放松与注意力支持工具，其收益及潜在依赖性须经实证评估；不作临床疗效声明。护栏与门控设计体现了对不确定证据的保守工程响应。

Abstract: Generative audio systems can produce large libraries of personalized bedtime content, but ordinary media objectives (retention, completion, and novelty) are poorly aligned with the goal of supporting a transition toward sleep. Direct evidence for narrated bedtime audio in adults is mixed, and a recent active-controlled trial did not support such audio as a standalone treatment for sleep disturbance. We translate this uncertain evidence into conservative engineering requirements and present a multilingual pipeline for generating and narrating low-arousal sleep stories. The implemented prototype separates deterministic planning from large language model generation, represents content and delivery parameters explicitly, shapes each script through a four-phase attention taper, validates structural and lexical constraints, performs a bounded error-aware retry, and quarantines unresolved outputs. Provider-agnostic text-to-speech synthesis is followed by paragraph-aware joining, two-pass loudness normalization, and a publication gate that re-measures the saved, encoded file against fixed loudness, loudness-range, and true-peak limits. The product data model records stop position, replay, early abandonment, and completion while treating these events as ambiguous behavioral proxies rather than clinical outcomes. We contribute an evidence-to-requirement mapping, a traceable guardrail architecture with explicit acceptance thresholds, technical evaluation criteria, and a prospective protocol for testing sleep-onset effects. No clinical efficacy is claimed; the system is positioned as a relaxation and attentional-support tool whose benefit and possible reliance must be evaluated empirically.

</details>


### [128] [The psychophysics of affect in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40985)
*Joseph Marks,Marius C. Vollberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文测试大语言模型（LLM）如何预期结果的情绪感受，以及是否将这种感受评分纳入决策，发现模型表现出"硅基增益放大"与情境依赖的、可塑的情感映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 日益介入决策流程，但其如何将结果映射为情感、以及在选择中如何使用该映射尚不清楚；而人类决策理论（前景理论中的价值函数与损失厌恶）为评估提供了参照。

Method: 让 LLM 对赌博或股票交易的结果进行情绪预期评分，分别在"孤立评分"与"共同评分（可直接比较）"两种情境下进行；随后检验模型在二选一情境中的选择，比较其自评情感与货币效用对选择的解释力。

Result: 孤立评分时，评分对量级呈递减敏感性，类似人类价值函数；但模型对收益的评分强度高于等量损失，构成经典损失厌恶的反转（"硅基增益放大"）。共同评分时评分趋于线性化。在选择题中，收益—损失不对称基本消失，模型自评评分并不比货币效用更好地解释其选择。

Conclusion: LLM 的结果—情感映射是可塑的、依赖情境的，且未被稳定地整合进选择；这引出关于具身智能体 LLM 是否以及应如何将预期感受纳入决策的开放问题。

Abstract: Good decisions often require considering how outcomes will feel. Yet large language models (LLMs) increasingly enter decision workflows, despite little being known about how they map outcomes to feelings or use those mappings in choice. We tested how LLMs anticipated gambling or stock-trading outcomes would feel and whether they incorporated these ratings into choice. When outcomes were rated in isolation, ratings exhibited diminishing sensitivity to magnitude, resembling human value functions; but models rated gains more intensely than equivalent losses, an inversion of canonical loss aversion we call silicon gain amplification. When outcomes were rated together, enabling direct comparison, ratings linearised. In choice, the gain-loss asymmetry largely disappeared, and models' own ratings did not reliably explain their choices better than monetary utility did. These findings reveal malleable mappings from outcomes to affect, raising questions about whether and how agentic LLMs should incorporate anticipated feelings into decisions.

</details>


### [129] [Can Personal Requirements Become Supervision for Planning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40992)
*Junwei Wu,Runze Yan,Yuzhang Xie,Hanqi Luo,Ziwei Dong,Jiaying Lu,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Noura：将 LLM 规划与"可执行个人规范"结合，把规范对计划的拒绝信号转化为监督，用于训练小型模型完成多慢病饮食周计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多慢病与用药并存的膳食管理需跨餐跨天同时满足疾病与药物约束，并保留既有饮食习惯；这些要求虽公开于临床指南与药品说明书，但将其转化为个体可行的周计划对语言模型智能体仍很困难——13 个模型直接生成完整合格计划的比例不足 3%，显式规则、更高推理努力或工具调用也仅使 GPT-6.1 Sol 达 8%。

Method: 构建由营养师认可的、基于 NHANES 的周计划评测。Noura 将 LLM 规划与从公开临床指南及个人健康/膳食档案实例化的可执行规范配对：规划中规范对食物进行 grounding、优化份量、校验完整多日计划并引导修订。核心思想是"拒绝计划的规范也能决定从中学什么"——将被执行的计划与上下文归因的修订转为紧凑模型的监督信号，即使轨迹从未完全成功也能回收目标；同一规范还提供 SFT 目标、偏好与强化学习奖励用于后训练。

Result: 在 4,993 名留出参与者上，Noura-SFT 在同一执行循环内将 Qwen3-14B 的完整计划通过率从 24.0% 提升至 72.5%，规则违规下降 97.5%，未观察到关键失败，膳食质量提升。本地部署的 14B 规划器违规数少于其 GPT-6 Luna 教师模型；匹配对比显示执行得到的监督目标比模型自写提案减少 41% 违规；在 Qwen、Phi、Llama 四类骨干上，仅自训练即在 1,249 人子集上将违规降低 41–61%。

Conclusion: 可执行规范既是规划时的约束校验器，也是可扩展的监督来源，可将小型本地模型的多约束膳食规划能力提升至超越大型教师模型的合规水平。

Abstract: Managing multiple chronic conditions through diet requires satisfying disease- and medication-specific requirements jointly across meals and days while preserving established eating habits. These requirements are public in clinical guidelines and drug labels, yet turning them into a feasible plan for one person remains difficult for language-model agents. In our dietitian-approved weekly planning evaluation built from NHANES, thirteen models, from medically fine-tuned 8B models to GPT-6.1 Sol, pass fewer than 3% of complete plans through direct generation. Explicit rules, higher reasoning effort, or model-directed tools leave GPT-6.1 Sol at no more than 8%. We present Noura, which pairs LLM planning with executable personal specifications instantiated from public clinical guidance and individual health and dietary profiles. During planning, the specification grounds foods, optimizes portions, checks the complete multi-day plan, and guides revision. Our key insight is that a specification that rejects a plan can also determine what to learn from it. Noura turns executed plans and contextually credited revisions into supervision for compact models, recovering targets even from trajectories that never achieve complete success. The same specification supplies supervised targets, preferences, and rewards for post-training. On 4,993 held-out participants, Noura-SFT raises Qwen3-14B's complete-plan pass rate from 24.0% to 72.5% within the same execution loop, cuts rule violations by 97.5% with no observed critical failures, and improves dietary quality. The locally hosted 14B planner incurs fewer rule violations than its GPT-6 Luna teacher. A matched comparison shows that executed targets reduce rule violations by 41% over model-written proposals. Across four Qwen, Phi, and Llama backbones, self-training alone reduces rule violations by 41-61% on a 1,249-person population subset.

</details>


### [130] [Violet Teaming in Practice: Lessons from a Pilot Expert Workshop on Governing Complex AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41018)
*Steph Batalis,Kait Connors,Ben C. Snyder,Vikram Venkatram,Josh Wentzel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文记录了将"紫色组队"（violet teaming）作为治理方法用于生物防御相关AI能力评估的试点专家研讨会，介绍其设计、产出与经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴技术的评估需要同时纳入技术、政策与社会考量，而传统做法往往将效益、滥用风险与实施挑战割裂开来单独审视。

Method: 开展试点专家研讨会，采用假设性技术情景，按能力开发、滥用潜力、落地实施三个递进阶段推进，并融入技术、安全、运营与政策等多方视角。

Result: 该递进式设计揭示了不易察觉的滥用路径，将潜在干预措施从纯技术防护拓展到更广范围，并识别出影响落地的运营与政策约束。

Conclusion: "紫色组队"可作为评估复杂两用技术的有效治理方法，本文总结了其工作坊设计、产出与可推广的经验。

Abstract: Violet teaming has been proposed as a method to integrate technical, policy, and societal considerations into the evaluation of emerging technologies. Here, we document a pilot expert workshop used to explore violet teaming as a governance methodology for biodefense-relevant AI capabilities and describe observations from the exercise. The workshop used a hypothetical technology scenario across three sequential stages - capability development, misuse potential, and implementation - while incorporating technical, security, operational, and policy perspectives. The sequential format provided an integrated way to examine potential benefits, misuse risks, and real-world implementation challenges, rather than considering these issues in isolation. The exercise surfaced non-obvious misuse pathways, expanded potential interventions beyond purely technical safeguards, and highlighted operational and policy constraints that could affect implementation. This report summarizes the workshop design, outputs, and lessons learned for applying violet teaming to the evaluation of complex, dual-use technologies.

</details>


### [131] [ReplayRCA: Measuring Post-hoc Diagnosis and Live Oversight of Agent Failures by Counterfactual Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41025)
*Jeremy Canale,Fabien Soulis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReplayRCA 基准，以"干预后成功率"而非与标注一致性来衡量多步任务的错误诊断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于标注归因的基准存在"最后一步捷径"，无法反映真实错误定位与修复能力，也无法评估实时监督场景。

Method: 将环境回滚到所选步骤前的状态、以诊断模型的建议动作替换原动作并让同一智能体继续；指标为 causal repair（相对安慰剂重放的成功率增益）。设 post-hoc autopsy 与 live oversight 两条赛道，任务来自确定性程序化生成的 OpsWorld（6 级难度，末级含多模态）。

Result: 62 个系统性失败上，最佳模型（Grok 4.7）causal repair 达 +51.3 点；前八名统计不可区分，两大厂商旗舰模型分列第 8、12，5 项无模型可修复。强制在最后一步干预损失 17.1 点。87 条运行中，最佳 live supervisor 含小开源模型 DeepSeek V4.1 Flash；post-hoc 表现优异的 GPT-6.1 Sol 在实时监督中最弱（-14.5 点，p = 0.002）。

Conclusion: 基于标注一致性的评估高估了真实诊断能力，且 post-hoc 诊断性能不能作为 live oversight 的可靠代理；应改用干预式因果评估。

Abstract: When a language-model agent fails a multi-step task, can another model find the decisive mistake and fix it with a single action? We introduce ReplayRCA, a benchmark that scores such diagnoses by intervention rather than by agreement with annotations: the environment is restored to the state preceding the chosen step, the proposed action replaces the agent's own, and the same agent continues. The score, causal repair, is the continuation success rate minus that of a paired placebo that replays the agent's original action through the same mechanism. Two tracks share this protocol: a post-hoc autopsy, where the diagnoser sees the whole failed run and is told that it failed, and live oversight, where a supervisor watches the run unfold and may intervene once, before an irreversible action, without knowing whether the run will fail. Tasks come from OpsWorld, a deterministic, procedurally generated back-office environment with six difficulty levels, the last of which is multimodal (QR codes, handwriting). On 62 calibrated systematic failures, the best of 13 frontier models (Grok 4.7) reaches a causal repair of +51.3 points; the top eight models are statistically indistinguishable, the flagship models of the two largest vendors rank 8th and 12th, and 5 items are repaired by no model. Forcing a strong model to intervene at the last step costs it 17.1 points, so the last-step shortcut of annotation-based attribution benchmarks does not transfer. On 87 runs, the two best live supervisors are statistically tied and one of them is a small open-weight model (DeepSeek V4.1 Flash); the supervisor that is among the best post-hoc diagnosers (GPT-6.1 Sol) is the weakest live one (-14.5 points on the same runs, p = 0.002), so post-hoc diagnostic performance can be a poor proxy for live oversight. The environment and the evaluation code are released at https://github.com/jeremy1392/RCAreplay.

</details>


### [132] [Capability-Grounded Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41040)
*Shikha Shrestha,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CGAP：将工具目录确定性编译为PDDL抽象模型，用LLM把请求转成目标并由经典规划器求解，执行中由LLM补充实体级调用、确定性校验拦截破坏计划的调用，最终由LLM验证器核验结果交付，在多基准上最高领先标准工具调用代理28.9分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理常需多次相互依赖的调用，而现有做法靠LLM试错选择调用，无法保证生成可执行的计划，缺乏可靠性保障。

Method: CGAP分三层：1）将工具目录确定性编译为PDDL模型；2）LLM将自然语言请求转为规划目标，经典规划器求解；因工具输出未知，模型为抽象模型，只跟踪输入是否可用而非其取值；3）执行阶段LLM补充抽象计划省略的实体特定调用，确定性检查拒绝任何破坏计划的调用；所有参数须来自可引用来源（对话片段或先前工具响应），结果须经LLM验证器对照请求核验后才计为交付。

Result: 在三个LLM、四个基准（ToolSandbox、ComplexFuncBench、BFCL多轮、τ²-bench retail）上，CGAP较标准工具调用代理最高提升28.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 以能力/资源抽象加经典规划替代试错式调用，配合参数溯源与结果核验，可显著提升多步工具调用的计划可执行性与任务成功率。

Abstract: LLM agents serve natural-language requests through tools, data sources, and other agents, and even simple requests often need several interdependent calls. Typically, the LLM picks these calls by trial and error, with no guarantee of an executable plan. We present Capability-Grounded Agentic Planning (CGAP), which instead reasons about what a request requires and what tools provide. CGAP compiles the tool catalog deterministically into a PDDL model, uses an LLM to turn the request into goals, and solves the model with a classical planner. Since tool outputs are unknown in advance, the model is abstract, tracking which inputs are available, not their values. During execution, an LLM adds entity-specific calls that abstract plans omit, and deterministic checks reject any that break the plan. Every argument is drawn from a cited source, such as a conversation span or an earlier tool response, and each requested result counts as delivered only after an LLM verifier checks it against the request. With three LLMs on ToolSandbox, ComplexFuncBench, BFCL multi-turn, and $τ^2$-bench retail, CGAP outperforms a standard tool-calling agent by up to 28.9 points.

</details>


### [133] [Consensus Within, Competition Between: Decentralized LLM Teams for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41044)
*Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Ananya Acharya,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在简化周界防御仿真中比较了集中指挥官、拍卖共识团队与独立智能体三种LLM多智能体架构在网络不可靠条件下的表现：共识团队突破率最低（15%），且专用外部冲突消解协议使团队突破最少；提示词中的一句预警可显著纠正视觉伪装误判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM协调多智能体团队时存在根本性架构问题：任务分配应集中于单一LLM控制器，还是分散给自主LLM智能体？尤其当通信不可靠时该问题更为关键，本文旨在通过受控仿真给出实证比较。

Method: 构建刻意简化的周界防御仿真，对手由LLM控制；在五种网络条件下对比三种架构——中央指挥官、基于拍卖的共识团队、独立智能体。共识团队中进一步降低负责竞价计算的LLM成本以考察模型能力影响。另在文本状态中为每类空中物体（鸟、民用无人机）附加照片，测试多模态识别，并检验伪装为目标标本鸟无人机的入侵者误判，以及提示中单句预警的纠偏效果。

Result: 突破率：共识团队15%、指挥官32%、独立智能体29%；五种网络条件中仅有限无线电通信范围会改变团队突破率（提高指挥官组）。共识团队中让低成本LLM更多参与竞价计算使突破率上升38个百分点。加入照片后仅近前沿模型能将鸟与民用无人机同入侵者区分；伪装成标本鸟无人机的入侵者被两类模型均误判为鸟，近前沿模型未追击比例由1–16%升至59–61%，加入一句预警后降至0–25%。

Conclusion: 具备专用外部冲突消解协议的LLM团队突破率最低；分布式共识优于集中控制与完全独立；低成本模型承担关键推理环节会显著削弱团队性能；多模态输入并不能自动解决伪装欺骗，而简洁的提示预警可有效缓解。

Abstract: The use of large language models (LLMs) to coordinate multi-agent teams raises a fundamental architectural question: whether task allocation should be centralized in a single LLM controller or distributed across autonomous LLM agents, particularly when communication is unreliable. In a deliberately simplified perimeter-defense simulation against LLM-controlled intruders, we compared a central commander, an auction-based consensus team and independent agents under five network conditions. Breach rates were 15% for the consensus team, 32% for the commander and 29% for independent agents, and of the different network conditions, only limited radio range changed a team's breach rate, raising the commander's. In consensus team, giving a low-cost LLM more authority over the bid computation raised breaches by 38 percentage points. With birds and civilian drones in airspace, we added a photograph for each to the input text state. Only the near-frontier model treated them apart from intruders. Intruders disguised as existing taxidermy bird drones were mistook as birds by both low-cost and near-frontier LLMs, going unpursued in 59-61% of near-frontier decisions (1-16% before). However, a one-sentence forewarning in the prompt lowered it to 0-25%. We find that LLM team with a dedicated external conflict resolution protocol results in fewest breaches. Code: https://github.com/the-kas-lab/consensus-within-competition-between.

</details>


### [134] [LanG 2.0: Energy-Efficient Agentic AI for Semi-Automated Security Operations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41072)
*Anes Abdennebi,Nadjia Kara,Laaziz Lahlou,Hakima Ould-Slimane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LanG 2.0 是一个 agentic 安全运营系统，通过结构化保障（实体锚定关联、人工审批、防篡改账本）而非模型精度来提供可信保证，证明本地小模型可低能耗、安全地承担大部分 SOC 威胁检测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC 需要将海量告警、日志与流量转化为可信且可负担的决策，以发现隐蔽威胁。本文探究：在结构性保障而非模型精度提供保证的前提下，小型本地模型能否以更低成本、更安全地替代前沿大模型。

Method: 系统包含实体锚定（entity-anchored）关联、规则变更的人工审批、以及防篡改账本（tamper-evident ledger）。以每个网络的正常流量为基线对流量打分。评估覆盖五个网络的八个流量数据集、真实 XDR 告警及多阶段攻击战役；与基线模型 Claude Opus 5.5 及 2.0 前代版本对比，并测试提示注入、标签投毒与账本篡改的鲁棒性。

Result: 相较前代，战役关联精确率由 18.75% 提升至 51.81%，规则接受率由最高 60.00% 提升至 98.33%。相较 Claude Opus 5.5，在未见网络上检测攻击达 73.42% F1 且无误报，后者为 58.10% F1 但误报率 49.00%，同时能耗低至少 650 倍。微调模型在告警分类与规则编写上与其统计上相当，能耗低 5.6–59 倍。系统 6.4 分钟处理 333 万条记录，每小时超 3000 万条；安全防护抵御了提示注入、标签投毒与账本篡改。

Conclusion: LanG 2.0 展示了实际可扩展性与可靠的结构性保障，表明小型本地模型能以低成本、安全地承担 SOC 的大部分检测工作。

Abstract: Security operations centers (SOCs) need to turn terabytes of alerts, logs and flows into trusted, affordable decisions to unveil hidden threats. We present LanG 2.0, an agentic system that detects threats, correlates evidence and writes rules, investigating whether small local models can replace frontier models affordably and safely when structural safeguards, not model accuracy, provide the guarantees. It incorporates entity-anchored correlation, human approval of rule changes and a tamper-evident ledger. Tests span eight flow datasets from five networks, real XDR alerts and multistage campaigns. Against its predecessor, campaign correlation precision rises from 18.75% to 51.81%, and rule acceptance from at most 60.00% to 98.33%. Against Claude Opus 5.5, scoring flows against each network's normal traffic detects attacks on unseen networks at 73.42% F1 without false positives, versus 58.10% with 49.00% false positives, for at least 650 times less energy. Finetuned models statistically match it on alert classification and rule writing for 5.6 to 59 times less energy. The work demonstrates LanG's practical scalability, processing 3.33 million records in 6.4 minutes, over 30 million hourly, and its safeguards resisted prompt injection, label poisoning and ledger tampering, so small local models can carry most SOC detection affordably and safely.

</details>


### [135] [BABA-Bench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Patient-Advocacy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41076)
*Shriram Vasudevan,Videet Mehta,Connor Sweeney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BABA-Bench，一个由 47 个源自真实患者权益倡导案例构建的交互式世界基准，逐步校验智能体在医疗协助任务中是否同时满足"结果正确"与"程序合规"，揭示当前前沿模型普遍存在"走对结果、走错流程"的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗场景中的 LLM 智能体不能只追求正确结果，还必须在过程中遵守必要程序（如共享病历前获得患者授权、行为需有证据支撑、不得执行被禁止的操作）。现有基准通常只评估最终结果，缺乏对过程合规性的细粒度检验。

Method: 构建 47 个交互式世界，每个世界基于去标识化的真实患者权益倡导案例，并经专家倡导者审阅；智能体需挖掘隐藏信息并协调保险公司、诊所、供应商、药房与社区组织。引擎在动作发生时即时校验：是否具备授权、是否有证据支撑、动作是否被禁止。评测 14 个前沿模型，每个世界 3 次运行，共 1,974 次运行；另设"提供已验证路线"的对照实验。

Result: 在 921 次达成全部必需结果的运行中，254 次（28%）未遵守必要程序。Claude Opus 5 达成全部结果的运行比例为 81.6%，但通过全部校验的仅 52.5%。在提供已验证路线后，两个模型的通过率分别从 29.8% 提升至 91.5%、从 44.0% 提升至 97.2%。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型的核心瓶颈在于"寻找合规路径"而非"执行已知路径"；结果导向的评估会显著高估智能体的实际能力，过程合规性应成为医疗智能体评测的一等指标。

Abstract: An LLM agent that helps a patient get care must do two things. It must reach the correct outcome, and it must follow the necessary procedure. For example, the agent must get the patient's permission before it shares the patient's records. We introduce BABA-Bench, a benchmark of 47 interactive worlds. We built each world from a de-identified, real patient advocacy case that expert advocates reviewed. In each world, the agent must find hidden information and coordinate insurers, clinics, suppliers, pharmacies, and community organizations. The engine checks each action at the time the agent does it. It checks if the agent has permission, if the agent has evidence for the action, and if the action is prohibited. We evaluated 14 frontier models with three runs on each world, for a total of 1,974 runs. Agents often reached the correct outcome by an invalid route. Of the 921 runs that reached all required outcomes, 254 (28%) did not follow the necessary procedure. Claude Opus 5 reached all required outcomes in 81.6% of its runs, but only 52.5% of its runs passed all checks. When we gave two models a verified route, their pass rates increased from 29.8% to 91.5% and from 44.0% to 97.2%. For these models, the main problem is to find a route, not to execute it.

</details>


### [136] [Optimization Techniques for Solving the Minimum Red--Blue Separating Set Problem with an Application to Human--AI Text Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41081)
*Marko Djukanović,Staša Vujičić Stanković,Žiga Zebec,Christian Blum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对源于计算几何的最小红蓝分离集问题（MRBSSP），论文提出紧凑集合覆盖ILP、安全预处理规则、GRASP启发式，以及LBBD与新型Branch-and-Benders-Cut两种精确算法，并在随机图与NLP文本鉴别应用中验证了各方法的互补优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRBSSP来自计算几何，具有组合优化结构，需要同时获得高质量可行解与强下界/最优性证明；现有文献仅有VNS等启发式，缺乏紧凑数学模型、预处理与精确算法。此外作者希望将该问题推广到新的NLP应用（区分人类与AI生成文本的词表图特征），以检验方法的实用性与可解释性。

Method: 1) 建立紧凑的集合覆盖ILP模型；2) 设计安全预处理规则（不损失最优性）；3) 构造GRASP元启发式；4) 两种精确方法：基于逻辑的Benders分解（LBBD）与新颖的分支-Benders-割（BBC）；5) 与ILP、GRASP及文献中的VNS对比；6) 用基于词表的图特征将文本鉴别建模为MRBSSP实例。

Result: 在250个顶点数至多500的随机图上：ILP给出最佳可行解；LBBD下界最强、平均最优性间隙最小；BBC最频繁地证明最优性，体现互补优势。NLP应用42个实例中，BBC平均最优性间隙最小并证明16个最优解，GRASP优于VNS。对272个测试中最优词表分离子的结构分析识别出113个必需特征与151个可替换特征，显示最优解间存在显著多样性。

Conclusion: 互补的优化策略（ILP、LBBD、BBC、GRASP、VNS）在本问题中各有侧重、组合使用更有效；基于最优词表分离子的鉴别具有良好的可解释性，且最优解高度多样，提示可利用冗余特征提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study the so-called Minimum Red''Blue Separating Set Problem (MRBSSP) which arises from computational geometry. In particular, we propose a compact Set-Cover ILP formulation, safe preprocessing rules, a Greedy Randomized Adaptive Search Procedure (GRASP), and two exact approaches: one is based on Logic-Based Benders Decomposition (LBBD) approach, and the second represents a novel Branch-and-Benders-Cut (BBC) approach. These two exact approaches are compared to ILP--alongside GRASP and the Variable Neighborhood Search (VNS) from literature. Experiments on 250 random graphs with up to 500 vertices reveal complementary strengths: ILP yields the best feasible solutions, LBBD provides the strongest lower bounds and smallest mean optimality gap, and BBC certifies optimality most frequently. We further introduce an NLP application distinguishing human- and AI-generated texts through vocabulary-based graph features. On 42 instances, BBC achieves the smallest mean optimality gap, certifying 16 optima, while GRASP outperforms VNS. Structural analysis of optimal vocabulary separators identifies 113 mandatory and 151 replaceable features across 272 tests, highlighting substantial variability among optimal solutions. Overall, our results demonstrate the effectiveness of complementary optimization strategies and the interpretability of optimal vocabulary-based discrimination.

</details>


### [137] [Payroll Fraud Detection in Synthetic HR/ERP Graphs: An Inductive Comparison of Rules, Path Features, and Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41085)
*Xiaobo Chen,Genliang Zhu,Ziyuan Wang,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将薪资欺诈筛查建模为HR/ERP图上的归纳式链路预测与实体排序任务，构建带匹配良性孪生样本的合成评估设计，比较分析师规则、显式路径特征与GNN；结果显示链式连接特征上的梯度提升模型总体AP最高（0.823 > 0.663），多跳优势并非GNN特有，但在标识符连接失败率40%时实体GNN更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薪资欺诈筛查需要发现隐藏的员工—收款人关联，并区分可疑关系与合法关系。现有研究缺乏在未见组织上可泛化、且能控制捷径与虚假相关（如仅凭结构即可区分）的严格评估设计，也缺乏对"多跳候选发现—基于记录的判别—复核优先级排序"三个环节的清晰拆分。

Method: 将问题形式化为归纳式链路预测与实体排序，在合成HR/ERP图上构建评估：24个留出组织 + 前瞻性冻结的扩展实验；为每个植入案例配备匹配的良性孪生样本；对比分析师规则、显式路径特征（chained-join特征上的梯度提升）与GraIL式GNN、关系型实体GNN；新合成队列正样本实体占2.0%；并开展结构-only审计与40%标识符连接失败的压力测试，在2%复核预算下评估精度。

Result: 链路AP：chained-join梯度提升0.823，优于GraIL式GNN的0.663；4跳AP为0.646 vs 0.272。新队列中chained-join提升再次领先（AP 0.798），实体GNN优于record-aware规则（0.707 vs 0.504）；2%复核预算下精度0.695 vs 0.602。40%标识符连接失败时实体GNN AP 0.712略高于chained-join的0.683（描述性）。结构审计发现初始生成器存在捷径，匹配孪生将审计孪生池AUROC降至0.493–0.514（接近随机）。

Conclusion: 多跳收益不具GNN特异性，显式连接特征亦可实现；实体级关系GNN在记录噪声（标识符连接失败）下更具鲁棒性。研究在明确的合成评估框架内区分了多跳候选发现、基于记录的判别与复核优先级排序三类子任务，并强调匹配孪生与结构捷径审计对评估有效性的必要性。

Abstract: Payroll-fraud screening requires finding hidden employee--payee links while distinguishing suspicious relationships from legitimate ones. We formulate this problem as inductive link prediction and entity ranking on synthetic human-resources and enterprise resource planning (HR/ERP) graphs, with unseen organizations and a matched benign twin for every planted case. An original study on 24 held-out organizations and a prospectively frozen extension compare analyst rules, explicit path features and graph neural networks (GNNs). A tuned gradient-boosted model on chained-join features achieved the highest overall link average precision (AP; 0.823), above the GraIL-style GNN (0.663); its 4-hop AP was 0.646 versus 0.272. Thus, the observed multi-hop gain is not specific to GNNs. On a new synthetic cohort with 2.0% positive entities, chained-join boosting again led (AP 0.798); the relational entity GNN also exceeded record-aware rules (0.707 versus 0.504). At a 2% review budget, their precision was 0.695 versus 0.602. Under 40% failed identifier joins, the entity GNN ranked above chained-join boosting (AP 0.712 versus 0.683, descriptively). Structure-only auditing exposed a shortcut in the initial generator; matched twins reduced the audited twin-pool AUROC to 0.493--0.514. These results separate multi-hop candidate discovery, record-based discrimination and review prioritization within an explicit synthetic evaluation design. Code and data are available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [138] [CIRCE Divines LLM Task Complexity from Constraint Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41089)
*Chenhao Hong,Jiakai Xu,Kaushiki Singh,Yusen Zhang,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CIRCE 用廉价 LLM 将题目转换为约束中间表示并编译为约束图，以此在模型尝试前预测 LLM 能否正确作答，显著优于现有估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预判问题对 LLM 是否过难，可用于模型路由、分配测试时计算量以及判断是否需要新工具。现有方法依赖题面文本、LLM 标注的难度标签，或模型尝试后的信号（如隐状态），均未显式利用问题内在的约束结构——即待赋值变量及其约束交互方式。

Method: 提出 CIRCE：用廉价 LLM 抽取问题结构，构建"约束中间表示"（Constraint Intermediate Representation），并确定性地编译为约束图；再由图神经网络（GNN）编码器在该约束图上进行预测，即使编码器随机初始化也可用于估计 LLM 作答正确性。

Result: 在七个推理基准（含 SAT、逻辑、数学）和四个 LLM 上，相对各指标最强基线，负对数似然由 0.465 降至 0.423，AUROC 由 0.691 提升至 0.727。

Conclusion: 显式利用约束结构是预测 LLM 正确性的有效信号；约束图表示可在模型尝试前提供可靠难度估计，且对编码器初始化不敏感，说明性能增益主要来自结构表示本身而非训练信号。

Abstract: Estimating whether a problem is too complex for a large language model (LLM) before the model attempts it can guide model routing, how to allocate test-time compute, and suggest the need for new tools to aid the agent.
