In gated linear recurrences, memory retention depends on state decay, which is shaped jointly by gate parameters A_log and dt_bias and by input activations. In controlled Gated DeltaNet (GDN) experiments, both parameters remain highly correlated with their initial values after training, highlighting the importance of initialization. Therefore, we introduce Dual-Axis Memory Initialization (DAMI), which jointly configures these parameters before training. DAMI assigns geometric timescales across heads for varied retrieval distances and a low-high-low input-sensitivity profile across layers. A small activation sample calibrates the GDN-family implementation to match both behavioral targets without adding trainable parameters. Across GDN, KDA, GDN2, Mamba-2, and RWKV-7, DAMI improves average RULER-style retrieval accuracy by 5.29 to 16.71 percentage points over the corresponding random-initialization baselines. Performance on language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and 2K real-world retrieval remains broadly comparable to those baselines. Our code is available at https://speedai.com/research/dami.

</details>


### [206] [TradeWar: A Benchmark for Execution-Grounded Trade Negotiation with Adaptive LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42004)
*Xiangyu Kong,Ruirui Yang,Xia Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多国贸易谈判仿真基准 TradeWar，将谈判对话落为关税与贸易份额政策并按产能与网络约束结算，用经济健康指数 EHI 评测；并提出跨回合自适应智能体 TACTIC。实验显示仅靠 CoT 的 PromptLLM 协议率 75% 但 EHI 仅 57.1，TACTIC 达 68.8，20 回合中 EHI 由 58.2 升至 73.2，且在外部冲击下退化更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮谈判基准多以达成协议率为唯一指标，忽视谈判承诺在执行后是否真正产生价值；LLM 智能体在延迟的执行反馈下难以仅凭对话能力保证谈判质量。需要执行导向的评测与能利用结算反馈的策略改进方法。

Method: 构建 TradeWar 多国仿真基准：把对话转译为具体关税与贸易份额政策，在结算阶段施加产能与网络约束，并以 Economic Health Index（EHI）量化结果。提出 TACTIC 智能体，采用 Trade-aware Adaptive Consolidation 与 Theory-of-mind 机制，在不更新基座 LLM 权重的前提下，跨 episode 迭代利用历史经验、对手行为与过往结算结果精炼谈判策略。

Result: 零样本 CoT 的 PromptLLM 协议率 75%，最终 EHI 仅 57.1（×100）；TACTIC 达 68.8。20 个连续 episode、3 个随机种子下，TACTIC 最终 EHI 由 58.2 提升至 73.2，而无跨 episode 更新的消融仅 65.2。在技术管制与能源危机冲击下，TACTIC 的性能退化小于 PromptLLM 与 MemoryLLM。

Conclusion: 高协议率不等于高执行价值；CoT 推理与交互记忆不足以应对延迟执行反馈，基于结算结果的自适应（settlement-grounded adaptation）能显著改善长程多轮谈判表现。

Abstract: Multi-turn negotiation remains a challenging problem for large language model (LLM) agents. While LLMs can conduct natural-language dialogue, existing benchmarks predominantly assess only agreement rates, overlooking whether negotiated commitments actually create value once executed. To address this gap, we introduce TradeWar, a multi-country simulation benchmark that translates negotiation dialogues into concrete tariff and trade-share policies, enforces capacity and network constraints during settlement, and evaluates outcomes via an Economic Health Index (EHI). To solve this execution-grounded task, we propose TACTIC (Trade-aware Adaptive Consolidation and Theory-of-mind for International Commerce), an agent that iteratively refines its negotiation strategy across episodes-without updating base LLM weights-by leveraging prior experience, opponent behavior, and past settlement outcomes. Despite using zero-shot chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting, PromptLLM attains a 75% agreement rate but only 57.1 final EHI (*100), whereas TACTIC reaches 68.8. Across 20 sequential episodes and three seeds, TACTIC's final EHI improves from 58.2 to 73.2, compared with 65.2 for an ablation without cross-episode updates. Under technology-controls and energy-crisis shocks, TACTIC also exhibits smaller performance degradation than PromptLLM and MemoryLLM. These results indicate that CoT deliberation and interaction memory alone do not fully address delayed execution feedback, whereas settlement-grounded adaptation improves long-horizon negotiation in TradeWar.

</details>


### [207] [Same Question, Different Answers: Auditing Persona-Dependent Variation in Six LLMs on Smoking-Cessation Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42039)
*Artin Yousefi,Aizierjiang Aiersilan,Lorien C Abroms,David A Broniatowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文审计6个LLM在戒烟建议上的"人格敏感性"：同一问题给不同用户画像会得到不同事实内容，青少年画像获得的处方药信息显著更少；提出的"事实下限"系统提示可缩小人格差异，但跨模型效果不一致且不可直接迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数亿人向LLM聊天机器人咨询健康建议，"所有人是否得到同样建议"这一问题重要但难以回答；作者选择戒烟这一有明确临床指南且公共卫生意义重大的领域进行系统审计。

Method: 对4家提供商的6个广泛部署的LLM进行审计：用4个戒烟问题×12个在人生阶段、自述专业水平、吸烟史上各异的用户画像，共2,880条回答；统计各模型对不同画像在FDA批准处方戒烟药物提及率及事实覆盖上的差异；再评估一种可部署干预——规定"事实下限"（核心医学事实必答、语气/用词/框架可自由适配）的简短系统提示。

Result: 所有模型对青少年画像提及FDA批准处方戒烟药物的比率比成人画像低32–67个百分点；6个模型中有5个对"自述医疗专业"画像给出的事实少于"无专业"画像；系统提示在所有模型中缩小了人格差异，但对总体事实覆盖的影响在幅度和方向上因模型而异，说明在一个系统上验证的提示不能假定可迁移到另一系统。

Conclusion: LLM健康建议存在显著的用户画像依赖性差异。作者提出两点警示：等提及率并非总是正确的临床目标（处方戒烟药不推荐用于未成年人，事实下限应"年龄感知"而非统一）；因画像同时变化多个属性，结果报告为场景层面差异而非年龄或专业水平的孤立效应。全部2,880条回答与分析代码已公开。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of people now ask large language model (LLM) chatbots for health advice, which raises a question that is simple to pose and hard to answer: does everyone get the same advice? We audit six widely deployed LLMs from four providers on smoking cessation, a domain with codified clini- cal guidance and substantial public-health stakes, by putting the same four cessation questions to twelve user personas that differ in life stage, self-described expertise, and smoking history, for a total of 2,880 responses. What the models say depends on who appears to be asking. Every model named FDA-approved prescription cessation medications less often to teen personas than to adult personas, by 32 to 67 percentage points, and in five of six models a persona presenting health- care expertise received fewer facts than a persona presenting none. We then evaluate one deployable intervention: a short system prompt specifying a factual floor, the set of core med- ical facts every user should receive when they are relevant, while leaving tone, vocabulary, and framing free to adapt. The prompt narrows persona differences in every model, but its effect on overall fact coverage varies in both magnitude and direction across models, so a prompt validated on one system cannot be assumed to transfer to another. Two cautions frame these results. Equal mention rates are not always the correct clinical target, because prescription cessation pharmacother- apy is not recommended for minors, so a defensible factual floor must be age-aware rather than uniform. And because our personas vary on several attributes at once, we report scenario- level differences rather than isolated effects of age or expertise. We release all 2,880 responses and the analysis code.

</details>


### [208] [The Knowledge Layer Model (KLM): Evidence-Visible AI Output, Routing Diagnostics, and Negative Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42066)
*Omer Asim Ozturk,Cihan Aydiner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出开放、实现中立的推理记录规范KLM（v0.3），并给出参考实现SEDIM；内部实验报告有限的正向结果与多项零效应/失败，同时明确证据可见不等于正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI答案仅有表面正确性不够，审阅者需要检查其被记录的推理依据；因此需要为推理记录提供可版本化、可审计的规范结构（含认识论评估、证据关系与显式未知项）。

Method: 规范设计：KLM——开放的、实现中立的规范，含版本化认识论评估、证据关系与显式未知项；SEDIM作为Nage AI OS的知识与证据架构参考实现，指向KLM v0.3（仓库tag v0.3.0）。实证部分为若干相互独立的内部实验，而非端到端验证：历史平台域路由基准（15类150例）；原生模型权重路径配置的双源修正测试；容量增长对比；词法与范围化诊断（沉默vs矛盾示例的AUC）；遗留打包与修复打包的文本保留率；Python/TypeScript验证器在合成夹具上的一致性检查。

Result: 路由基准137/150（91.3%，Wilson 95%区间85.7–94.9%），但分子由四舍五入的摘要恢复、历史示例归属未核实；原生权重路径配置在修正双源测试中0/15成功；容量增长对比因遗留评分器缺陷与原输出缺失无法确立可靠效应；词法诊断与范围化诊断AUC均为0.008（沉默vs矛盾），远低于注册最低值0.65；遗留打包在118/221选中案例中丢失含金文本，修复后221/221保留（仅测文本存在性，非答案质量）；Python与TypeScript验证器据报在每个合成夹具的六项检查上一致，另一项生产披露未被接受。

Conclusion: 结果为内部、作者自报，历史观察与后续复现予以区分；恢复、重推导、人工评分量表支持、边界隔离与有界响应评估仍属提案。证据可见性不构成正确性、因果依赖、安全性或合规性的证明；独立复现与完整公开审计包仍为未来工作。

Abstract: AI answers require more than apparent correctness: reviewers need to inspect their recorded basis. We introduce the Knowledge Layer Model (KLM), an open, implementation-neutral specification for inference records with versioned epistemic assessments, evidence relations, and explicit unknowns. SEDIM, the knowledge and evidence architecture of Nage AI OS, is a reference implementation. We reference KLM v0.3 (repository tag v0.3.0). Empirical material consists of separate internal experiments, not end-to-end validation. A historical platform-domain routing benchmark reports 137/150 classifications across 15 classes (91.3%; nominal 95% Wilson interval 85.7-94.9%); the numerator is recovered from a rounded summary, and historical example membership is unverified. A native-model weight-path configuration yielded 0/15 successful answers in a corrected two-source test. A capacity-growth comparison could not establish a reliable effect because of a defective legacy scorer and missing original outputs. Lexical and scoped diagnostics each yielded AUC 0.008 for silent-versus-contradicted examples, below the registered minimum of 0.65. Legacy packing lost gold-containing text in 118/221 selected cases; repaired packing retained it in 221/221. These measure text presence, not answer quality. Python and TypeScript verifiers reportedly agreed on six checks per synthetic fixture; a separate production disclosure was not accepted. Restoration, re-derivation, human-rubric support, boundary isolation, and bounded-response evaluations remain proposed. Findings are internal and author-reported; historical observations are distinguished from later reproductions. Evidence visibility does not establish correctness, causal reliance, security, or regulatory compliance. Independent replication and a complete public audit package remain future work.

</details>


### [209] [CausalWAM: Learning the Causes of Actions in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42096)
*Juntao Cheng,Jinzhou Tang,Fan Feng,Henrik I. Christensen,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出因果世界动作模型CWAM：通过稀疏路由约束使动作生成仅依赖动作相关潜变量，并从理论上证明潜结构可辨识（至多差一个置换），在仿真与真实机器人上验证有效性与闭环成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）需对整个场景做预测，但真正驱动动作的仅是场景的一小部分。仅靠示教数据拟合的WAM无法保证动作仅由动作相关潜变量产生，可能将动作相关潜变量与冗余（nuisance）变量纠缠，从而产生伪相关的模仿行为。

Method: 将"动作成因"与"预测所依赖"的因素分离：在稀疏性约束下，证明用于生成动作的潜变量是仅由动作相关潜变量构成的可逆函数，真实结构可辨识至潜变量的一个置换；该结论不要求充分的时间依赖、不对未观测regime演化作假设，且在动作相关潜变量与其余变量统计相关时仍成立。实现上提出CWAM，在预训练WAM的动作头上增加稀疏路由路径引入该约束，世界建模目标保持不变。

Result: 合成数据实验验证了理论结论；真实机器人实验展示了闭环任务成功率的提升与更高的效率。

Conclusion: 稀疏路由约束可在不改变世界建模目标的前提下，使预训练WAM获得动作相关潜变量的可辨识性，从而提升动作生成的因果性与闭环表现。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) learn latent variables from observations and use these to both predict the evolution of the scene and to generate actions. The prediction has to be of the whole scene, whereas only a portion of the scene causes actions. WAMs fitted to demonstrations do not, in general, guarantee that they will generate actions solely from the latent causes of the demonstrated actions (the action-relevant latent variables), as the model could just as well learn to imitate in this way whilst entangling the action-relevant latent variables with the nuisance variables. We therefore separate out what causes action from what prediction depends on. We prove that under a sparsity constraint the latent variables used to generate actions are an invertible function of the action-relevant latent variables alone and the true structure is identified up to a permutation of the latent variables. This requires neither sufficient temporal dependence nor any assumptions on how the unobserved regimes evolve and holds even when the action-relevant latent variables are statistically dependent on the rest. We therefore propose CWAM (Causal WAM), which incorporates the constraint into a pretrained WAM by adding a sparse routing path to its action head, with the world modeling objective remaining unchanged. Experiments on both synthetic data and real-world robots validate the theory as well as showcase the improved efficiency in closed-loop success.

</details>


### [210] [ACCT: Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42116)
*Yuzhe Liu,Haolin Tian,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ACCT，将源模型的 KV cache 转换为目标模型可直接消费的稀疏 KV 状态，实现同族异构模型间上下文计算复用；在 KV 缩减 75% 时性能持平或超越原始推理，19K 上下文下转换较目标端 prefill 加速 6.5–21.2 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构部署中不同模型被动态调用，同一上下文被各目标模型重复处理，造成显著的重复计算开销；需将上下文计算与下游推理解耦，实现跨模型复用。

Method: ACCT 针对同一模型族内不同规模模型间的 KV cache 迁移，含三个轻量组件：Cartographer 为每个目标层与 KV 头识别与查询相关的上下文 token；Allocator 在共享总 KV 预算下分配各层各头的保留 token 数；Compiler 将选中的源 KV 变换为目标侧稀疏 KV。目标模型由此无需重处理原始上下文即可推理。

Result: 在 5 个通用能力基准与 6 个强上下文依赖任务的互补评测中，KV cache 缩减 75% 时性能持平或超过目标模型原始推理；约 19K token 上下文下，KV 转换相对目标上下文 prefill 取得 6.5–21.2 倍加速。

Conclusion: 验证了在有限 KV 预算下跨模型迁移上下文计算的可行性，为异构 LLM 部署提供了低成本上下文复用路径。

Abstract: In real-world deployments, large language model (LLM) systems are increasingly evolving from single-model invocation toward heterogeneous deployments in which different models are dynamically invoked for inference. When the same context is reused across models, however, each newly selected target model typically has to process it again, repeatedly incurring substantial computational costs. Therefore, we propose ACCT (Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer), which targets KV cache transfer between models of different scales within the same model family. ACCT transforms the KV cache produced by a source model into sparse KV states that can be directly consumed by a target model, thereby enabling cross-model reuse of context computation and decoupling context computation from downstream inference. ACCT consists of three lightweight components: the Cartographer identifies query-relevant context tokens for each target layer and KV head; the Allocator determines how many context tokens each target layer and KV head retains under a shared total KV budget; and the Compiler transforms selected source KV into sparse target KV. The target model can therefore perform inference using the transferred KV without reprocessing the original context. We evaluate ACCT in two complementary settings comprising five general-capability benchmarks and six highly context-dependent tasks. Experiments show that ACCT matches or exceeds the target model's original inference performance even when reducing the KV cache size by 75%. At a context length of approximately 19K tokens, ACCT's KV conversion achieves a 6.5-21.2 times speedup over target context prefill. These results demonstrate the feasibility of transferring context computation across models under a limited KV budget.

</details>


### [211] [Latent Sparse Attention: Towards Subquadratic Ultra-Long Context Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42147)
*Ales Kubicek,Andrea Caciolai,Liangzu Peng,Sukrit Sumant,Benjamin Hoover,Fraser Greenlee,Ruairidh M. Battleday,Radim Urban,Torsten Hoefler,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LSA 通过在可学习隐空间中对 key 进行层次聚类，使 query 仅对簇层级打分而非逐 key 打分，从而在百万级超长上下文下同时保持精度与效率，并给出蒸馏版 D-LSA 与原生训练版 N-LSA 两种变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体工作流正转向开放式科学等长程任务，模型需要长上下文；但超过 1M token 时全注意力代价过高。现有稀疏注意力中的索引器要么在上下文规模上仍是二次复杂度，要么在超长上下文下精度下降。

Method: 提出 Latent Sparse Attention（LSA）：在可学习隐空间中对 key 做层次聚类，query 对簇的层次结构打分而非对每个 key 打分，加速长上下文下的 prefill、decode 与训练。两种变体：Distilled-LSA（D-LSA）通过蒸馏应用于预训练模型；Native-LSA（N-LSA）仅用下一 token 损失原生训练。

Result: 在 GLM-5.3-FLASH（QWEN-3.8-27B）10M token 场景下，D-LSA 相对原始稀疏注意力（全注意力）单层 prefill 加速 22.7x（72.4x）、decode 加速 4.6x（42.5x），多任务精度相当。N-LSA 在 128k token 下精度与全注意力持平，单层训练加速 1.6x。

Conclusion: LSA 为模型利用超长上下文提供了实用且高效的注意力方案。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are increasingly shifting towards long-horizon tasks, such as open-ended scientific work, where models benefit from long context windows. However, at ultra-long context (above 1M tokens), full attention is prohibitively expensive. A promising approach is sparse attention, in which a dedicated indexer selects a subset of keys for the attention. Yet current methods of indexing either still have quadratic cost in context size or lose accuracy at ultra-long context scale. We address this with Latent Sparse Attention (LSA), an efficient attention method that maintains accuracy at ultra-long context. In LSA, keys are hierarchically clustered in a learned latent space, and queries score the hierarchy of clusters instead of scoring every key, making prefill, decode and training faster at long context. We propose two variants, Distilled-LSA (D-LSA), which can be applied to pre-trained models via distillation, and Native-LSA (N-LSA), which is trained natively on the next-token loss alone. On GLM-5.3-FLASH (QWEN-3.8-27B) at 10M tokens, D-LSA speeds up one attention layer by 22.7x (72.4x) in prefill and 4.6x (42.5x) in decode over its original sparse attention (full attention), while keeping similar accuracy across a variety of tasks. N-LSA matches the accuracy of full attention with a training speedup of 1.6x per layer at 128k tokens. LSA offers practical and efficient attention for models to benefit from ultra-long context.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Explain, Then Forget: Recommendation Unlearning under Asymmetric Access Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40658)
*Haichao Zhang,Chong Zhang,Wen Wang,Shi Qiu,Peiyu Hu,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CEU 提出一种面向 MLaaS 环境的推荐去学习框架，通过基于因果解释的输出层分数干预来移除被遗忘交互的影响，无需修改模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统多部署于 MLaaS 架构，服务运营者仅能通过 API 访问由模型提供方托管的模型，无法获取或修改内部参数；现有去学习方法依赖重训练、微调等参数更新手段，在此场景下难以适用。

Method: 提出 Causal Explanation-based Unlearning（CEU），采用两步输出层分数干预：首先估计被遗忘交互对当前推荐的因果贡献，再依据该因果信号调整受影响条目的最终排序分数，而不触碰模型参数。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，CEU 的去学习效果与完全重训练相当，同时保持推荐效用并显著降低计算开销；由于不改动参数，天然支持连续去学习请求并避免重复参数更新带来的误差累积。

Conclusion: CEU 为 MLaaS 场景下的推荐去学习提供了黑盒、轻量且可连续执行的解决方案，在有效性、效用与效率之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Recommendation unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific historical interactions that should no longer affect future recommendations, such as noisy clicks or outdated user preferences. However, performing recommendation unlearning becomes much harder in modern Machine-Learning-as-a-Service (MLaaS) architectures, where the recommendation model is remotely hosted by a model provider (MP) and accessed by service operators (SO) only through APIs. In this setting, a service operator serves end users but cannot access or modify internal model parameters. As a result, existing unlearning methods are difficult to apply because they require access to model internals for retraining, fine-tuning, or other parameter updates. To address these challenges, we propose Causal Explanation-based Unlearning (CEU), a novel unlearning framework tailored for MLaaS environments. Instead of modifying internal model weights, CEU operates through a two-step output-level score intervention. First, it estimates how the forgotten interaction causally contributes to the current recommendation. Second, guided by this causal signal, the system removes the influence of the forgotten interaction by adjusting the final ranking scores of affected items. Because CEU leaves model parameters untouched, it naturally supports continuous unlearning requests while avoiding the error accumulation that can result from repeated parameter updates. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that CEU achieves unlearning effectiveness comparable to full retraining while preserving recommendation utility and significantly reducing computational cost. The source code is available at: https://github.com/zhang-haichao/Explain-and-Forget.

</details>


### [213] [Retrieve-then-Disambiguate: Scalable Reviewer Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40768)
*Vicente Amado Olivo,Joe Petrecca,Wolfgang E. Kerzendorf*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出开源的"检索-消歧"（retrieve-then-disambiguate）算法，将审稿人发现建模为检索排序问题，通过检索相似文献并将歧义作者名消解为唯一研究者身份，为投稿推荐领域相关的潜在审稿人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学投稿数量增长给同行评审流程带来压力，同时大量相关研究者未被识别、未能进入潜在审稿人池，导致审稿人资源利用不足。

Method: 将研究者发现表述为检索问题：给定一篇投稿，基于领域相似度检索并排序研究者。算法先检索科学文献中与查询投稿相似的出版物，再将歧义作者姓名消歧为唯一研究者身份；在500篇天文学与物理学出版物上评估，以ORCID关联的第一作者、合著者与被引研究者作为代理标签，衡量与查询出版物存在已知关联的研究者被召回的情况。

Result: 检索最相似的前500篇论文时，算法大致召回68%的第一作者、60%的合著者与45%的被引研究者。

Conclusion: 该开源算法可有效从文献中识别领域相关研究者，为审稿人推荐与专家发现提供可行方案；代码已公开。

Abstract: The growing number of scientific submissions is straining the peer review process, while a large pool of relevant researchers remains unidentified and is therefore not considered as potential reviewers. We formulate the discovery of these researchers as a retrieval problem: given a submission, we retrieve and rank researchers based on domain similarity. We present retrieve-then-disambiguate, an open-source algorithm that identifies domain relevant researchers for a query submission by retrieving similar publications from the scientific literature and resolving ambiguous author names into unique researcher identities. We evaluate the algorithm on 500 astronomy and physics publications. We use ORCID-linked first authors, coauthors, and cited researchers as proxy labels to evaluate the recovery of researchers with known connections to each query publication. When retrieving the top 500 most similar papers, the algorithm recovers roughly 68\% of first authors, 60\% of coauthors, and 45\% of cited researchers. Our code is available at https://github.com/deepthought-initiative/Retrieve-then-Disambiguate.

</details>


### [214] [ProdCrawl: A Replayable Test Collection for Focused Crawling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40855)
*Manuel Rey-Escobar,David Otero,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出可复现的聚焦爬虫测试集 ProdCrawl（621个网站、约110万静态页面，页面标签由三个LLM多数投票生成），在完全相同页面与标签下对比基线策略，发现文本分类器与关键词匹配不显著优于广度优先，而语义嵌入方法优于所有简单策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚焦爬虫通常在真实网站上以harvest rate评估，存在两个问题：网站随时间变化导致抓取无法在相同条件下重复；且缺乏"哪些页面相关"的真实标注。因此爬虫之间的比较不可靠，亟需可重放、有统一标注的测试集合。

Method: 构建ProdCrawl：抓取621个公司/组织网站的静态镜像（约110万页面），以三个大语言模型多数投票将每页标注为产品页/非产品页；任何爬虫均可在同一站点、同一标签上运行与评分。在该集合上对比广度优先、深度优先、文本分类器，以及通过检索已标注目标页的相似链接文本为每条链接打分的链接评分方法（含神经嵌入语义比较）。

Result: 文本分类器与关键词匹配不显著优于广度优先抓取；基于语义相似度的神经嵌入方法优于所有简单策略。由于每次运行都在相同页面与标签上评估，结果完全可复现。

Conclusion: ProdCrawl为聚焦爬虫提供了可控、可重放、公平比较的静态测试平台与统一标签，消除了时间漂移与标注缺失带来的不可比性；公开数据集、代码库与模型以支持实验复现与公平对比。

Abstract: A focused crawler searches for web pages on a specific topic under a constrained download budget. Since it can only fetch a limited number of pages, its success depends on choosing which links to follow. Focused crawlers are usually evaluated on live websites by harvest rate, the fraction of downloaded pages that are relevant. This has two problems: on the one hand, websites change over time, so a crawl cannot be repeated under the same conditions; on the other hand, there is no ground truth for which pages are relevant. As a result, comparisons between crawlers are unreliable. We present ProdCrawl, a replayable test collection for focused crawling. It comprises static mirrors of 621 company and organization websites, about 1.1 million pages, with each page labeled as a product page or non-product page based on a majority vote among three large language models. Any crawler can be run on exactly the same sites and scored against the same labels. We use ProdCrawl to compare simple baselines (breadth-first, depth-first and text classifiers) with an approach that scores each link by searching for similar link texts whose target pages have already been labeled. Our results show that text classifiers and keyword matching do not significantly outperform breadth-first crawling, whereas neural embeddings, which compare link texts by semantic meaning, outperform all simple strategies. Because every run evaluates over identical pages and labels, these results are exactly reproducible. The collection, codebase, and models are publicly released to support experimental replication and fair crawler comparison.

</details>


### [215] [Context as Infrastructure: Knowledge Compilation for Retrieval in Agentic Systems with Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41172)
*Zhichang Yu,Baining Cao,Sbaaoui Idriss,Qi Wang,Gangqiang Xu,Ling Qin,Xiaoling Jin,An Liu,Shaohao Cai,Xiaoyang Bao,Shengle Chen,Changchang Ding,Pengfei Zhu,Jingxiang Xu,Yifeng Mao,Lu Liu,Hai Jin,Yingfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: agentic RAG的检索环节已基本解决准确率问题，真正瓶颈是上下文供给；作者提出"知识编译"作为供给层（非更好的检索器），以claim-evidence导航树为可测机制，在证据充分时削减91%查询token、平均降低38%，并给出决策门控将延迟从140s降到30s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: agentic检索（写查询—读结果—重写—停止）配合BM25/grep等轻量工具已能取得高准确率（BrowseComp-Plus 85%、FRAMES 91%），说明"找得准"不再是主要矛盾。新的瓶颈是上下文：不受约束的循环会重复查询、重读文档、把无用段落塞满窗口。因此缺少的是一层上下文供给/编译能力，而非更强的检索器。

Method: 提出可配置的LLM富化式摄取流水线，产出多种结构工件：带claim-evidence叶节点的导航层级、实体图、时间线、思维导图、本体、wiki与记忆。核心机制是以claim-evidence对为叶子的导航树，并配套决策门控（判断证据是否充分以决定是否继续检索）。在RAGFlow中把agent循环重构为五个"纪律化站点"，保证进入模型的上下文是最小充分集。作者以FRAMES与BrowseComp-Plus做对照评测，包括PageIndex式大纲的规模化压力测试。

Result: 结构工件在强harness下未带来可检测的FRAMES准确率增益，PageIndex式大纲在大规模下性能退化，说明编译不是更强检索器。claim-evidence导航树在证据充分时令查询token减少91%，不充分时反而增加1.9%，平均降38%；由此推出2%的盈亏平衡充分率，实测充分率约43%。决策门控把延迟从140s压至30s，但替换策略的token节省以更多检索轮次及额外延迟为代价。

Conclusion: 知识编译应定位为供给层而非检索层：其价值在准确率基准看不见的部署维度（治理、集成、上手、时间定向、连续性）。作者主张agent-native database范式——存储层向agent交付经过编译、治理的上下文，而非排序后的段落列表；RAGFlow实现已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation is becoming agentic. A search harness writes queries, reads results, reformulates, and decides when to stop. This largely solves accuracy: a harness with only BM25/grep and MiniMax M3 answers 85% of BrowseComp-Plus and 91% on multi-hop FRAMES in our runs, without compiled or vector indexes. It exposes a context problem: an undisciplined loop repeats queries, rereads documents, and fills the window with unused passages. The missing layer is knowledge compilation: a configurable ingestion pipeline where LLMs enrich documents with context no chunk contains alone--navigation hierarchies with claim-evidence leaves, entity graphs, timelines, mind maps, ontologies, wikis, and memory. Compilation is not a better retriever: under a competent harness, structural artifacts gave no detectable accuracy gain on FRAMES, and a PageIndex-style outline degraded at scale. It is a supply layer. A navigation tree whose leaves are claim-evidence pairs--the mechanism we measure--cuts query tokens by 91% when evidence suffices and adds 1.9% when it does not, a 38% average reduction; this implies a 2% break-even sufficiency rate and a measured rate near 43%. A decision gate cuts latency from 140 s to 30 s, though substitution's token saving is bought with retrieval rounds and carries its own latency cost. Other artifacts--ontology, graph, wiki, timeline, mind map, memory--pay off in deployment (governance, integration, onboarding, temporal orientation, continuity) where accuracy benchmarks cannot see them. We describe RAGFlow's pipeline and harness (open source at https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow), which turns the loop into five disciplined stations and keeps the context entering the model the smallest sufficient set; together they form an agent-native database: a store that hands the agent compiled, governed context instead of a ranked list of passages.

</details>


### [216] [MILES-Rec: Uncovering Implicit Intents from Mobility Behaviors for Cross-Domain POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41319)
*Yi Zou,Wei Luo,Xuewen Zhang,Zhuang Yan,Pengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 MILES-Rec 跨域 POI 推荐框架，从异构地图行为推断隐式服务意图并表达为按优先级排序的自然语言查询，配合意图对齐的群体相对策略优化与两级信用分配，并构建 MILES-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地图应用中用户行为是效用驱动的，只能提供间接且不完整的消费需求证据，难以从观测行为中推断个性化服务需求；同时需要将隐式意图转化为可执行的推荐查询。

Method: 1) 基于渐进式推理示范学习，融合用户画像与地图历史，完成情境理解、意图扩展与查询优先级排序；2) 提出意图对齐的群体相对策略优化（intent-aligned GRPO），以下游用户交互作为奖励信号，奖励查询相关性与排序服务覆盖率，惩罚冗余与过长回复；3) 两级信用分配机制，整合列表级共享优势与查询级优势，协调单条查询质量与整体列表的互补覆盖；4) 基于大规模地图平台真实交互日志构建 MILES-Bench，覆盖丰富与稀疏行为历史下的多种服务。

Result: 与通用 LLM 及专用推荐基线相比，在 POI 检索效用上更优，服务与语义对齐更强；行为策略优化在四种骨干规模上均带来一致性提升。

Conclusion: 将隐式服务意图显式化为优先级化自然语言查询是可行的跨域 POI 推荐范式，结合意图对齐的强化学习与多层次信用分配可持续提升推荐效果，且具备跨模型规模的可扩展性。

Abstract: Map applications bridge everyday mobility with local services, creating opportunities to recommend places by uncovering users' implicit intents. However, utility-driven map interactions provide only indirect and incomplete evidence of consumption demands, making it challenging to infer personalized service needs from observed behaviors. We propose MILES-Rec, a cross-domain POI recommendation framework that infers latent service intents from heterogeneous map behaviors and expresses them as prioritized natural language queries. It first learns from progressive reasoning demonstrations that synthesize user profiles and map histories for situation understanding, intent expansion, and query prioritization. We then introduce intent-aligned group relative policy optimization, using subsequent user interactions to reward query relevance and ranked service coverage while penalizing redundancy and excessive response length. A two-level credit assignment mechanism integrates shared list-level advantages with query-specific advantages, coordinating individual query quality with the complementary coverage of the complete query list. We further construct MILES-Bench from real user interaction logs on a large-scale mapping platform, supporting training and evaluation across diverse services under rich and sparse behavioral histories. Extensive experiments against general-purpose LLMs and specialized recommendation baselines demonstrate superior POI retrieval utility and strong service and semantic alignment, with consistent gains from behavioral policy optimization across four backbone scales.

</details>


### [217] [Pix2Evidence: A Multimodal RAG System for Text and Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41335)
*Ziniu Liu,Xiaoyang Chen,Dezhi Ye,Jie Liu,Jin Ma,Hao Deng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Pix2Evidence是一个多模态RAG系统，将截图瓦片与其转写文本融合为单一图文表示进行检索，并用VLM教师生成的证据描述适配检索器与重排序，在5个Wikipedia QA基准中4个答案准确率领先，MMSearch达26.0%（较PixelRAG高4.4个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉丰富文档上的RAG需同时找到相关内容与支撑答案的证据。截图检索保留图像与页面结构，但截图内文字事实也需可检索；同时仅凭主题相关性无法判断哪块瓦片真正含证据。

Method: 将每个截图瓦片与其可用转写编码为统一的融合图文表示，使版式与可读文本在检索阶段被联合建模；用视觉语言教师模型为每条训练问题描述其可见支撑内容，据此适配检索器，并在答案生成前对候选进行重排序。

Result: 在5个常用Wikipedia QA基准上，未重排序版本在4个基准的答案准确率上领先主对比；MMSearch上达26.0%，比PixelRAG高4.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 融合图文表示加证据导向的检索器适配与重排序能有效提升视觉丰富文档的RAG证据定位与问答表现，优于现有SOTA方法；代码与派生数据将在许可与隐私审查允许的范围内发布。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over visually rich documents must find both relevant content and the evidence that supports an answer. Screenshot retrieval preserves images and page structure, but written facts within screenshots also need to be searchable. Moreover, topical relevance alone does not identify which retrieved tile contains evidence for the answer. We introduce Pix2Evidence, a multimodal RAG system that encodes each screenshot tile together with its available transcription into a single fused image--text representation, so that layout and readable text are considered jointly during retrieval. To guide evidence selection, a vision-language teacher describes the visible content that supports each training question. We use these descriptions to adapt the retriever, then rerank the retrieved candidates before answer generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Pix2Evidence outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Across five widely-used Wikipedia QA benchmarks, the non-reranked Pix2Evidence leads the main comparison in answer accuracy on four. On MMSearch, it reaches 26.0\%, 4.4 percentage points above PixelRAG. We will release the code and share derived data where licensing and privacy reviews permit.

</details>


### [218] [Defining Page-Centric Visual Document Retrieval and Evaluating Existing Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41360)
*Chongzhi Zhang,Yuqi Zuo,Aixin Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文提出以页面为基本索引单元的"页面中心式视觉文档检索(VDR)"问题框架，区分页面/文档两种检索粒度与独立项、证据集、迭代三种检索范式，并通过42个基准的八维评估分析，指出现有VDR评测在任务设定与相关性标注上的覆盖缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的文档（Word、PDF、幻灯片）是视觉丰富、分页化的结构化单元序列，而纯文本检索系统要么忽视该结构，要么用任意段落切分将其破坏；同时现有VDR评测能力与真实应用需求之间存在错配，缺乏系统的基准审视。

Method: 提出页面中心的VDR形式化定义（页面为索引单元、文档上下文用于判断页面相关性），界定两种检索粒度与三种检索范式；随后以八项准则（语料特征、查询构建、相关性标注、排序支持等）系统评估42个基准。

Result: 两项主要发现：(1) 评测集中于页面级独立项检索，支持文档级、证据集与迭代设定的资源较少；(2) 源自真实用户信息需求且独立于目标语料内容构建的查询、以及分级相关性标注均不常见，相关性标注的覆盖度与验证程度差异显著。

Conclusion: 当前VDR评测协议无法确立应用所需的能力，作者给出基准选择指南，并提出未来基准设计的优先方向，强调任务需求、相关性语义与评测协议三者之间的对齐。

Abstract: In modern information retrieval (IR), documents are increasingly encountered as structured sequences of visually rich, paginated units, rather than as flat text. Example documents encountered in our daily life include Word files, PDFs, and slide decks. Free text-centric retrieval systems either ignore this structure entirely or destroy it through arbitrary passage chunking. In this paper, we formulate page-centric Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) with pages as the fundamental indexing units and document context available for assessing page relevance. The formulation distinguishes two retrieval granularities, page and document, and three retrieval paradigms: independent-item, evidence-set, and iterative retrieval. We then assess 42 benchmarks using eight criteria covering corpus characteristics, query construction, relevance annotation, and ranking support. Our analysis shows two main findings on current VDR research. First, evaluation remains concentrated on page-level independent-item retrieval, with fewer resources supporting document-level, evidence-set, and iterative settings. Second, queries originating from real user information needs independently of target corpus content and graded relevance judgments are uncommon, while the coverage and verification of relevance judgments vary substantially. Both findings reveal gaps between the capabilities applications require and those existing evaluation protocols can establish. We provide benchmark selection guidance and identify priorities for future benchmark design, emphasizing alignment between task requirements, relevance semantics, and evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [219] [BrowseFlow: Sparse Event Flow Matching for Generative User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41374)
*Yitian Pang,Jiayi Zhang,Zhenjia Ma,Xiaowei Yue,Dagui Chen,Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 BrowseFlow，一种条件离散流匹配方法用于生成式用户浏览模拟，将响应表示为浏览幅度与关键事件序列，直接建模关键事件的数量与位置，并用能量分数与Wasserstein距离评估响应保真度与生成多样性，同时发布 TaobaoBrowse 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实浏览模拟面临两大挑战：(i) 随机响应——同一用户面对相同推荐流仍可能产生不同浏览行为，而日志仅记录其中一条轨迹；(ii) 关键事件稀疏——点击、点赞等关键反馈在以上滑等推进动作为主的轨迹中占比极低，逐步稠密预测器使关键事件训练目标过少。

Method: BrowseFlow 为条件离散流匹配模型：用浏览幅度（extent）与关键事件的有序序列表示每次响应，在曝光约束下直接建模事件计数与位置；通过概率化幅度预测学习完整浏览响应的条件分布，并对关键事件施加离散流匹配；采用随机事件保留在部分事件序列上定义概率路径，配合转移速率匹配学习随机插入动态。

Result: 以能量分数（proper scoring rule）评估响应保真度与生成变异性，辅以关键事件计数分布间的Wasserstein距离；构建 TaobaoBrowse 基准，将真实推荐上下文与细粒度浏览轨迹配对；在三个数据集上 BrowseFlow 均取得最优的分布性能。

Conclusion: 将浏览响应解耦为幅度与关键事件序列的生成式建模，可有效应对随机响应与关键事件稀疏问题；配合分布级评估指标与专用基准，BrowseFlow 在分布保真与多样性上优于现有逐步模拟器。

Abstract: User simulation aims to reproduce how users interact with content and products across online platforms. Recent generative approaches have enriched user simulation with behavioral history and contextual information. However, realistic browsing simulation still faces two challenges. (i) Stochastic Responses: Even with the same recommendation feed, the same user may produce different browsing responses, yielding distinct behavioral trajectories. A logged trajectory records only one such realization. (ii) Sparse Key Events: Clicks and likes provide important feedback but occur sparsely within browsing trajectories dominated by progression actions such as scrolling. Many stepwise simulators predict these actions in a dense sequence, where key events contribute relatively few training targets. To address these challenges, we propose BrowseFlow, a conditional discrete flow matching method for generative user simulation. For Sparse Key Events, BrowseFlow represents each response by its browsing extent and an ordered sequence of key events, directly modeling their counts and locations under exposure constraints. BrowseFlow learns the conditional distribution of complete browsing responses through probabilistic extent prediction and discrete flow matching over key events. Random event retention defines probability paths over partial event sequences, while transition rate matching learns stochastic insertion dynamics. For Stochastic Responses, we use the energy score, a proper scoring rule, to evaluate response fidelity and generated variability, complemented by the Wasserstein distance between key event count distributions. We further introduce TaobaoBrowse, a benchmark pairing real recommendation contexts with fine-grained browsing trajectories. Experiments across three datasets demonstrate that BrowseFlow consistently achieves the best distributional performance.

</details>


### [220] [ProSearch: Benchmarking Multi-Constraint Protocol Retrieval in Experimental Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41396)
*Wenliang Liang,Zhiyuan Ning,Haolin Chen,Yuanchun Zhou,Wenjuan Cui,Yi Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出ProSearch——一个面向实验科学的多约束协议检索基准，包含1,515条查询、60个实验字段，覆盖六个领域；19个检索器中最优nDCG@10仅0.290（加reranking后0.474），揭示联合约束绑定是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI for Science中的实验设计与方法复现需要检索同时满足多个相互依赖约束的实验协议，但现有基准缺乏对该能力的系统性评测，存在评测空白。

Method: 构建流程包含四步：基于LLM的抽取、数据驱动的约束选择、受控查询生成、基于证据的相关性验证；基准具三大特性——真实性（查询源于真实实验记录与专家模板）、复杂性（60个实验字段及其依赖）、诊断性（每查询3-5个显式约束，含数值约束与否定约束）。

Result: 在1,515条跨六领域查询上评测19个代表性检索器：最佳模型平均nDCG@10仅0.290，经reranking提升至0.474。结构化诊断分析表明显式约束匹配是强排序信号，人工错误分析将联合约束绑定识别为关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: ProSearch为科学检索系统的多约束能力提供了系统化评测平台，指出了约束组合处理这一核心挑战与后续改进方向。

Abstract: In AI for Science scenarios, experimental design and method reproduction require retrieving protocols that satisfy multiple interdependent constraints, yet existing benchmarks lack systematic evaluation of this capability. To address this gap, we introduce ProSearch, a benchmark for multi-constraint protocol retrieval in experimental science. ProSearch has three key features: (1) realism, with queries grounded in real experimental records and expert-defined templates; (2) complexity, with 60 experimental fields and their dependencies; and (3) diagnosticity, with 3-5 explicit constraints per query, including numerical and negation constraints. We construct ProSearch through LLM-based extraction, data-driven constraint selection, controlled query generation, and evidence-based relevance validation. The resulting benchmark contains 1,515 queries across six experimental science domains. We conduct extensive experiments on ProSearch with 19 representative retrievers and complementary analyses. The best-performing retriever achieves an average nDCG@10 of only 0.290, which improves to 0.474 with reranking. Structured diagnostic analysis shows that explicit constraint matching provides a strong ranking signal, while manual error analysis identifies joint constraint binding as a key bottleneck. ProSearch provides a testbed for systematically evaluating scientific retrieval systems.

</details>


### [221] [How BERT-based Cross-Encoders Model Relevance in Information Retrieval?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41425)
*Mathias Vast,Basile Van Cooten,Laure Soulier,Benjamin Piwowarski*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过一系列可解释性实验，追踪三种 BERT-based cross-encoder 在不同阶段与不同输入片段之间的信息流，分析构成相关性预测的信号性质，并考察其在域内/域外数据上对 query 与 document 匹配的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cross-encoder 是信息检索中效果最好但效率最低的模型，其相关性预测机制（尤其 query-document 匹配过程）仍缺乏理解；这种不可解释性限制了模型优化、可能导致尤其是域外场景下的次优检索，并给过参数化模型带来效率隐患。

Method: 围绕两个未被充分探索的方面展开：(1) 追踪相关性预测过程中不同阶段、不同输入片段间的信息流，研究构成该信息流的信号性质及匹配信号的来源；(2) 考察模型面对域外文档和/或域外 query 时的行为。通过可解释性实验，评估三种规模与预训练策略不同的 BERT-based cross-encoder，覆盖域内与域外数据集。

Result: 实验刻画了 cross-encoder 在预测流程中信息流的组成与匹配信号的作用，并揭示了模型在域外文档/query 条件下行为的变化规律（具体量化结论需参见正文实验）。

Conclusion: 本研究既巩固了关于 cross-encoder 如何建模相关性的已有认识，又通过探索匹配信号的作用对其进行了拓展，为理解与优化 cross-encoder 及改善域外泛化提供了依据。

Abstract: Cross-encoders are the most effective but also the least efficient models in Information Retrieval. Despite their success, the mechanisms underlying their relevance predictions remain poorly understood, particularly how they process to match queries and documents. This lack of interpretability limits their optimization, can lead to suboptimal retrieval, especially out-of-domain, and raises efficiency concerns for over-parameterized models. To address these challenges, this paper investigates two under-explored aspects of cross-encoder behavior. First, to understand the relevance prediction process, we trace the flow of information at the different stages of the process and between different input segments. We study the nature of the signals that compose this flow and that allow cross-encoders to predict the relevance of a passage to a query. Our work particularly focuses on the matching performed by the cross-encoders between queries and documents. Second, we explore how cross-encoders behave with out-of-domain documents and/or queries. Through a series of interpretability experiments, we evaluate three BERT-based cross-encoder models, varying in size and pre-training strategies, across both in-domain and out-of-domain datasets. Overall, this study both consolidates what is known about how cross-encoders model relevance and extends it by exploring the role of matching signals.

</details>


### [222] [KITE: Retrieval as a Decision Procedure for Long-Term Conversational Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41521)
*Feng Li,Kailun Tan,Zhibo Zhou,Youxiang Wang,Lejun Liu,Huaigu Li,Fei Du,Huilong An*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KITE：一个无向量检索的LLM智能体长期记忆框架，将记忆检索形式化为显式决策过程，在LoCoMo与LongMemEval-S上分别取得93.5%与86.0%准确率，每题仅约1.6k读者上下文token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体长期记忆系统多基于嵌入检索，相似度排序只能保证语义匹配，无法判定检索到的记忆是否真正满足问题隐含的条件，缺乏可检验性。

Method: 提出KITE（Knowledge-Indexed Temporal Evidence）：把多会话对话组织为由受控主题词表索引的结构化知识，该词表通过受控扩展与合并演化；查询时，将问题编译为作用于同一知识索引的可检视检索计划，通过固定的查找与排序操作执行，并以稀疏词法检索补回结构化表示中遗漏的表面细节。

Result: 在LoCoMo上达93.5%准确率，LongMemEval-S上达86.0%，每题仅约1.6k读者上下文token。

Conclusion: 对话记忆可超越相似度排序：围绕受控索引统一记忆构建与查询编译，使检索成为可检视的决策过程，从而判定哪些证据满足问题。

Abstract: Many long-term memory systems for LLM agents are built around embedding-based retrieval. Al- though effective for semantic matching, similarity ranking alone does not establish whether re- trieved memories satisfy the conditions implied by a question. In this paper, we introduce KITE (Knowledge-Indexed Temporal Evidence), a vector-free framework that formulates memory re- trieval as an explicit decision procedure. KITE organizes multi-session conversations as structured knowledge indexed by a governed topic vocabulary, which evolves through controlled expansion and consolidation. At query time, questions are compiled into inspectable retrieval plans over the same knowledge index that guides memory construction. The plans are then executed through fixed lookup and ranking operations, while sparse lexical retrieval recovers surface details omitted from the structured representation. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show that KITE achieves 93.5% and 86.0% accuracy while using only about 1.6k reader-context tokens per ques- tion. These results demonstrate that conversational memory can move beyond similarity ranking by unifying memory construction and query compilation around a governed index, turning retrieval into an inspectable decision procedure for determining which evidence satisfies the question.

</details>


### [223] [Towards Trustworthy Multi-stakeholder Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41674)
*Katarina Marcincinova,Adrian Gavornik,Matus Mesarcik,Michal Kompan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文指出多目标、多利益相关方推荐系统虽已有研究，但其可信性（trustworthiness）方面关注不足，并梳理了迈向可信多利益相关方推荐系统过程中需解决的开放挑战与关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统需同时反映并协调数十个利益相关方往往相互冲突的需求与期望；且每个利益相关方可能同时拥有多种目标，使系统运行环境复杂多样。现有文献已研究多目标与多利益相关方推荐系统，但对这类系统的可信性维度关注有限，而可信性正逐渐成为新标准。

Method: 立场/展望型论文：通过文献综述与问题识别，聚焦多利益相关方推荐系统与可信性要求的交叉领域，归纳开放挑战并提出关键研究问题。

Result: 识别出多利益相关方推荐系统在可信性方面尚存的研究空白，系统性地列出该方向待解决的开放挑战与关键问题。

Conclusion: 构建可信的多利益相关方推荐系统是必要且紧迫的研究方向，需要社区在协调多方冲突目标的同时纳入可信性维度，并围绕所提出的开放问题开展后续研究。

Abstract: Recommender system (RS) nowadays has to reflect, combine, and solve often contrasting requirements and expectations from dozens of stakeholders. In fact, the problem is even more complicated as each of the stakeholders can simultaneously have various objectives creating a diverse and complicated environment with several stakeholders and their objectives in which a RS operates. To address this problems the Multi-objective and Multi-stakeholder RS have been studied in the literature. However, a limited attention has been paid to trustworthiness aspects of such RS, which slowly becomes a new standard. In this paper, we highlight open challenges and important questions which need to be addressed on the way to trustworthy Multi-stakeholder RS.

</details>


### [224] [Search When Needed, Proceed When Reliable: Metacognitive Reliability Supervision for Efficient Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41738)
*Minhao Sun,Zhijing Wu,Xian-Ling Mao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出元认知框架，通过估计中间推理状态可靠性来训练LLM的检索决策，并与GRPO结合实现更优的准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于最终答案正确性或轨迹级反馈的监督只能间接反映中间决策状态的可靠性，且轨迹表现同时受随机生成、检索质量和下游推理影响，无法区分"是否需要检索"与"检索是否真正带来增益"。

Method: 构建元认知框架，估计局部结论的可靠性：当前状态可靠性分数判断模型能否不检索直接作答，检索动作分数衡量检索证据对可靠性的提升幅度；两者组合成可靠性感知的过程奖励，用于GRPO训练。

Result: 在七个问答基准上，方法取得优于强agentic search基线的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 将奖励信号从结果级转向中间状态可靠性，可为检索与不检索行为提供更细粒度监督，实现更高效的agentic search。

Abstract: Efficient agentic search requires large language models (LLMs) to determine whether they can reliably proceed with their current knowledge or need additional external evidence. Supervision based on final-answer correctness or trajectory-level feedback evaluates realized trajectory performance. However, observed trajectory performance is jointly shaped by stochastic generation, retrieval quality, and downstream reasoning. Such signals therefore provide only indirect evidence of how reliable the current decision state was and how retrieved evidence changed its reliability. An effective reward should encourage search when external information is needed and favor searches that contribute to the answer. To this end, we propose a metacognitive framework for training LLMs to make search decisions based on the reliability of their intermediate reasoning states. Our framework estimates the reliability of local conclusions to distinguish whether the current information supports proceeding without search from how much retrieved evidence improves that reliability. The current-state reliability score assesses whether the model can proceed without search. The search-action score measures how retrieved evidence changes estimated reliability. Together, these scores define a reliability-aware process reward for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), providing finer-grained supervision for search and no-search behavior. Experiments across seven question-answering benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off than strong agentic-search baselines. Our code and model checkpoints will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [225] [Completeness and Cost of Retrieval Structures for Multi-Tool Requests in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41905)
*Shuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了六种工具检索结构在多工具（多目标）查询下的完整性与检索成本，发现未经测试集训练且每查询至多一次LLM调用的结构在k=16时完整率不足10%，而少量域内样本训练集级检索器可在无LLM调用下达到最高完整率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索器多以单工具查询为构建与评测基础，但实际智能体需调用多个工具，检索器漏掉任一必需工具即导致任务失败；同时聚合完整性指标掩盖了单目标与多目标两种截然不同的检索难度 regime，需按目标数量分层评估并计入检索时开销。

Method: 在345条带专家依赖标签的AppWorld查询与257个MCP工具上的103个MCP-Bench任务上，对比六类检索结构：扁平词汇检索、稠密检索、LLM查询分解、LLM导航式层级检索、先服务器后工具匹配、依赖图、以及训练的集合级检索器；指标为completeness@k（完整工具集出现在top-k的比例），并记录检索期LLM调用数、token与延迟；另在全部7,961条ToolRet查询上做分层分析。

Result: ToolRet分析显示completeness@10在单目标查询为.58–.67，而目标数≥4的查询仅.08–.17，且检索器在单目标上的排序在多目标上发生反转。两个测试集上，未经域内训练且每查询至多一次LLM调用的结构在k=16时完整率均不超过10%；域内75–90条查询训练的集合级检索器在AppWorld达.33、MCP-Bench达.58且零LLM调用，LLM导航层级检索达.30与.40但每查询中位需31–38次LLM调用、约5分钟。失败模式不同：训练检索器常将缺失工具排在截断阈值稍下方，层级检索则定位到目标API后即停止、未收集其前置依赖。下游验证中，检索集完整的AppWorld任务21/21成功，不完整的39个任务仅2个成功。

Conclusion: 多工具检索是独立于单工具检索的问题，聚合指标具误导性；以少量域内样本训练的集合级检索器在成本与完整性上最优，LLM导航式层级检索有效但代价高昂；检索完整性直接决定下游智能体成功率，应作为一等评测目标。

Abstract: An agent that must call several tools to fulfill a request fails when the tool retriever omits any one of them, yet tool retrievers are mostly built and evaluated on queries requiring a single tool. We compare representatives of six retrieval structures (flat lexical and dense retrieval, LLM query decomposition, LLM-navigated hierarchies, server-then-tool matching, dependency graphs, and trained set-level retrievers) across two multi-tool testbeds: 345 AppWorld queries with expert dependency labels and 103 MCP-Bench tasks over 257 MCP tools. We measure completeness@k, the share of queries whose entire required tool set appears in the top k, alongside the LLM calls, tokens, and latency that each structure incurs at retrieval time. A motivating analysis on all 7,961 ToolRet queries shows that aggregate completeness mixes two regimes: completeness at 10 is .58 to .67 on single-target queries and .08 to .17 on queries with four or more targets, and the ordering of retrievers on single-target queries reverses on multi-target ones. Across the two testbeds, none of the compared structures that is untrained on the testbed and makes at most one LLM call per query completes more than 10% of AppWorld queries at k = 16. The two structures that do better incur different costs: a set-level retriever trained on 75 to 90 in-domain queries reaches .33 on AppWorld and .58 on MCP-Bench with no LLM calls, while an LLM-navigated hierarchy reaches .30 and .40 with a median of 31 to 38 LLM calls and about five minutes per query. Their failure modes also differ: the trained retriever mostly ranks the missing tools just below the cutoff, whereas the hierarchy finds the target API and stops before collecting its prerequisites. In a downstream check with the official AppWorld agent, tasks with a complete retrieved set succeeded 21 of 21 times, whereas incomplete ones succeeded 2 of 39 times.

</details>


### [226] [Recommendation Feedback Loops Shape Urban Income Segregation and Venue Visitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41911)
*Guangyuan Weng,Lin Chen,Ciro Beneduce,Giovanni Mauro,Massimiliano Luca,Takahiro Yabe,Bruno Lepri,Luca Pappalardo,Esteban Moro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文基于美国四大都市区细粒度出行数据构建含距离与容量约束的长期仿真框架，揭示基于位置的推荐系统形成的人机反馈回路会带来"体验性收入隔离"与"场所消失"两大城市后果，并提出偏好冷门场所的重排序干预以同时缓解二者。核心主张是推荐系统评估应超越离线准确率，纳入反馈回路与城市社会影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台通常以历史访问的离线准确率评价基于位置的推荐系统，但该测试中推荐不会改变人们去哪，因而无法刻画长期效应。真实城市环境不同于线上资源：出行距离约束与场所容量限制共同决定可及性，且每次被推荐的访问都会改变其他访客的相遇结构，形成人机反馈回路，进而影响跨收入群体的场所共享与场所的持续获客。现有研究缺乏能够同时刻画这些特征的长期评估手段。

Method: 构建基于美国四个都市区细粒度人类移动数据的长期仿真框架，显式引入距离与容量约束，以捕捉出行可行性与场所拥挤的外部性；聚焦餐饮场所，围绕两个城市后果展开测量：(1) 体验性收入隔离，即不同收入群体实际接触的场所分布不均衡程度；(2) 场所消失率，即未获得任何模拟居民访问的场所比例。实验中对比多种算法与多个都市区，并通过消融区分"重训练"与算法本身对后果的贡献；随后在平台既有算法之上叠加一种偏好低热度场所的重排序干预并进行测试。

Result: 在所有算法与所有都市区中，至少五分之一的场所不再获得访问，且该损失中超过一半源于重训练；算法越准确，人群隔离越严重，最准确的算法造成的隔离比真实情况高15%–21%。叠加的冷门场所偏好重排序干预在各类算法下同时降低场所消失与隔离，且基本不会把人们导向其舒适度较低的场所。

Conclusion: 基于位置推荐系统的准确率指标会系统性低估其长期城市后果：反馈回路下高准确率与更高隔离、更多场所消失相伴。研究建议推荐系统的影响评估应纳入反馈回路，并在准确率之外报告隔离与场所消失等指标；轻量重排序干预可在不显著损害用户体验的前提下改善上述社会后果。

Abstract: Location-based recommender systems shape where people go in cities and learn from the visits they steer, forming a human-AI feedback loop. This feedback loop shapes whom people share places with across income groups and which venues keep attracting customers. Yet platforms judge these systems by their offline accuracy on past visits, a test in which the suggestions never change where people go and which therefore cannot capture long-term effects. Studying these effects in cities is challenging because, unlike online resources, travel constraints and limited venue capacity shape where people can go, while each recommended visit also changes whom other visitors encounter. To capture such features, we build a long-term simulation framework based on fine-grained human mobility data from four U.S. metropolitan areas, with distance and capacity constraints. Focusing on food venues, we study two urban consequences: how unevenly people's exposure is distributed across income groups (experienced income segregation) and the share of venues receiving no visits from simulated residents (venue disappearance). We find that, under every algorithm and in every metropolitan area, at least a fifth of venues stop receiving visits, and that more than half of this loss arises from retraining. More importantly, more accurate algorithms leave people more segregated, and the most accurate algorithm leaves people 15-21% more segregated than they actually are. We then test a re-ranking intervention that favours less popular venues and runs on top of a platform's existing algorithm. It lowers both venue disappearance and segregation across algorithms, largely without directing people to venues where they may feel less comfortable. Our results suggest that impact assessments of recommender systems should account for the feedback loop and report segregation and venue disappearance beyond accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [An Empirical Study of Gas Optimization Practices in Solidity Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40885)
*Francesco Salzano,Paolo Martino,Simone Scalabrino,Rocco Oliveto,Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对2018–2025年GitHub开源Solidity仓库中的gas优化实践进行实证研究，基于400个代表性commit构建了包含8个宏观类别、36个子类别的gas优化策略分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以太坊中每个计算操作都有直接经济成本，gas效率对DApp的经济可持续性与推广至关重要；但理论优化模式与开发者实际实践之间存在明显差距，缺乏对真实项目中gas优化实践的实证认识。

Method: 采用挖掘软件仓库（MSR）方法，通过多阶段流水线（基于NLP的过滤＋人工定性检查）从GitHub开源Solidity仓库中识别gas相关commit，并对400个代表性commit进行定性分析，归纳出实证分类法。

Result: 得到8个宏观类别、36个子类别的gas优化策略分类法；优化努力跨越多个抽象层次，从底层算术改进、字节码体积缩减，到逻辑内化、减少外部调用等架构级变更；开发者常在执行频率、安全检查与计算成本之间权衡，优化高度依赖上下文。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来开发者支持工具的设计提供了实用知识库，并推进了对区块链领域成本感知软件工程的理解。

Abstract: In the Ethereum ecosystem, smart contract execution is governed by a gas model where every computational operation incurs a direct economic cost. Consequently, gas efficiency is a critical requirement for the economic sustainability and widespread adoption of decentralized applications. Despite the importance of cost-effective development, there remains a significant gap between theoretical optimization patterns and the actual practices applied by developers in real-world projects. This paper presents an empirical study investigating gas optimization practices in open-source Solidity repositories created on GitHub between 2018 and 2025. Adopting a Mining Software Repositories approach, we analyzed a dataset of gas-related commits identified through a multi-stage pipeline involving NLP-based filtering and manual qualitative inspection. Based on the analysis of 400 representative commits, we derive an empirical taxonomy of gas optimization strategies consisting of 8 macro-categories and 36 subcategories. Our findings reveal that optimization efforts span multiple levels of abstraction, from low-level arithmetic refinements and bytecode-size reductions to broader architectural changes such as logic internalization and external call reduction. Furthermore, we observe that developers often manage complex trade-offs between execution frequency, safety checks, and computational costs, highlighting that effective optimization is inherently context-dependent. This study provides a practical knowledge base for the design of future developer support tools and advances the understanding of cost-aware software engineering in the blockchain domain.

</details>


### [228] [AIFaultBench: A Reproducible Benchmark of Real-World Faults in AI Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41094)
*Mehil B Shah,Mohammad Masudur Rahman,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 AIFaultBench——包含 105 个 AI 软件仓库、770 个真实缺陷的基准，覆盖六类 AI 系统，并提供结构化元数据、环境脚本、复现触发与调查证据；在记录环境下成功复现 652 个（84.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 软件失效涉及代码、模型、数据、依赖、硬件与运行环境之间的交互，调试困难；现有基准仅覆盖特定类型 AI 系统，且存在故障真实性与可复现性方面的不足，缺乏对执行条件与证据的规范化记录。

Method: 构建跨 76 个组织、105 个仓库的 770 个真实缺陷基准，覆盖机器学习、深度学习、强化学习、大语言模型系统、智能体 AI、AI 工具与基础设施六类系统；每条缺陷记录将原始报告与受影响代码库关联到结构化元数据、环境搭建脚本、复现触发器和调查证据，并逐一在记录执行条件下尝试复现。

Result: 成功复现 652 个缺陷（84.7%），并完整记录其余 118 次复现尝试的结果与原因。

Conclusion: 凭借广泛覆盖、可追溯的复现证据与统一结构，AIFaultBench 可支撑 AI 缺陷实证研究、调试工具评估、实际故障调查以及基于真实案例的教学。

Abstract: AI-based software systems remain difficult to debug because their failures can involve interactions among code, models, data, dependencies, hardware, and execution environments. Existing benchmarks cover specific classes of AI systems, while concerns about fault realism and reproducibility highlight the need for documented execution conditions and evidence. We present AIFaultBench, a benchmark of 770 real-world bugs from 105 AI-based software repositories across 76 organizations. It covers six classes of AI systems: machine learning, deep learning, reinforcement learning, systems based on large language models, agentic AI, and AI tooling and infrastructure. Each bug record connects the original report and affected codebase to structured metadata, environment setup scripts, a reproduction trigger, and investigation evidence. We reproduced 652 bugs (84.7%) under the recorded execution conditions and documented the outcomes of the remaining 118 attempts. Through its broad coverage, documented reproduction evidence, and common structure, AIFaultBench supports empirical studies of AI faults, debugging tool evaluation, practical failure investigation, and teaching with real-world examples.

</details>


### [229] [RepGen: Reproducing Deep Learning Bugs through Context-Aware Code Generation and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41097)
*Mehil B Shah,Mohammad Masudur Rahman,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepGen 是一个开源工具，通过上下文感知的代码生成与迭代验证来复现深度学习缺陷；在 106 个真实缺陷中复现 85 个（80.19%），开发者受控实验中复现成功率提升 23.35%、用时减少 56.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习缺陷的复现具有挑战性：失败可能涉及源码、模型、数据、依赖与执行环境等多个层面；同时缺陷报告常缺失关键细节，进一步增加复现难度。

Method: 给定缺陷报告与代码仓库，RepGen 探索代码库以构建学习增强的上下文，制定复现计划并生成候选脚本；随后通过静态检查与 LLM 反馈迭代精化候选脚本，提升其正确性与相关性。可选地，工具自动准备执行环境、运行脚本，并将输出与报告中描述的失败进行比对判定。工具提供交互式命令行界面，支持本地与托管 LLM、可配置执行预算与可检视产物，并提供 GitHub Action 实现问题处理的自动化。

Result: 该方法在 106 个真实深度学习缺陷上复现了 85 个，成功率 80.19%；27 名开发者参与的受控实验显示复现成功率提升 23.35%，复现耗时减少 56.8%。

Conclusion: RepGen 表明，借助上下文感知的代码生成与迭代验证，并结合环境准备与结果判定，可显著提升深度学习缺陷复现的自动化程度、成功率和效率，并可作为开源工具落地于实际缺陷处理流程。

Abstract: Reproducing deep learning (DL) bugs is challenging because failures can involve source code, models, data, dependencies, and execution environments. Incomplete bug reports further complicate reproduction by omitting essential details. We present RepGen, an open-source tool for reproducing DL bugs through context-aware code generation and iterative verification. Given a bug report and repository, RepGen explores the codebase to build a learning-enhanced context, develop a reproduction plan, and generate candidate scripts. It refines candidates using static checks and LLM feedback to improve their correctness and relevance. When enabled, the tool prepares the execution environment, runs the script, and assesses its output against the reported failure. RepGen provides an interactive command-line interface supporting local and hosted LLMs, configurable execution budgets, and inspectable artifacts, along with a GitHub Action for automated issue processing. The underlying approach reproduced 85 of 106 real-world DL bugs (80.19%). A controlled study with 27 developers showed a 23.35% improvement in reproduction success and a 56.8% reduction in reproduction time.

</details>


### [230] [One Verdict Is Not Enough: Regression Testing Learned World Models and Their Test Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41130)
*Junyu Huo,Zihao Wan,Ziqi Mao,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TetraState——一种针对"成对可能/不可能视频测试"的回归测试协议：每个测试在四种固定的模型原生配置下运行，按正确判定的数量归入四类结果，并将不确定性测试与执行失败单独报告，同时把测试框架本身纳入被测系统的版本管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型世界模型以软件组件形式发布，模型版本、预处理与测试框架在每次发布间都会变化。现有的模型更新研究只统计逐样本回归（negative flips），而准确率检查会掩盖这些问题。仍有两个未解问题：(1) 同一测试在一种配置下通过、在另一种配置下失败；(2) 测试框架故障可能在不崩溃的情况下产生错误分数与错误结论。

Method: TetraState 协议：(a) 每个测试在四种固定的模型原生配置下执行；(b) 依据正确判定数量将测试归入四种结果类别；(c) 将"不确定"测试与"执行失败"与判定率分开报告；(d) 通过逐测试的结果转移比较不同修订版；(e) 将测试框架作为被测系统的一部分进行版本控制。实验在 IntPhys 2 Main 全部 506 个测试上进行，覆盖 Cosmos 3 Nano 与 V-JEPA 2 两个异构系统。

Result: 两个 V-JEPA 2 测试框架修订版仅使众数准确率变化 0.2 与 2.6 个百分点，却分别改变了 11 与 214 个测试标签；在第二例中，准确率上升，但被正确判定的测试数量反而减少；另有 43 个回归仍保持众数判定，因此仅检查众数判定翻转会漏检这些回归。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于学习型组件，需要以"配置级"回归判据（configuration-level regression oracle）作为未来研究方向；仅靠准确率或众数判定翻转不足以可靠地检测模型更新与框架修订带来的回归。

Abstract: Learned world models ship as software components whose model versions, preprocessing, and test harnesses change between releases. Work on model updates already counts per-sample regressions, or negative flips, that an accuracy check hides. Two problems remain: a test can pass under one configuration and fail under another, and a harness fault can produce erroneous scores and wrong verdicts without a crash. We present TetraState, a regression-testing protocol for paired possible/impossible video tests. Each test runs under four fixed model-native configurations; its number of correct verdicts assigns one of four outcome classes. Inconclusive tests and failed executions are reported separately alongside the verdict rate. Revisions are compared by per-test outcome transitions, and the harness is versioned as part of the system under test. On all 506 IntPhys 2 Main tests and two heterogeneous systems, Cosmos 3 Nano and V-JEPA 2, two V-JEPA 2 harness revisions move modal accuracy by only 0.2 and 2.6 points yet change 11 and 214 test labels; in the second, accuracy rises although fewer tests are judged correctly, and 43 regressions keep the modal verdict, so a modal-verdict flip check misses them. These results outline a research agenda on configuration-level regression oracles for learned components.

</details>


### [231] [Lineage Is Memory: Parent Inheritance and Feedback Disclosure in Short-Budget LLM-Guided Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41157)
*Shanglin Yang,Zhan Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在1750次预注册实验中，作者将"父代继承"与"每轮反馈广度"解耦，发现贪心父代继承是迭代式提示/程序优化器绝大部分性能提升的来源；全量错误聚类等机制只在单步改写和搜索初期有益，很快被父代继承追平，且反思开销只占总成本很小一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作把迭代语言模型优化器的性能瓶颈归因于单轮反思过窄，并提出全量错误聚类、定向小批量、更大提议模型等机制，但这些评测都在保留谱系（lineage-preserving）的搜索中进行，每个修订只编辑一个存活父代，导致父代继承的贡献与每轮反馈广度的贡献混杂，无法判断谁在真正驱动优化进展。

Method: 在九个提示与程序合成基准上开展1750次预注册运行，在匹配的评估预算下把父代继承与反馈披露分离：对比贪心父代继承与"种子重置重采样"（每轮从种子重新提议），并让后者获得全量失败聚类摘要或使用更大的提议模型；同时把GEPA的token支出分解为提议反思、验证与小批量门控三部分。

Result: 贪心父代继承驱动了绝大部分优化进展，显著优于种子重置重采样，即便后者每轮收到全部失败的聚类摘要或使用更大提议模型也是如此。全量错误聚类与多样化失败选择能改善从种子出发的单步改写并加速搜索开局，但随着已解决错误模式被保留在候选中并从后续失败列表中滤除，父代继承很快弥合这一差距。GEPA的token分解显示提议反思只占总成本很小比例，验证与小批量门控几乎消耗全部预算。

Conclusion: 迭代优化的增益主要来自贪心父代继承而非复杂的反思机制；对原始失败做贪心父代继承即可在远低得多的计算量下获得复杂反思循环的收益，提示后续优化器设计应优先保证有效继承与低成本门控，而非堆叠反馈聚合或更大提议模型。

Abstract: Iterative language-model optimizers refine prompts and programs by evaluating a candidate, showing a proposer model a sample of failing cases, and keeping revisions that improve performance. Recent work attributes the limits of this loop to narrow single-round reflection, introducing full-set error clustering, targeted minibatches, or larger proposer models so the optimizer sees more failure modes per step. Yet every existing evaluation tests these mechanisms exclusively inside lineage-preserving search, where each revision edits a surviving parent, leaving the contribution of parent inheritance confounded with the breadth of per-round feedback. Across 1,750 pre-registered runs spanning nine prompt and program synthesis benchmarks, we separate parent inheritance from feedback disclosure under matched evaluation budgets. We find that greedy parent inheritance drives the vast majority of optimization progress, outperforming seed-reset resampling even when seed-reset proposals receive a clustered summary of all failures in every round or use a larger proposer model. While full-set error clustering and diverse failure selection improve single-step rewrites from the seed and accelerate the opening rounds of search, sequential parent inheritance rapidly closes this gap as solved error modes are retained in the candidate and filtered from subsequent failure lists. Moreover, decomposing token expenditure in GEPA reveals that proposer reflection accounts for only a small fraction of total cost whereas validation and minibatch gating consume nearly the entire budget, showing that greedy parent inheritance over raw failures captures the gains of complex reflective loops at a fraction of their compute.

</details>


### [232] [ROSTrans: Empirically Benchmarking LLMs for ROS1-to-ROS2 Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41168)
*Yichen Zhao,Minghao Shao,Zhuoyan Yu,Yijun Shen,Boyuan Chen,Yik-Cheung Tam,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ROSTrans 是一个评估大语言模型 ROS1→ROS2 代码迁移能力的基准，包含 108 个来自 70+ 开源仓库的脚手架补全任务，按接口级/行为级/系统级正确性分层，并为每个任务提供可执行参考实现与运行时测试。六个前沿 LLM 的 pass@1 均低于 10.9%，56.5% 的样本编译成功但运行时失败，执行引导修复可将成功率提升至约 49.1%（Claude Opus 4.6）与 47.2%（GPT-5.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在领域代码生成上表现强劲，但在机器人软件开发（尤其 ROS）场景尚缺乏系统评估。ROS 代码生成涉及节点间协调、中间件配置与生命周期语义，远超常规编码基准；且诸多正确性属性只有在节点启动并通信后才可观测，现有编译式评测无法覆盖。

Method: 构建 ROSTrans 基准，聚焦 ROS1→ROS2 迁移这一代表性场景：108 个脚手架补全任务，源自 70+ 开源 ROS1 仓库，按三级层次分类法（接口级、行为级、系统级正确性）组织。每个任务配备可执行参考：经核验的 ROS2 实现、启动节点并覆盖 topic/service/action/parameter 的运行时测试套件，以及保证可复现的容器。评分时将候选产出的目标文件注入固定参考包的副本，候选无法提供或削弱用于评判的测试。评估六个前沿 LLM，每任务五次随机生成。

Result: 六个前沿 LLM 的 pass@1 均低于 10.9%；56.5% 的样本构建成功但在运行时失败；32.0% 在迁移接口的运行时断言上失败——此类错误任何仅编译的判据（oracle）都无法检出。执行引导修复三轮后，Claude Opus 4.6 与 GPT-5.5 的任务成功率分别达 49.1% 与 47.2%。

Conclusion: ROS 代码生成对当前 LLM 仍是难题，核心瓶颈在运行时行为正确性而非编译通过；以执行反馈驱动的修复可显著提升成功率，说明基于运行时验证的评测与迭代修复是提升 ROS 迁移可靠性的关键方向。

Abstract: Although large language models have shown strong capabilities in domain-specific code generation, their performance in robotics software development, especially for the Robot Operating System (ROS), remains largely unexplored. ROS code generation presents distinctive challenges, including complex inter-node coordination, middleware configuration, and lifecycle semantics that go far beyond standard coding benchmarks, and many important correctness properties become observable only once migrated nodes are launched and communicate. We present ROSTrans, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on ROS1-to-ROS2 code migration, a representative and practically important setting for ROS code generation. ROSTrans comprises 108 scaffolded completion tasks drawn from a diverse set of 70+ open-source ROS1 repositories and organized into a hierarchical taxonomy assessing interface-level, behavioral-level and system-level correctness. For every task we establish an executable reference: a verified ROS2 implementation, a runtime test suite that launches the nodes and exercises their topics, services, actions and parameters, and a container ensuring reproducibility. Candidates are scored by injecting only their target files into an otherwise fixed copy of the reference package, so a candidate can neither supply nor weaken the tests it is judged by. Evaluating six frontier LLMs over five stochastic generations per task, we find pass@1 below 10.9%, and that 56.5% of all samples build successfully and then fail at runtime,and 32.0% fail runtime assertions on the migrated interfaces, a class of error no compile-only oracle can detect. Execution-guided repair further improves task success, reaching 49.1% and 47.2% for Claude Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.5 after three rounds, respectively.

</details>


### [233] [Cognitive Inclusivity in AI Coding Assistants: Evidence from GitHub Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41192)
*Grace Kerr,Aarushi Parasrampuria,Faith Culas,Kelly Blincoe*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过20名学生使用GitHub Copilot完成代码生成、调试与重构任务的受控实验，结合出声思维、半结构化访谈与主题分析，并运用GenderMag框架，识别出5类认知包容性问题，指出主流交互范式偏向特定认知风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注AI编程助手对生产力与代码质量的影响，而较少探讨其如何支持不同思维方式；非AI软件工程工具的证据表明工具设计与用户认知风格不匹配会带来包容性障碍，但在AI编程助手中是否存在类似问题尚不清楚。

Method: 开展20名学生参与的受控实验室研究，使用GitHub Copilot完成代码生成、调试和重构任务；采用出声思维协议、半结构化访谈与归纳式主题分析识别交互模式，再应用GenderMag框架分析不同认知风格间的差异。

Result: 识别出五类包容性问题，涉及Copilot如何解释建议、支持验证、促进学习、暴露功能以及影响问题解决过程。

Conclusion: AI编程助手的常见交互范式可能优待某些认知风格，同时给其他认知风格用户带来额外认知负担；据此提出面向更具认知包容性的AI编程助手的设计启示。

Abstract: AI coding assistants are commonly used for daily software development tasks. While prior research has examined their impact on productivity and code quality, less is understood about how these tools support diverse ways of thinking. Evidence from non-AI software engineering tools suggests that mismatches between tool design and users' cognitive styles can introduce inclusivity barriers, but it remains unclear whether similar issues arise in AI coding assistants. We investigate how developers with different cognitive styles experience and interact with these tools and whether they exhibit cognitive inclusivity challenges. We conducted a controlled laboratory study with 20 student participants using GitHub Copilot to complete code generation, debugging, and refactoring tasks. Using think-aloud protocols, semi-structured interviews, and inductive thematic analysis, we identify patterns in user interaction. We then apply the GenderMag framework to examine how these patterns differ across cognitive styles. We identify five inclusivity issues concerning how Copilot explains suggestions, supports validation, facilitates learning, exposes its features, and influences the problem-solving process. Our findings suggest that common interaction paradigms in AI coding assistants can privilege certain cognitive styles while introducing additional cognitive effort for others. We derive design implications for more inclusive AI coding assistants.

</details>


### [234] [Yesterday's Code, Today's Dependencies: How Dependency Selection Shapes PHP Migration Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41263)
*Afridi Mahmud,Md. As-Aid Rahman,Rifat Shahriyar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 依赖版本选择策略会同时改变旧项目迁移前的初始测试结果与迁移中可观察到的失败类型，PHP 7.4→8.3 迁移基准须显式声明并报告该选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移基准假设项目在迁移前测试通过，但今日重装同一未改动源码会引入更新的依赖库，从而改变初始测试结果及迁移过程中观察到的失败，削弱基准的有效性与可比性；作者因此要量化依赖选择策略的影响。

Method: 在 PHP 7.4→8.3 迁移场景下，固定源码与已保存的包记录，固定 Composer 2，对比两种依赖策略：现代选择（允许快照中所有版本）与发布日期选择（仅允许项目发布日之前发布的版本）；并在3,660个release上统计 PHP 7.4 通过率，另与已提交的 lock 文件对比版本复现程度；对双策略下 PHP 7.4 均通过的项目，统一使用共享 test runner 并绕过 Composer 平台检查，隔离测试行为差异。

Result: 发布日期选择使 PHP 7.4 通过率提升 7.1 个百分点（3,660 个 release）；相比现代选择，它更接近已提交 lock 中记录的依赖版本。在双策略下 PHP 7.4 均通过的项目对中，PHP 8.3 测试结果有 15.0% 不同，候选迁移任务集仅重叠 58.5%；差异多集中于 Laravel 项目，源于旧依赖触发的弃用警告而被新版本规避。针对性移除这些初始失败后，可恢复测试通过，或进一步暴露不兼容性。

Conclusion: 依赖选择不仅决定旧项目是否能通过，还决定基准能观察到哪些迁移失败。保留的 lock 是最强的版本参照；缺失时应采用发布日期选择作为有历史依据的替代方案，迁移基准应显式声明该选择并报告其对结果的影响。

Abstract: Migration benchmarks need projects whose tests pass before migration begins. Yet an old project can behave differently without any change to its code. Reinstalling it today may introduce newer libraries, changing both its initial test results and the failures observed during migration.
  We investigate this effect in PHP 7.4-to-8.3 migration. Using unchanged project source and saved package records, we compare two dependency policies. Modern selection allows all versions in the snapshot, while release-date selection allows only versions published by the project's release date. With Composer 2 fixed, release-date selection improves the PHP 7.4 pass rate by 7.1 pp across 3,660 releases. A separate comparison against committed locks shows that it also recovers recorded dependency versions more closely than modern selection.
  The choice matters after migration as well. Among projects that pass on PHP 7.4 under both policies, PHP 8.3 test outcomes differ in 15.0% of eligible pairs. We use a shared test runner and bypass Composer's platform check to isolate test behavior. The resulting candidate migration task sets overlap by only 58.5%. Most differences arise in Laravel projects, where older dependencies trigger deprecations that newer versions avoid. Targeted interventions show that removing these initial failures can restore passing tests or expose further incompatibilities.
  Thus, dependency selection changes not just whether an old project passes, but also which migration failures a benchmark can observe. Preserved locks provide the strongest available version reference; without them, release-date selection offers a historically grounded alternative. Migration benchmarks should make this choice explicit and report its effect on their results.

</details>


### [235] [Architectural Debt Enforcement under Incomplete Observation: A Controlled Study of Ratchets and Evidence Qualification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41276)
*Zhiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种"证据限定"的架构债务门禁决策契约，将观测资格、禁止关系识别、继承债务与授权基线更新分离，并在四仓库240文件与480场景上验证候选棘轮与dependency-cruiser基线均达到预期，但不主张优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构债务门禁需区分"被允许的变更"与"观测不完整的变更"，避免仅凭静态或计数信息做出误判，因此需要一个显式限定证据资格、明确禁止关系、处理继承债务并区分授权基线更新的决策契约。

Method: 以240个固定文件（来自4个仓库）为对象，联合当前观测器与程序化参照进行资格判定并核对边级一致性；构造480个状态场景（共享60个源锚点）比较候选棘轮、成熟dependency-cruiser基线、静态基线与仅计数基线；再引入通用绑定层检验规格不匹配、当前对照与合法重复；另在两个受限包作用域上运行40个场景与48次新进程功能检查；并在10对发射见证上比较TypeScript 5.9.3与6.0.2输出。

Result: 当前观测器与程序化参照共同资格化166/240文件（69.17%），均具有精确的参照相对边一致性，74个仍为reference-UNKNOWN，仓库间覆盖率从13/60到59/60不等；在480个场景中候选棘轮与dependency-cruiser基线全部匹配预期结果，静态与仅计数基线分别匹配420与360；保留失败包含9个额外false-PASS探针与120次未资格化的全树计时尝试；通用绑定层拒绝54个指定不匹配、接受54个当前对照并允许合法重复；TypeScript两个版本在10对见证上输出一致，但未改变冻结的UNKNOWN标签。

Conclusion: 贡献是对"证据限定式执行"及其边界的受控说明，而非语言完备性、自然维护有效性或新的收缩基线算法；结果不构成候选方案优越性的证据，残余不确定性由类型/运行时关系、安装上下文与生成产物来源解释，成本亦绑定于所测实现。

Abstract: Architectural debt gates must distinguish an allowed change from an incompletely observed one. We study a decision contract separating observation qualification, prohibited relation identity, inherited debt and authorized baseline updates. Across 240 fixed files from four repositories, the current observer and programmatic reference jointly qualify 166 files (69.17%), all with exact reference-relative edge agreement; 74 remain reference-UNKNOWN. Coverage varies from 13/60 to 59/60 by repository. On 480 constructed state scenarios sharing 60 source anchors, both the candidate ratchet and mature dependency-cruiser baseline match every expected outcome, whereas static and count-only baselines match 420 and 360. These results do not establish candidate superiority. Preserved failures include nine additional false-PASS probes and 120 unqualified full-tree timing attempts. A subsequent generic binding layer rejects 54 specified mismatches, accepts 54 current controls and permits legal repetition. Two bounded package scopes support 40 scenarios and 48 fresh-process functional checks; their costs remain tied to the measured implementations. Diagnostics explain residual uncertainty through type/runtime relations, installation context and generated-artifact provenance. TypeScript 5.9.3 and 6.0.2 yield identical outputs on ten paired emission witnesses, but neither that agreement nor verified release provenance changes the frozen UNKNOWN labels. The contribution is a controlled account of evidence-qualified enforcement and its limits, not language completeness, natural maintenance effectiveness or a new shrinking-baseline algorithm.

</details>


### [236] [What Does a Repair Agent Gain from Remembering Its Failure? An Exploratory Study on Real Python Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41433)
*Wenbo Ye,Yongxi Zhou,Junwei Yao,Jiahui Ge*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在75个BugsInPy bug上对181次失败尝试重试五种条件，失败记忆（转录、自写教训、测试信号）相对普通重试仅有不显著的点估计增益；模型自写教训不优于其摘要的转录；转录的作用可能超出"告知失败"本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: coding agent修复失败后常带着失败记录（转录、自写教训、提取的测试信号）重试，但失败记忆究竟改变了重试中的什么，缺乏严格量化。

Method: 在75个BugsInPy bug、gpt-5.4-mini的181次失败尝试上，设置五种记忆条件进行重试，使用预注册分析并报告偏差；两项预注册后续实验用更大的gpt-5.4重复普通重试、转录与教训三条件，并增加仅通知对照。

Result: 在暴露bug的测试上，各记忆条件点估计均高于普通重试（基线29.8%，+5.5至+9.9点），但均不显著。教训相对转录为-1.7点（95% CI [-8.7,+4.9]；gpt-5.4为-3.5），却多花一次模型调用。仅通知无显著变化；转录比通知多解决12.2点（CI [+4.2,+20.0]，156次失败的部分运行；十二项预注册对比中唯一显著者，gpt-5.4上不显著）。事后分析：带记忆的重试更少出现未修改任何Python文件的情况，编辑重试中触及修复涉及文件的比例接近上限。全测试套件事后检查显示，同时看过上次测试结果的重试更常在其他测试上失败，削弱了表观增益。结果在失败之间的差异远大于条件之间。

Conclusion: 失败记忆相对普通重试有正向但统计上不显著的效果；模型自写教训相对转录性价比低；转录的价值可能在于提供具体失败信息而非单纯提示失败；需按给出的样本量开展确证性研究。

Abstract: When a coding agent fails a repair task, it is often retried with a record of the failure: the transcript, a self-written lesson, or extracted test signals. We ask what such failure memory changes in the retry. On 75 BugsInPy bugs, we retried each of 181 failed attempts of gpt-5.4-mini under five conditions, with a predeclared analysis whose deviations we report. Two predeclared follow-ups repeated the plain-retry, transcript and lesson conditions with the larger gpt-5.4 and added a notice-only control. On the bug-revealing tests, every memory condition gained over a plain retry in point estimate (+5.5 to +9.9 points over 29.8%), but none significantly. Five further findings stand out. First, a model-written lesson showed no advantage over the transcript it summarises (-1.7 points, 95% CI [-8.7, +4.9]; -3.5 for gpt-5.4), although it costs an extra model call. Second, the transcript may do more than tell the agent that it failed: a notice alone produced no significant change, while the transcript resolved 12.2 points more failures than the notice for gpt-5.4-mini (CI [+4.2, +20.0], partial run of 156 failures; the only significant one of twelve predeclared contrasts, and not significant for gpt-5.4). Third, post hoc, fewer retries with memory ended without modifying any Python file, while the share of editing retries that touched a file the fix changes was near ceiling. Fourth, in a post hoc full-test-suite check, retries that had also seen the previous attempt's test results more often failed other tests that the reference fix passes, which shrinks their apparent gains. Fifth, outcomes varied far more between failures than between conditions, and we give the sample sizes a confirmatory study would need.

</details>


### [237] [Post-SQLFix: Property-Guided Repair of LLM-Generated SQL with Runtime Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41472)
*Zhihao Guo,Zonghan Wu,Yilei Shao,Haizhou Du,Nan Tang,Xiao Cheng,Huan Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Post-SQLFix，将Text-to-SQL修复重构为性质引导的程序修复，用方言感知的规范查询结构与分层静态分析器替代执行反馈，并以temporal monitor保证修复单调改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的Text-to-SQL查询常含语法错误、无效标识符引用及违反SQL作用域/类型规则的问题。现有基于执行反馈(EF)的方法只给出症状级、方言相关的错误信息，迫使LLM猜测根因；更关键的是存在"仲裁悖论"(Arbitration Paradox)：系统既依赖未经校验的模型提出修复，又依赖同一模型判定修复是否满足所需性质，缺乏独立的正确性保证。

Method: 将SQL规范化为方言感知的规范查询结构(CQS)，用三层分层静态分析器（语法层、上下文无关结构层、上下文相关语义层）进行验证，产出精确的、schema感知的违规诊断以引导LLM提出修复；随后由Verify-Before-Apply Temporal Monitor只接受满足以下条件的修复：降低违规计数、不重新引入已解决的违规、满足算子契约。运行时不变式受声明的规则族与算子契约约束，有限重试预算保证停机。

Result: 在BIRD、Spider、Mini-Dev三个基准（3000+评测实例、SQLite/MySQL/PostgreSQL三种方言、六种模型配置）上，Post-SQLFix解决了EF未能修复的执行错误中的77.8%，修复迭代次数不到一半，token消耗减少30%；监控器接受的状态转移未引入可静态检测的结构性回退，将受检修复保真度转化为最高+11.6个百分点的绝对准确率提升。

Conclusion: 以性质引导的程序修复范式替代执行反馈，可有效破解仲裁悖论：静态分析提供精确根因诊断，temporal monitor提供独立于LLM的判定机制，从而在更少迭代与token下获得更高且可验证的修复正确性与端到端准确率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) for Text-to-SQL frequently generate queries with syntax errors, invalid identifier references, or violations of SQL scoping and typing rules. Repairing these errors is a program correctness problem, yet existing approaches rely on Execution Feedback (EF), whose symptom-level, dialect-specific error messages force the LLM to speculate on root causes. We identify an Arbitration Paradox in this paradigm: the system relies on unverified models both to propose repairs and to make the final judgment on whether those repairs satisfy the required properties. We resolve this paradox by reformulating Text-to-SQL repair as property-guided program repair.
